### [1] [Horizon-Dependent Tube MPC for Spacecraft Rendezvous on Elliptical Orbits with Conditional Robust Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08921)
*Omer Burak Iskender*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对椭圆轨道航天器交会问题，提出了一种基于管道（tube）的模型预测控制器，将约束收紧管道方法从圆轨道推广到Yamanaka-Ankersen线性时变椭圆轨道动力学，并处理推进剂质量不确定性驱动的推力误差。通过逐元素误差界、乘性-加性转换和Perron-Frobenius谱半径条件，在保持早期预测步骤几乎整个安全走廊的同时，将走廊违规平均降低一个数量级以上，并给出显式的输入-状态稳定性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆轨道交会必须在导航噪声、未建模扰动以及推进剂质量不确定性引起的推力误差下维持安全走廊。现有管道MPC方法主要针对圆轨道（线性时不变）结构，难以直接适用于Yamanaka-Ankersen线性时变椭圆轨道动力学；且如何在质量不确定性下进行约束收紧并保证收敛性与稳定性，缺乏系统的方法与可计算的判定条件。

Method: 1) 将约束收紧管道方法从圆轨道（LTI）推广到椭圆轨道（Yamanaka-Ankersen LTV）动力学；2) 提出一个与预测视界相关的逐元素误差界，沿预测窗口传播实际闭环矩阵，使早期预测步保留几乎完整的走廊（而不像恒定宽度管道那样过早放弃）；3) 采用乘性到加性（multiplicative-to-additive）转换，将质量与推力不确定性并入同一收紧递推式中；4) 基于Perron-Frobenius谱半径条件提供收紧收敛的可计算证书，并推导显式的输入-状态稳定性增益；5) 构建仅需逐元素算术和开源二次规划求解器的实现，超出设计包络时自动退化为饱和线性回退方案。

Result: 在火星样本返回轨道的成对蒙特卡洛实验中，管道控制器相比名义预测基线，在相当燃油成本下将平均走廊违规降低一个数量级以上；在远超假设扰动预算的非线性真实模型测试（含扁率摄动）中，走廊仍未被违反；当超出设计包络时，收紧问题变为不可行，控制器显式退化为饱和线性回退，使保证的丧失显式化而非静默失效。

Conclusion: 该管道MPC方法为椭圆轨道交会提供了一种计算上经济（仅需逐元素运算与开源QP求解器）、鲁棒性可证（谱半径增益、显式稳定性）且安全性显式（超限即回退而非静默失败）的解决方案，相较于恒定宽度管道，逐元素时变误差界能更好地保留短视界内的走廊裕度。

Abstract: Spacecraft rendezvous on elliptical orbits must hold a safety corridor under navigation noise, unmodelled perturbations, and thrust errors driven by propellant mass uncertainty. This paper develops a tube-based model predictive controller for the Yamanaka-Ankersen linear time-varying dynamics, carrying constraint-tightening tube methods from circular to elliptical orbits. A horizon-dependent, element-wise error bound propagates the actual closed-loop matrices along the prediction window, so early prediction steps keep nearly the full corridor that a constant-width tube would surrender. A multiplicative-to-additive conversion folds mass and thrust uncertainty into the same tightening recursion, and a Perron-Frobenius spectral-radius condition supplies a computable certificate that the tightening converges, with an explicit input-to-state stability gain. In a paired Monte Carlo campaign on a Mars sample-return orbit, the tube controller cuts mean corridor violations by more than an order of magnitude against a nominal predictive baseline at comparable fuel cost. A nonlinear truth-model test with oblateness perturbations well beyond the assumed disturbance budget leaves the corridor unviolated, and when the design envelope is exceeded the tightened problem becomes infeasible and the controller reverts to a saturated linear fallback, making the loss of guarantee explicit rather than silent. The construction requires only element-wise arithmetic and an open-source quadratic-programming solver.

</details>


### [2] [Closing the loop in learning with missing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09030)
*Dimitrios Pylorof,Humberto E. Garcia*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究训练数据缺失问题，将数据缺失建模为影响参数误差动力学可控性的结构化驱动缺失，并提出具有Lyapunov稳定性特性的自适应学习机制，在部分、间歇性可观测条件下通过节流模型更新保持学习一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据缺失是机器学习中的常见挑战，但现有方法缺乏对"模型应学习什么"这一根本问题的系统性理论视角。作者希望从动力系统与可控性角度为数据缺失提供新的理论框架。

Method: 将数据缺失视为结构化驱动（actuation）缺失，限制参数误差动力学的可控性；基于Lyapunov稳定性推导自适应调整机制（节流模型更新）；通过ISS型残差-状态界分析，刻画损失残差与预条件更新几何之间的有界闭环失配。

Result: 在循环激励条件下，建立了ISS型残差-状态界；在多种模态的极端稀疏数据场景中评估了方向性可观性感知的自适应学习方法，验证其在促进学习一致性与稳定性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方向性可观性感知自适应学习方法能够在病态稀疏领域和问题中保持学习的一致性与稳定性，为数据缺失场景下的学习过程提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: What should a machine learning model learn when data is missing during training? We look at the learning process from a dynamical systems perspective, cast data missingness as a structured loss of actuation that limits controllability of the parameter error dynamics, and ultimately derive adaptation mechanisms with Lyapunov stability characteristics that throttle model updates in ways that preserve learning coherence under partial, intermittent observability. Under recurrent excitation, our analysis provides ISS-type residual-to-state bounds with respect to a bounded closed-loop mismatch between the loss residual and the preconditioned update geometry. We evaluate the efficacy of our directional observability-aware adaptive learning approach on multimodal contexts, reinforcing its premise in promoting learning coherence and stability even in pathologically sparse domains and problems.

</details>


### [3] [Parameter-Dependent LMI Synthesis for Semi-Global Differential ISS Trajectory Tracking of Nonholonomic Mobile Robots Under Multiplicative Wheel Slip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08049)
*Mohammad Sabouri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数依赖的线性矩阵不等式框架，用于在剧烈乘法轮滑和复杂地形条件下实现非完整移动机器人的轨迹跟踪。通过网格到连续域的残差认证、半全局微分输入-状态稳定性及增益有界反馈代理，在Kanayama误差坐标下显式建立了有界乘性滑移与加性扰动之间的联系，并通过对参考速度进行仿射参数化实现了稳定性的级联分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整移动机器人在可变地形上行驶时面临严重的乘性轮滑，导致传统固定增益控制器的跟踪性能显著退化甚至失效。现有LMI框架难以直接处理由物理滑移机制引发的非线性扰动，需要一个能够桥接滑移物理机理与凸优化综合范式的统一框架，以实现鲁棒的轨迹跟踪。

Method: 采用参数依赖的LMI框架，对采样凸公式进行网格到连续域的残差认证；在Kanayama误差坐标中推导出有界乘性滑移所诱导的加性扰动的显式上界；辅助增益矩阵和逆存储度量关于参考速度仿射参数化，而存储度量通过点对点矩阵求逆继承非线性依赖；通过变分收缩、正向不变性、滑移诱导扰动界和基于耗散的轨迹重构进行级联稳定性分析。

Result: 在100次蒙特卡洛运行中，所提控制器实现了完全轨迹包含于认证包络内。在可变地形场景下，峰值跟踪误差较固定增益LMI基线降低12%，较手动调参基线降低49%，而恒定增益基线的预定义衰减速率下不可行。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论和数值层面均验证了其有效性，能够同时满足半全局微分输入-状态稳定性、指定指数衰减率、区域极点配置和有界增益反馈代理，特别适用于执行器受限场景下的严重滑移鲁棒轨迹跟踪。补充实验（高斯传感器噪声、复合压力测试、嵌入式平台可行性）进一步证实了其实际部署价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a parameter-dependent linear matrix inequality (LMI) framework for trajectory tracking of nonholonomic mobile robots subject to severe multiplicative wheel slip on variable-terrain surfaces. The sampled convex formulation, augmented with grid-to-continuum residual certification, simultaneously establishes semi-global differential input-to-state stability, a prescribed exponential decay rate, regional pole placement, and a gain-bounded feedback proxy for actuator-limited operation. A central contribution is an explicit upper bound on the additive disturbance induced by bounded multiplicative slip in the Kanayama error coordinates, bridging the physical slip mechanism and the convex synthesis paradigm. The auxiliary gain matrix and inverse storage metric are parameterized affinely in the reference velocities, while the storage metric inherits nonlinear dependence through pointwise matrix inversion. Stability is established via a cascade analysis combining variational contraction, forward invariance, slip-induced disturbance bounds, and dissipation-based trajectory reconstruction. Numerical validation compares three controllers across six reference trajectories, six disturbance classes, and a 60-second variable-terrain test featuring six severe slip patches with bidirectional slip ratios reaching +/-50%, replicated on two geometries. Supplementary studies address Gaussian sensor noise, compound stress-testing, and embedded-platform computational feasibility. Across 100 Monte-Carlo runs the proposed controller achieves complete trajectory containment within the certified envelope. On the variable-terrain scenario, peak tracking error is reduced by 12% against the fixed-gain LMI baseline and 49% against the manual baseline, with the constant-gain baseline infeasible at the prescribed decay rate.

</details>


### [4] [Preview-Based Relative-Motion Control of an Insertion Tool for Neural-Thread Placement in Pulsating Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08860)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人神经线放置手术中的相对运动控制问题，提出基于预览机制的相对运动控制器，利用延迟周期表面运动的估计与短时域预测，结合无偏移模型预测控制（MPC），在保持作动器力受限和侧向相对速度受限的同时，实现高精度的相对放置。在MuJoCo仿真中，1自由度下实现12.0μm自由空间和1.9μm接触RMS误差，3自由度下耦合预览将侧向剪切速度从1.34降至0.50mm/s，并通过可行性恢复的软八边形剪切约束在感知退化下保持QP可解且无违规。最后给出双顶点李雅普诺夫证书，验证有限时域误差反馈增益在±50%反射质量失配下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人神经线放置手术中，插入工具尖端必须相对于随心脏和呼吸搏动而运动的心律表面进行精确调节。传统延迟反馈阻抗控制和实验室系PD控制在动态组织运动场景下精度不足（RMS误差分别达18.3/176.8μm和286.1/275.5μm），且需要同时满足作动器力受限和侧向速度受限等多重约束，因此需要一种能估计、预测并主动补偿周期性表面运动的前瞻性控制方案。

Method: 提出基于预览的相对运动控制器：(1)估计延迟滞后的周期表面运动并通过短时域预测；(2)采用无偏移模型预测控制（offset-free MPC）调节相对放置，同时约束作动器力和侧向相对速度；(3)在3自由度中引入耦合预览以降低接触侧向剪切；(4)提出可行性恢复的八边形剪切约束公式，在感知退化下通过有界共享松弛变量保持QP可解；(5)采用双顶点李雅普诺夫证书验证实际有限时域误差反馈增益在反射质量失配下的稳定性。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真中，1自由度控制器实现12.0μm自由空间和1.9μm接触RMS相对放置误差，显著优于延迟反馈阻抗（18.3/176.8μm）和实验室系PD（286.1/275.5μm），但峰值接触力更高（3.43 vs 2.00mN）。3自由度中耦合预览将接触侧向剪切从1.34降至0.50mm/s，侧向RMS误差2.1μm。在10μm RMS感知噪声下，匹配代价仅控制器在全部10个种子中违反0.80mm/s预算（均值/最大值0.988/1.175mm/s），而软八边形控制器10个种子全部完成且无误违规（0.653/0.712mm/s），运行包络可扩展至15μm RMS。李雅普诺夫证书在-40%/+50%反射质量失配范围内成立。

Conclusion: 预览式无偏移MPC控制器在仿真中展现出显著优于传统方法的相对放置精度，且通过软约束松弛设计在感知噪声下保持了可行性与安全性，双顶点李雅普诺夫分析为实际有限时域增益提供了稳定性保证。然而，该研究仍属仿真阶段，模型将尖端简化为刚性接触点，实际部署前还需解决柔性线和载体针力学建模、经验证的瞬态力约束、生物损伤阈值以及硬件级感知与时序等关键问题。

Abstract: Robotic neural-thread placement requires regulating the insertion-tool tip relative to tissue that moves with cardiac and respiratory pulsation. This paper develops a preview-based relative-motion controller that estimates latency-delayed periodic surface motion, predicts it over a short horizon, and uses offset-free model predictive control to regulate relative placement while limiting actuator effort and lateral relative velocity. In MuJoCo, the 1-DOF controller achieves 12.0\um\ free-space and 1.9\um\ contact RMS relative-placement error, versus 18.3/176.8\um\ for delayed-feedback impedance and 286.1/275.5\um\ for lab-frame PD, at the cost of higher peak contact force (3.43 versus 2.00~mN) since offset-free tracking drives the tip fully to the commanded depth rather than yielding against the tissue. In 3 DOF, coupled preview reduces contact lateral shear from 1.34 to 0.50~mm/s with 2.1\um\ lateral RMS error. A feasibility-restored octagonal shear formulation keeps the QP solvable under degraded sensing by adding a bounded shared slack: at 10\um\ RMS per-axis sensing noise, where a matched cost-only controller violates the 0.80~mm/s budget in all 10 seeds (mean/maximum 0.988/1.175~mm/s), the soft-octagon controller completes all 10 seeds with no fallback and no measured violation (0.653/0.712~mm/s), with its operating envelope characterized up to 15\um\ RMS. A two-vertex Lyapunov certificate for the controller's actual finite-horizon error-feedback gain holds over $-40\%/{+}50\%$ reflected-mass mismatch. The modeled tip is a rigid contact point, and the study is simulation-only: flexible-thread and carrier-needle mechanics, a validated transient-force constraint, biological damage thresholds, and hardware-realistic sensing and timing remain required before deployment.

</details>


### [5] [Real-Time Nonlinear MPC via Sequential Quadratic Programming with Structure-Exploiting ADMM and Interior-Point Methods for Underactuated Double-Pendulum Swing-Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09272)
*Nick Karydakis,Konstantinos Chatzilygeroudis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SQP（序列二次规划）的非线性模型预测控制方法，用于解决欠驱动二连杆系统的摆起与稳定控制问题，并在第四届"AI Olympics with RealAIGym"竞赛（IJCAI-ECAI 2026）的云平台上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛要求参赛者在不知道系统模型参数的情况下，在有限的交互时间内用真实硬件（CloudPendulum）开发全局控制策略，以完成欠驱动二连杆系统的摆起和直立稳定控制。这需要一种不依赖精确模型、具有实时性和鲁棒性的最优控制方法。

Method: 采用基于实时非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）的最优控制方法，通过序列二次规划（SQP）求解器在每次控制周期内求解非线性优化问题，从而实现系统的摆起与稳定控制。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于SQP的MPC控制器能够实现可靠的摆起与稳定性能，并且在面对外部扰动时保持良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于SQP的MPC方法在欠驱动二连杆系统的摆起与稳定控制任务中表现可靠且具有鲁棒性，适用于模型参数未知、交互时间受限的云平台竞赛场景。

Abstract: The 4th "AI Olympics with RealAIGym" competition, to be held at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 in Bremen, challenges participants to develop a global control policy for swinging up and stabilizing an underactuated two-link system in its upright position. In contrast to previous editions, participants develop and evaluate their control strategies directly on remotely accessible CloudPendulum hardware, with limited interaction time and without prior knowledge of the system's model parameters. This paper presents an optimal-control-based approach employing real-time nonlinear model predictive control implemented using sequential quadratic programming. The results demonstrate that the proposed SQP-based MPC controller achieves reliable swing-up and stabilization performance, while maintaining robustness against disturbances.

</details>


### [6] [Trajectory-Induced Self-Calibration for Hidden-Target Localization Through an Unknown-Pose Range-Bearing Relay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09464)
*Yash Bagla*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究隐藏目标定位问题：智能体仅通过一个全局位置和偏航角均未知的中继信标提供的距离-方位数据包来定位隐藏目标。理论结果表明，单次车辆位姿观测存在连续规范（gauge）模糊性，而两次不同的车辆-中继观测足以在无噪声情况下唯一确定中继偏航、位置及目标位置。蒙特卡洛实验显示，所提估计器在30mm测距噪声下达到5.5mm RMSE，较朴素EKF基线精度提升13倍，且在10%异常值污染下通过Huber加权仍保持毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定位方法（如纯方位网络定位、相对帧定位、目标包围控制）均假设目标可直接观测或被纳入相对感知图，但在实际场景中目标可能是隐蔽的，且中继信标的位置和朝向未知。因此需要一种仅依赖中继信标提供的局部帧距离-方位数据来标定自身并定位隐藏目标的方法，同时厘清最小运动条件以消除校准模糊性。

Method: 本文首先通过理论推导刻画了隐藏目标定位中的校准模糊性：单个车辆位姿留下偏航/平移/目标的连续规范，而两次来自未知位姿中继的不同观测可构造性地确定中继偏航（模2π）、中继位置及锚定目标。在此基础上提出局部秩推论、共享目标多信标扩展和轨迹扩展条件引理，将中继自校准与有限窗口激励及原生距离-方位估计联系起来。估计器结合构造性求解与Huber加权鲁棒化处理，并在蒙特卡洛模拟中评估。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛评估中，所提估计器在30mm单包测距噪声下达到5.5mm RMSE，精度为噪声水平的五分之一，比朴素EKF基线精确13倍；从2m目标偏移和2.4rad偏航误差下也能收敛到相同精度。Huber加权在10%异常值污染下保持毫米级精度，而未经保护的估计器成功率降至0.10。轨迹扩展度预测估计器质量：两条弱激励轨迹条件数超过100，成功率为0.82和0.70，而所有强激励轨迹均达到完全成功。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了两次不同车辆位姿观测足以消除未知位姿中继引入的校准模糊性，并给出了构造性求解方案。实验验证了该方法的鲁棒性和精度优势，同时揭示了轨迹扩展度（激发程度）对估计器质量的直接影响，为实际部署中轨迹规划和激励设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies hidden-target localization from range-bearing packets reported by a relay beacon whose global position and yaw are unknown. The vehicle knows its own trajectory but never directly senses the target; the relay packet contains only local-frame range and bearing to the vehicle and to the hidden target. Unlike bearing-only network localization, relative-frame localization, and target-enclosing control, the target is neither directly observed in the vehicle frame nor treated as a node in a relative-sensing graph. The main result characterizes the minimal motion that removes the resulting calibration ambiguity: one vehicle pose leaves a continuous yaw/translation/target gauge, whereas two distinct vehicle-relative observations from one unknown-pose relay constructively determine relay yaw (modulo 2 pi), relay position, and the anchored target in the noiseless case. A local rank corollary, a shared-target multi-beacon extension, and a trajectory-spread conditioning lemma connect relay self-calibration to finite-window excitation and native range-bearing estimation. In Monte Carlo evaluation the estimator recovers the hidden target with 5.5 mm RMSE, five times below the 30 mm per-packet range noise and thirteen times more accurate than a naive EKF baseline; it converges to the same accuracy from 2 m target offsets and 2.4 rad yaw errors, and Huber weighting preserves millimeter accuracy under 10% outlier corruption that drops the unprotected estimator to a 0.10 success rate. Trajectory spread predicts estimator quality: the two weakly excited trajectories carry condition numbers above 100 with success rates of 0.82 and 0.70, while every well-excited trajectory attains full success.

</details>

### [7] [The cosine measure of a function at a point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07716)
*Warren Hare,Gabriel Jarry-Bolduc,Chayne Planiden*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"函数的余弦测度"这一新概念，用于分析在函数不可微或梯度为零时，向量集合需要多大的余弦测度才能保证包含该函数的下降方向，并给出了相关理论、算法及示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 余弦测度在优化算法收敛性理论中具有重要意义，它描述了向量集合覆盖所有方向的程度。然而现有的余弦测度理论仅适用于函数可微且梯度非零的情形。当函数不可微或梯度为零时，如何刻画向量集合包含下降方向的充分条件仍是一个开放问题，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文引入"函数在某点的余弦测度"这一新概念，给出其数学定义并建立相关理论框架；通过构造示例证明光滑函数的余弦测度可取[-1,1]中的任意值；进一步设计用于计算函数余弦测度的算法，并在光滑与非光滑函数上进行演示。该结果同时也揭示了对无限集合及非凸锥的余弦测度的新认识。

Result: 建立了函数余弦测度的数学理论，证明了光滑函数在一点处的余弦测度可以取到[-1,1]区间内的任意值；提出了计算函数余弦测度的算法，并在光滑与非光滑函数的示例上进行了验证；相关结果进一步深化了对无限集合和非凸锥余弦测度的理解。

Conclusion: 函数的余弦测度为非可微或驻点情形下的下降方向存在性问题提供了刻画工具，拓展了经典余弦测度的适用范围，该概念及其算法有望在更广泛的优化算法收敛性分析中发挥作用。

Abstract: The cosine measure of a set of vectors in $\mathbb{R}^n$ measures how well the set covers all directions in $\mathbb{R}^n$. It identifies the direction furthest, in angle, from the set. It is used in the convergence theory of various optimization algorithms, but also highlights interesting geometric properties of sets. For example, the cosine measure of a set $S$ is greater than zero if and only if given any $\mathcal{C}^1$ function $f$ at a point $\mathbf{x}$ where the gradient is nonzero, $S$ must contain a descent direction of $f$ at $\mathbf{x}$. In this paper, we examine the question of what can be said when the function $f$ is non-differentiable or if it has a gradient equal to the zero vector. To examine these cases, we introduce the novel concept of the {\em cosine measure of a function} at a point. This value provides an infimum on the value of the cosine measure that a set of vectors requires to guarantee it contains a descent direction of the function at the point of interest. We present mathematical theory around this concept, including examples showing that the cosine measure of a smooth function can have any value in $[-1,1]$. We further present algorithms to compute the cosine measure of a function, and examples demonstrating the algorithm on smooth and nonsmooth functions. These results also shed light on the the cosine measure of infinite sets and nonconvex cones.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用三步方法：(1)利用Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto点采样状态向量并进行快速傅里叶变换，将近平动点轨道近似到近机器精度；(2)运用Pontryagin原理推导最小时间和时间约束最小推进剂问题的最优性必要条件，包括出发/到达点判据；(3)结合通用Birkhoff理论用于轨迹优化与快速无猜测谱算法生成候选解，并通过Hamiltonian最小化条件和横截条件验证解的极值性。推进剂消耗采用与比冲无关的余弦逆二次模型。

Result: 研究表明，Birkhoff理论的谱算法能够在没有任何动力学系统理论辅助或初始化的情况下生成可验证的极值解。候选解均通过了Hamiltonian最小化条件和横截条件的验证，证明了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了Birkhoff理论的谱算法可作为椭圆限制性三体问题中有限推力轨迹优化的独立求解框架，无需动力学系统理论的先验指导即可生成满足严格最优性条件的可验证极值解，为深空轨迹优化提供了新的通用方法论基础。

Abstract: The computation of finite-thrust extremal arcs in the elliptic restricted three-body trajectory optimization problem is considered. Libration-point orbits are approximated to near-machine precision using a fast Fourier transform of the sampled values of the state vector at Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto points. Checkable optimality conditions are derived by applying Pontryagin's principle to minimum-time and time-constrained minimum-propellant problems. These necessary conditions include criteria for optimal departure and arrival points. For propellant consumption, a recently developed computational model is employed. This model is agnostic to the specific impulse of the propellant and varies as the inverse quadratic of a cosine term. Candidate optimal solutions are generated by combining the universal Birkhoff theory for trajectory optimization with the fast, guess-free spectral algorithm. The extremality of the Birkhoff-computed solution is validated against the Hamiltonian minimization condition and the transversality conditions. It is shown that the Birkhoff-theoretic spectral algorithm can generate verifiable extremals without any assistance or initialization from dynamical systems theory.

### [9] [The Rank-Collapse Principle for Quadratic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07828)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian,Ahmad Mousavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"秩坍缩原理"(rank-collapse principle)：当二次型矩阵Q为半正定且低秩时（Q=BB^T），目标函数仅通过低维影子y=B^T x依赖x，且最优影子y*在自身方向上唯一最大化线性泛函并满足定量二次边际。该原理将非线性最优化坍缩为低维自生成线性暴露方向问题，但其本身不保证有限候选集，还需结合可行族的投影/活跃几何结构（投影影子散射与活跃结构坍缩）。文章统一了zonotope、凸组合、边骨架、投影法锥及固定秩稀疏PCA等相关方法，并给出二进制/有限相位向量、基数约束、拟阵基和稀疏PCA的定量结论及稳定性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次优化在矩阵曲率不利或可行集离散/组合/非凸时通常难以求解；但已有的互补现象表明，当矩阵低秩时一些看似困难的二次规划存在固定秩的多项式时间精确算法。作者希望找到这一现象背后的统一半正定几何框架，并系统化"低秩⇒可精确优化"的机理。

Method: 定义Q=BB^T的半正定结构，将目标函数表示为其在B^T x映射下的低维影子；证明每个最优影子y*在其自身方向上唯一最大化线性泛函并满足量化二次边际（秩坍缩原理）。进一步区分两种几何塌缩机制：投影影子散射（projected-shadow scattering）与活跃结构坍缩（active-structure collapse），并关联到zonotope、凸组合、边骨架、投影法锥、固定秩稀疏PCA等既有方法。还给出方向稳定性和近似低秩证书。

Result: 建立了统一的秩坍缩框架，证明非线性最优性可降维为低维自生成线性曝光方向；明确了"秩坍缩原理"本身仅提供最优性刻画而不保证有限候选集，效率还取决于可行家族的投影/活跃几何。推导了二进制、有限相位向量、基数约束、拟阵基和稀疏PCA的tie-safe定量推论，并提供可复现实验。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提供一个统一而严谨的框架及定量后果，而非声称首次发现固定秩可解性结果。该框架揭示了低秩二次规划的几何本质，为后续算法设计与近似低秩情形的处理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Quadratic optimization becomes hard as soon as either the matrix in the quadratic form has an unfavorable curvature or the feasible set is discrete, combinatorial, or otherwise nonconvex. A complementary phenomenon is also well known in the signal-processing and optimization communities: when the matrix in the quadratic form has small rank, some hard-looking quadratic programs admit exact polynomial-time algorithms for fixed rank. We study the common positive-semidefinite geometry behind this phenomenon. If $Q=BB^\top$ is positive semidefinite, the objective depends on $x$ only through the rank-space shadow $y=B^\top x$. Every optimal shadow $y^*$ uniquely maximizes the linear functional defined by its own direction and satisfies a quantitative quadratic margin. Thus nonlinear optimality collapses to a low-dimensional, self-generated linear exposure direction. We call this the rank-collapse principle. The principle alone does not imply a finite candidate set: efficient exact optimization additionally depends on the projected or active geometry of the feasible family. We organize this distinction through projected-shadow scattering and active-structure collapse, relate it explicitly to established zonotope, convex-combinatorial, edge-skeleton, projected-normal-fan, and fixed-rank sparse-PCA methods, and derive tie-safe consequences for binary and finite-phase vectors, cardinality constraints, matroid bases, and sparse PCA. We also give directional-stability and approximately low-rank certificates, together with reproducible experiments. The paper's contribution is a unified, careful framework and a set of quantitative consequences, rather than a claim to originate the known fixed-rank tractability results that motivate it.

</details>

*Hongbo Li,James C. Spall*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出LMC-SPSA（带噪声的Langevin Monte Carlo结合SPSA梯度近似），使用每次迭代仅两次带噪声的函数评估来近似目标对数密度的梯度，并证明其收敛性质，将Wasserstein距离下的维度依赖从O(p^4)优化到O(p^2)，同时给出总噪声oracle复杂度为O(p/ε²+δ²p³/ε³)，优于已有ZO-LMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度类采样方案（如LMC）通常比非梯度方法混合更快，但实际应用中目标对数密度的梯度往往不可得，且函数评估带有噪声（如随机模拟器或黑盒模拟器）。这限制了LMC类方法的适用性，需要一种无需显式梯度、且能应对噪声函数评估的采样方法。

Result: 证明LMC-SPSA在带噪声梯度估计下以Wasserstein距离收敛；给出W_2精度ε所需的总噪声oracle复杂度为O(p/ε²+δ²p³/ε³)，其中δ为配对噪声水平，改进了Roy等人的ZO-LMC方法在噪声依赖精度上的缩放；在衰减步长和扰动序列下建立Wasserstein误差渐近趋于零的结果，并给出了平衡调度下的显式收敛速率。数值实验验证了LMC-SPSA在相同函数评估预算下比ZO-LMC具有更小的经验采样误差。

Conclusion: LMC-SPSA是一种适用于梯度不可得且函数评估含噪声情形的有效采样方法，在理论上具有更优的维度依赖和噪声依赖收敛保证，在经验上也优于已有的ZO-LMC方法，拓展了Langevin型采样在实际黑盒和随机模拟场景中的适用性。

Abstract: In sampling problems, gradient-based schemes such as Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC) mix faster than non-gradient-based methods, but their applicability is limited by access to the gradient of the target log-density. In practice, gradients are often unavailable and function evaluations are noisy, e.g., stochastic simulators or black-box simulators, so we propose LMC-SPSA with noise, which approximates the gradient of the target log-density using two noisy function evaluations per iteration. We prove, under noisy gradient estimates, that LMC-SPSA converges in distribution by proving the convergence in Wasserstein distance. Furthermore, we construct a diminishing step-size schedule that still drives the Wasserstein error bound to convergence, extending convergence guarantees beyond the constant-step setting. Further, we sharpen the dominant dimension dependence of the Wasserstein error from $O(p^4)$ to $O(p^2)$ (with $p$ denoting the dimension), and support this analysis with numerical results. We show that LMC-SPSA achieves $W_2$-accuracy $\varepsilon$ with total noisy-oracle complexity of $O(p/\varepsilon^2+δ^2p^3/\varepsilon^3)$, where $δ$ is the paired-noise level. This improves the noise-dependent accuracy scaling relative to the ZO-LMC method of Roy et al. We further establish asymptotically vanishing Wasserstein error as the number of iterations $\to\infty$ under diminishing step-size and perturbation sequences and derive an explicit convergence rate for a balanced schedule under noisy zeroth-order feedback. Empirical experiments are conducted to verify the performance of LMC-SPSA with noise. We provide an oracle-budget-matched comparison with the ZO-LMC method, showing smaller empirical sampling errors under the same function-evaluation budget.

</details>

Abstract: This article studies the computation of the maximal controlled invariant set (MCIS) for neural network control systems (NNCSs) with a nominal plant model and a neural network residual model. An interval-based method is developed to construct a control-affine inclusion function for the NNCS dynamics and to exploit the induced hyperplane arrangement in the input space, which reduces the verification of controlled invariance from an infinite control search to a finite set of representative evaluations. Based on this finite characterization, verification-guided algorithms iteratively construct inner and outer approximations of the the MCIS, which are naturally parallelizable. Numerical examples demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

Abstract: This study develops an analytical and decision framework for a pooled hospital service comprising first-time patients who require diagnosis followed by treatment and referred patients who require only their prescribed treatment mode. The system is represented as a Markovian phase-type queue. probability generating functions are derived for two-treatment cases, while a matrix-analytic method is developed for an arbitrary number $n$ of treatment modes. The key performance measures are derived and interpreted. A load-triggered control is designed to determine the minimum capacity increment required to achieve a prescribed utilization level. Numerical experiments are conducted to validate the analytical results. Furthermore, the total-cost problem admits a strictly convex reformulation in reciprocal service-time variables and has a unique global minimum. Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Simulated Annealing (SA), and the Sine Cosine Algorithm (SCA) are additionally used as independent heuristic solvers.

On the computational side, we design an iterative BSDE-based procedure to approximate the stochastic Riccati equation from above and below, where the initial upper and lower bounds are obtained by solving two linear BSDEs. Empirical experiments based on sector ETF data show that the proposed multifactor mean--variance strategy produces smooth target-return wealth dynamics, with improved drawdown control and downside-risk protection relative to benchmark strategies. These results demonstrate the practical potential of combining stochastic Riccati equations, neural BSDE solvers, and multifactor market modeling for dynamic asset allocation.

For convex-concave minimax optimization, a subset of MVI problems, Chen, Liu, Luo, and Zhang (COLT 2025) recently improved the complexity to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( T^{-1.75})$ . Abstract: Data Envelopment Analysis (DEA) is a widely used non-parametric method for evaluating the relative efficiency of decision-making units (DMUs). However, the classic Charnes-Cooper-Rhodes (CCR) model often suffers from a lack of discriminatory power, particularly when the number of input and output variables is large relative to the number of DMUs. Like the traditional super-efficiency model, the proposed MM-DEA model excludes the target DMU from the reference set used to evaluate it; unlike the super-efficiency model, however, MM-DEA imposes an explicit upper bound on the target DMU's own score, ensuring that every score remains within the standard $[0,1]$ range rather than potentially exceeding one. In our numerical experiments, MM-DEA produced a unique ranking for a high-dimensional synthetic dataset on which the CCR model failed to discriminate among any of the DMUs. Our results suggest that the "margin" serves as a critical metric for managerial resilience and competitive advantage.

Abstract: We provide an explicit mathematical characterization of regional characteristics and the resulting epidemiology to evaluate whether elimination or suppression is feasible using targeted measures, such as case isolation and contact tracing. We prove that the optimal controls are to immediately implement public health measures at their maximum levels. We find that elimination or suppression strategies are feasible with targeted measures if public health capacity is high, the transmission rate is low, or if the arrival rate of infected travellers is low. Our results illustrate that a particular country that implemented a mitigation strategy during the pandemic may not necessarily have achieved better outcomes if it had instead implemented elimination because elimination may not have been feasible in countries with low public health capacity to support control measures, high transmission rates, or high traveller arrival rates. Combinations of regional characteristics determine whether an elimination, suppression or mitigation strategy without resurgence is feasible. We find that regions with the low traveller arrival rates and the capacity to implement and enforce isolation or quarantine of arriving travellers are less likely to exceed their capacity to contact trace and isolate community members. Travel measures prevent only a small number of community infections via their direct effect, but indirectly, by protecting contact tracing capacity, travel measures can prevent epidemic resurgence and substantially reduce the number of people infected during a pandemic before a vaccine or therapy is developed.

Abstract: Optimization under zeroth-order (i.e., bandit) feedback is central to many engineering problems where the analytic forms of objectives and/or constraints are unavailable. In modern applications, such as online control and online learning, optimization problems often evolve with time, requiring adaptive optimization methodologies. Yet, existing methods in this seting are largely confined to adaptations of methodologies developed for time-invariant or first-order optimization, and thus often rely on gradient surrogates that fail to fully exploit the zeroth-order structure of the available information. The resulting analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem and possible oracle errors. Our complexity bounds recover the complexity of existing zeroth-order methods in the time-invariant setting, while extending direct- search methods beyond static settings. As an illustrative application, we show that the methodology is naturally suited to solve optimal (equilibrium-selection) control problems for dynamical systems. In this setting, the analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem, measured through the effects of plant dynamics and exogenous disturbance variations.

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a systematic framework for deriving explicit error bounds for semidefinite feasibility problems without assuming strict feasibility (Slater's condition), a setting in which existing results are limited.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出使用输入凸神经网络（ICNN）替代FNN作为代理模型。ICNN的MIP公式具有更紧的LP松弛；利用ReLU激活的epigraph表示，构造基于LP的重构（不一定精确）；当不精确时，利用ICNN性质在箱式域上构造其图的最强连续松弛——凸包，下界由epigraph给出、上界由凹包给出。在此基础上设计分支定界算法，在每个节点构造该松弛，直接对输入变量分支而非MIP重构中的中间变量，当epigraph嵌入有效时在根节点终止。

Conclusion: ICNN是结构上优于FNN的代理模型选择，尤其当底层响应函数具凸性或凹性时，其计算优势显著，应被作为此类场景下的默认选择。

Abstract: Embedding trained neural networks as surrogates within optimisation problems is an established practice in operations research. The prevailing approach uses feedforward neural networks (FNNs) with ReLU activations, whose piecewise-linear structure admits an exact but computationally intensive mixed-integer programming (MIP) reformulation as the networks grow. We advocate input convex neural networks (ICNNs) as structurally superior surrogates when the underlying response is approximately convex or concave. The convex architecture offers two computational advantages. First, the ICNN-MIP formulation tends to yield a tighter linear programming (LP) relaxation than its FNN-MIP counterpart, with no integrality gap in favourable instances. Second, ICNNs uniquely admit an LP-based reformulation via epigraph representations of ReLU activations, though this embedding is not always exact. When it is not, we exploit the properties of ICNNs to construct the strongest continuous relaxation over box domains, namely, the convex hull of the ICNN's graph, bounded below by the epigraph and above by the concave envelope; this construction is tractable under input convexity but hard for general ReLU networks. On this basis, we develop a branch-and-bound algorithm that builds this relaxation at each node, branches directly on input variables rather than intermediate variables as in MIP reformulations, and terminates at the root node whenever the epigraph embedding is valid. Case studies on humanitarian food aid, oil well routing, and wine blending show that ICNN surrogates match FNN accuracy and deliver gains in solve time and scalability, supporting ICNN as the default surrogate when the underlying function is convex, concave, or well-approximated as such.

</details>

Abstract: Observation tasking is critical to satellite operations, enabling observation of planetary phenomena, orbital debris monitoring, and space domain awareness. To improve the effectiveness of satellite operations, orbital maneuverability is introduced as a leading-edge concept of operations within constellation reconfigurability for response to dynamic events. Previous investigations regarding constellation reconfigurability consist of deterministic mission environments, in which the formulations consider a priori knowledge of the environment; however, observation objectives inherently involve uncertainty at any given time. As such, scheduling satellite operations must account for uncertainties to ensure adequate observation tasking. In response, we present a stochastic variant of the Multistage Constellation Reconfiguration Problem (MCRP) which is solved using Stochastic Dual Dynamic Integer Programming (SDDiP). We additionally explore other stochastic problem-solving techniques for solution quality comparison. To demonstrate each solution method, two computational experiments with stochastic target properties are conducted. The first concerns random orbital targets, and the second concerns simulated hurricanes. The results of the experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the SDDiP solution approach over other stochastic problem-solving methods. Overall, the stochastic MCRP accounts for target stochasticity while obeying visible time windows and maneuver feasibility.

Abstract: The quadratic optimization-free (QO-free) method is a class of powerful and effective algorithms for solving nonlinearly constrained optimization problems in Euclidean spaces. The aim of the present work is to extend this method to solve optimization problems on manifolds with additional equality and inequality constraints. We first present a specific algorithm in the manifold setting. At each iteration, three linear systems sharing a common linear operator are solved to determine the master search direction. In addition, a higher-order correction direction is obtained by solving a reduced linear least squares subproblem to circumvent the Maratos effect which is assumed not to arise in existing related literature. A Riemannian arc search is then performed within the tangent space of the current iterate to generate the new iterate. Under appropriate assumptions, we establish the global and strong convergence of the proposed method. Moreover, we prove that the unit step size will eventually be accepted by the arc search, upon which the superlinear convergence of the algorithm is established. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method is very competitive compared with other existing approaches.

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem is a canonical setting for studying how a
  population-based method should allocate a fixed search budget between
  exploration and progressively stronger local intensification. We propose
  ADPSO-ERLS, a discrete swarm algorithm that treats this allocation as an
  explicit, tunable design variable. It couples memory-guided swap mutation,
  heterogeneous initialization, selective candidate-restricted 2-opt during
  evolution, and an incumbent-only final refinement combining candidate-restricted
  and optional full 2-opt with double-bridge perturbations. The method is
  PSO-inspired, using personal and global memories yet dispensing with velocity,
  inertia, and acceleration coefficients. All six algorithms are implemented in
  Rust, run on identical hardware, and stopped at a strict, recorded limit of
  100,000 candidate-solution assessments, so that programming language, hardware,
  and evaluation budget are held common across methods; wall-clock time is
  reported separately because equal assessment counts need not correspond to
  equal arithmetic work. Over 50 runs on five symmetric TSPLIB instances under the integer
  \texttt{EUC\_2D} convention, ADPSO-ERLS attains the lowest best and mean cost on
  every instance, with best-tour Gap of $1.93$--$4.17\%$ and relative error of
  $3.04$--$5.50\%$. It ranks first under the Friedman test, and all twenty-five
  multiplicity-controlled Wilcoxon comparisons favor it with large, near-complete
  distributional separation. A paired ablation with common seeds links
  initialization, in-run local search, and final refinement to quality gains,
  while candidate restriction chiefly cuts runtime, by up to a factor of roughly
  $38$. Further experiments up to $16{,}862$ cities keep best-tour Gaps below
  $6.7\%$, solving the largest case in under eleven minutes.

</details>

Abstract: The goal of the paper is to provide a complete analysis of a stochastic control problem when the cost to minimize involves the running maximum of the underlying controlled state process. The model is motivated by the electricity cost minimization of a data center facing coincident peak charges and possibly benefiting from participation in a demand-response program. From a mathematical standpoint, the main challenge comes from the inclusion of the running maximum in the state of the system forcing the original problem into a singular stochastic control problem on a closed wedge in the plane with reflection on parts of the boundary. We prove that the value function is locally Lipschitz continuous in space using a gap-monotonicity estimate for coupled reflected loads and a deterministic total-variation bound for the difference of two running maxima. The dynamic programming principle and viscosity formulation are then derived for ordinary progressively measurable controls. The corresponding HJB equation includes a reflecting Neumann condition on the lower boundary for the load direction as well as an oblique boundary condition on the diagonal part of the boundary of the domain due to the effect of the running-maximum process. We prove existence of a solution in the viscosity sense as well as a comparison theorem providing conditional uniqueness in the corresponding viscosity class. Finally we provide numerical results illustrating the trade-off between the two conflicting incentives comprising the expected cost of the data center.

</details>

Abstract: Vehicle platooning offers significant benefits, including reduced energy consumption, lower emissions, improved road utilization, enhanced safety, and reduced driver fatigue. As intelligent driving technologies continue to advance, platoon sizes are expected to increase substantially, making the efficient sequencing and resequencing of vehicles increasingly important. We study the vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing problem on road networks with varying segment lengths under two fundamental objectives: minimizing total energy consumption and minimizing the maximum energy consumption of any vehicle. For the typically encountered combinations of vehicle and road characteristics, we provide a complete computational complexity classification, either developing polynomial-time algorithms or proving computational intractability. For several intractable cases, we design fully polynomial-time approximation schemes and polynomial-time heuristics with provable performance guarantees. A computational study demonstrates that the proposed heuristics achieve average solutions within 1\% of optimal. We also consider settings in which only limited information about position-dependent energy savings is available and develop a heuristic with bounded worst-case performance. In addition, we present an efficient algorithm for on-road vehicle resequencing when only limited position changes are permitted. Together, these results provide a comprehensive algorithmic framework for energy-efficient vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全感知的决策制定和AI训练需要同时优化平均情况目标并缓解罕见灾难性事件的发生。然而，尾部目标对决策变量的极端敏感性使得传统的随机梯度下降算法在方差缩减集成中面临根本性挑战：无论选择何种步长，都会导致收敛的二分式失败，无法保证实际可行的运行时间。

Method: 首先理论分析尾部目标下SGD与方差缩减结合的局限，指出步长选择的两难困境；然后提出"安全起点"（safe start）的新概念，允许在优化初期对决策变量施加约束以确保尾部行为可控，从而支持高效的有限时间误差控制；进一步将安全起点与方差缩减技术结合，分析其采样复杂度的有利缩放特性。

Result: 在投资组合优化和基于神经网络的鲁棒分类两个应用中验证了所提方法。结果表明安全起点与方差缩减的组合能够实现高效有限时间误差控制，采样复杂度随问题规模有利扩展，克服了单纯SGD在尾部目标上的二分式收敛失败问题。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，对于尾部敏感的风险感知随机优化问题，需要超越标准SGD框架，通过"安全起点"概念为优化提供初始可行域保障，并结合方差缩减技术才能获得实际可行的算法与可证明的收敛保证，为安全感知决策和AI训练提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: We consider stochastic optimization where the goal is not only to optimize an average-case objective, but also to mitigate the occurrence of rare catastrophic events. This problem is motivated by safety-aware decision-making and AI training. We first argue that, in the presence of a simulation model, natural attempts to integrate variance reduction into optimization, even executed in a reasonable adaptive fashion, encounter fundamental challenges in guaranteeing realistic runtime when using common stochastic gradient descent algorithms. This challenge arises from the extreme sensitivity of tail-based objectives with respect to the decision variables, which renders a dichotomic failure of convergence regardless of what step size we select. We offer remedies based on a new notion of safe start that allows for efficient finite-time error control, and show how the sampling complexity scales favorably under the combination of safe start and variance reduction. We illustrate our methodologies on examples in portfolio optimization and robust classification with neural networks.

</details>

*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的执行器级策略（ALP）来控制绳索驱动并联机器人（CDPR），该策略不依赖特定机器人配置，可在任意CDPR上共享使用，并通过仿真到真实环境的迁移成功在真实8电机3D CDPR上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRL方法在CDPR控制中面临两大问题：训练时间长，且策略难以推广到不同机器人配置或不同数量的执行器。由于CDPR具有多样化的结构形态和复杂的控制挑战，需要一种更具泛化能力的DRL控制方法。

Result: 实验表明ALP在鲁棒性和精度方面均优于传统强化学习方法。训练于4电机平面2D CDPR的策略成功迁移到真实8电机3D CDPR上实现三维运动控制，验证了该方法不受执行器数量和布置方式限制的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次将DRL应用于CDPR的执行器级控制，证明了执行器级策略可以作为与配置无关的通用控制方法，显著提升CDPR控制的泛化能力和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>

*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SC²-WM，一个自纠正世界模型框架，通过内部反馈实现VLN-CE中的闭环决策，在行动执行前进行状态级计划细化，并在测试时根据反馈条件性地更新世界模型以进行模型级纠正，在基准测试上提升了导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法大多依赖开环执行，缺乏在推理过程中检测和纠正内部状态漂移的机制，导致在部分可观测环境下无法做出可靠的细粒度导航决策。

Method: SC²-WM框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 通过世界模型前瞻产生内部反馈，在行动执行前进行状态级计划细化（闭环决策）；(2) 引入条件世界感知适应，当反馈表明模型容量不足时，在测试时选择性地更新世界模型以实现模型级纠正。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准测试上，方法展示了改进的导航鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 内部反馈驱动的闭环决策框架能有效应对VLN-CE中的状态漂移问题，提升智能体的导航鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [44] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督视觉操作管线，机器人自动在目标位姿周围生成演示数据并直接从腕部RGB图像学习相对位姿修正，无需相机到机器人的外参标定。采用粗到精控制策略，在仿真中将平面弥散从9.69mm降至5.38mm，在真实UR5e机器人上对三个物体达到63.6%-66.6%的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作依赖对象特定编程、人工数据标注或标定感知管线，限制了实际场景中的快速部署。虽然示教学习提供更直接的替代方案，但收集演示仍需人工遥操作或示教。因此需要一种无需人工干预、无需外参标定的自动化视觉操作方法。

Method: 该方法基于自监督学习框架：利用ROS 2和Isaac Sim自动生成标注的图像-位姿对数据；分别生成用于平面精修和粗三维接近的独立数据集；训练卷积网络从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移和旋转；执行阶段采用粗到精控制器，先通过含高度变化的模型进行物体接近，再用平面数据细化最终对齐。

Result: 仿真中精修阶段将平面弥散从9.69mm降至5.38mm。真实实验中，在UR5e协作机器人上对三个物理物体进行端到端抓取尝试，两个物体在无旋转条件下成功率分别达66.6%和63.6%，在旋转条件下仍保持部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自动生成的演示数据能够支持实际视觉操作，显著减少了部署所需的设置工作量，但在深度预测和物体依赖的泛化方面仍存在挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [45] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRobotics，一个后端无关的控制平面，将编码智能体的"主智能体-子智能体-工具"架构移植到机器人策略改进中。其核心创新在于认识到机器人工具（训练策略、训练流程、数据采集）会频繁失败，因此每个工具的调用质量必须被测量、记录，并在底层工件变化时过期。系统通过可持久化的"训练-评估-改进"事务、不可变目标、控制器所有权的测量、提交键控的崩溃恢复、证据分级的技能库和标准化的工具注册表，实现了可操作的"无人值守"声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（如Claude Code和Codex）通过主智能体管理循环、子智能体分析与执行、工具执行工作的架构成功闭环了软件开发。将该架构移植到机器人策略改进时，面临一个关键差异：机器人工具（训练策略、训练流水线、数据收集）经常失败，导致工具质量必须被测量、每次调用记录、并在底层工件变化时失效。现有系统缺少这种可靠性保障。

Method: AgenticRobotics包含：1) 不可变的目标定义；2) 控制器所有权的测量机制；3) 提交键控的崩溃恢复（commit-keyed crash recovery）；4) 证据分级的技能库（evidence-graded skill library）；5) 具有标准化记录调用面的工具注册表。系统通过可持久化的训练-评估-改进事务，由LLM控制器驱动一次性worker执行。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 与线上版本相比，加固后的系统将虚假提升控制率从0.005-0.021降至0.001/次运行；2) 在可选停止（optional stopping）下提供随时有效的决策（anytime-valid decisions）；3) 在中断注入下方零丢失或重复效果；4) 六类工件篡改全部被签名验证器捕获。

Conclusion: 标题中的"可操作者离开"是操作性声明而非选择性声明：操作者可以离开是因为提升受证据门控、状态可恢复、能力质量从记录中推导——而非因为循环选择了比人类更好的检查点。实际上，在作者测量的单条谱系上，该系统的选择并不优于人类。门控机制切实购买了虚假提升控制、随时有效的决策、中断下的状态完整性和工件完整性验证等方面的收益。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [46] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种零成本的自动化直接偏好优化（DPO）流程，用于无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）。通过物理仿真状态回滚自动生成负反馈数据，在无需人工标注的情况下，使轻量级2B模型在成功率（49.16%）和碰撞抑制上超越大规模7B基线，成为新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端UAV-VLN模型依赖数十亿参数的大语言模型，推理延迟高，难以满足边缘部署需求。同时，纯行为克隆（BC）策略缺乏显式负反馈，导致智能体在分布外（OOD）场景下无法内化空间约束，碰撞率过高。作者希望在不依赖不可扩展的人工标注的前提下，解决轻量级模型的鲁棒性问题。

Method: AeroDPO包含四个核心步骤：(1) 碰撞检测后通过确定性物理仿真状态回滚，将环境恢复到碰撞前状态；(2) 提取导致碰撞的因果推理错误作为被拒绝动作（rejected actions）；(3) 施加解耦的特权干预（decoupled privileged interventions）合成避碰偏好动作；(4) 利用离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉模糊样本。整个流程是一个自动化数据飞轮，为2B轻量级模型提供DPO训练所需的成对偏好数据。

Result: AeroDPO使2B模型在未测绘场景下成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，完全匹配乃至超越7B大规模基线模型的表现，确立了自主航空智能体的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 感知质量而非语言推理能力是UAV-VLN任务的关键瓶颈。通过自动化数据飞轮提供负反馈信号，轻量级模型可以克服BC策略的固有缺陷，实现低成本、低延迟、高鲁棒性的边缘端自主导航。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [47] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-ART分类法，从多模态感知与多阶段系统设计的统一视角，系统综述了力觉与触觉感知的机器人学习方法，填补了该领域缺乏统一分类框架的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触敏感操作任务的成功执行不仅依赖视觉感知和运动生成，还依赖力调节与自适应控制。虽然近期机器人学习方法在融合力觉、触觉、视觉、语言和本体感受等模态方面取得了显著进展，且许多系统采用多阶段架构（高层策略、动作精炼模块、低层控制器）来桥接语义理解与反应式物理执行，但现有综述尚未从统一视角同时涵盖多模态感知与多阶段系统设计来审视力觉与触觉感知的机器人学习。

Method: 提出TF-ART（Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy），一个面向多模态和多阶段框架的力觉/触觉感知机器人学习分类法。该框架将各方法映射到统一的分层结构中，刻画了近期工作如何组织观测模态、编码和融合异构感官输入、跨多阶段生成与精炼动作，以及将学习策略连接到反应式机器人末端控制。此外，论文从方法论视角进一步考察物理交互的任务设置与基础设施需求，整合算法与实践两个层面。

Result: 构建了一个统一的分层分类体系TF-ART，系统梳理了力觉与触觉感知机器人学习方法在观测模态组织、多模态编码与融合、多阶段动作生成与精炼、以及策略到反应式控制连接等方面的技术路径，并整合了物理交互任务设定与基础设施要求。

Conclusion: TF-ART分类法填补了力觉与触觉感知机器人学习缺乏统一综述框架的空白，从多模态感知和多阶段系统设计的统一视角，为研究者提供了理解该领域方法组织方式和技术演进的结构化工具，同时兼顾了算法设计与工程实践两个维度。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [48] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI)，在采样动力学内部通过投影-回缩操作强制执行操作任务中的等式约束（如闭合运动链）和不等式约束（如关节极限、障碍物避让），使约束满足达到数值精度且不受任务权重影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制广泛用于操作任务，因其无梯度、可并行处理非凸代价，但操作任务常包含全程持续成立的约束（闭合运动链、关节极限、障碍物间距），MPPI仅能通过代价作为软惩罚近似满足这些约束，在强任务代价下会失效。

Method: 在每一步rollout中对采样速度执行投影：等式约束将速度限制在子空间中，各不等式约束在该子空间内限制为半空间，从而不等式处理不会破坏等式满足。然而这种投影仅一阶满足约束，有限步长会在等式上产生微小漂移，因此进一步将返回的命令回缩（retract）回约束集，使其达到数值容差且独立于任务权重。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统上验证：仿真中通过关节极限压力测试和随机避障场景，返回命令均使闭合链等式满足达到数值容差；在真实硬件上，Unitree H1-2人形机器人的双臂对移动障碍物进行反应式避障。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI通过将约束嵌入采样动力学内部而非仅作为代价项，实现了操作任务中硬约束的精确满足，弥补了传统MPPI软惩罚方式的不足，并在仿真与真实硬件上均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [49] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将几何智能编码于材料系统中的自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，由连通的立方八面体-八面体单元组成，实现机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合的统一，并通过机器臂和移动装配器演示了家具及建筑尺度的可重构循环建造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料系统与机器人作为独立设计问题，智能主要集中于硬件、传感、运动规划和控制，忽视了通过材料系统本身的几何设计来编码智能，从而简化机器人交互需求并同时满足多维度建造要求。

Method: 设计由立方八面体-八面体复合单元的嵌套点阵模块：立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对齐的明确表面；八面体特征集成可旋拧释放的卡扣连接器及对应受体；通过嵌套排列实现沿x、y、z轴三个方向互锁聚合，并采用机械臂与移动装配器进行组装验证。

Result: 构建了座椅、跨结构、表面及竖向框架等多类结构。压缩测试显示复合模块刚度4556 N/mm、最大载荷3445 N、压缩模量17.5 MPa。模块支持拆卸和跨配置复用。

Conclusion: 将几何智能编码于模块化材料系统中可同时满足机械抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合的需求，实现可重构、循环的建筑建造范式。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [50] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA，一种局部跨层动作条件化机制，通过深度感知的信息路由优化VLM特征到动作解码器的接口设计，在不改变主干架构和监督训练方式的前提下，在多基准上显著提升VLA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中，从预训练VLM中间特征到动作解码器的路由接口研究不足。已有的接口设计要么只暴露表示层级中狭窄的一部分，要么将每个解码器块僵化地对应到单个VLM层，限制了跨深度互补任务证据的访问能力，难以充分利用VLM中不同层次的特征信息。

Method: LIRA将VLM到动作的条件化建模为深度感知的信息路由问题。算法作用于任务令牌特征和从中间VLM状态派生的LIRA查询特征，为每个并行融合块分配一个与对应VLM层深度对齐的局部窗口。并行融合块聚合邻近的LIRA查询特征，并将其与任务令牌特征和本体感知输入整合后进行动作预测。该路由接口不改变主干架构、动作解码器或监督训练流程。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操控任务中，与VLA-Adapter基线使用相同0.5B参数配置相比，LIRA在主要聚合指标上均有提升。在LIBERO-Plus的零样本迁移场景中，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提升了18.9个百分点，表明在受控分布偏移下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LIRA通过局部跨层动作条件化机制，有效改善了VLM与动作解码器之间特征路由的质量，在不增加模型规模或改变训练范式的前提下显著提升了VLA模型的性能和鲁棒性，为VLA架构中接口设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [51] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，集成了抓取生成、可行性预测和自适应精化，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联的联合表征，实现接触前规划与接触后精化的协同，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升抓取成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致抓取鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。人类通过视觉与触觉的无缝整合实现稳定自适应抓取，这一能力在机器人系统中仍难以复现。

Method: 提出统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含三个核心组件：抓取生成、可行性预测和自适应精化。核心创新在于高效的视觉-触觉联合表征，将触觉信号与手指身份关联，实现物体几何与触觉反馈的紧密融合。该表征在规划阶段支持接触感知的抓取位姿生成，在接触后支持触觉引导的精化调整，使系统能够推理细粒度的手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验表明，该方法显著提高了抓取成功率，并在多样化物体上展现了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过统一视觉-触觉信息的联合表征和抓取-预测-精化的一体化框架，该方法有效弥补了现有视觉驱动抓取系统在接触后适应性方面的不足，为机器人在复杂场景中实现稳定自适应抓取提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>

*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩展现实(XR)的3D可视化平台，通过结合5自由度计算机视觉跟踪算法与3D打印机电编码器实现6自由度导管实时跟踪，在20名医学生的概念验证研究中显示其比传统2D可视化快5倍以上、导管行程减少约5倍，并显著提升定位精度和运动控制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术已取得进展，但大多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，缺乏深度感知和空间理解能力，限制了手术的精度和效率，亟需一种能够提供真实3D空间认知的解决方案。

Method: 开发了一个XR平台，将定制机器视觉算法（用于5自由度导管跟踪）与3D打印的机电编码器（测量导管滚动，提供第6自由度）相结合，实现完整的6-DOF运动重建。在概念验证研究中，20名医学生分别在沉浸式3D可视化和传统2D导管室视图下导航心内超声(ICE)导管至6个解剖目标，并对任务完成时间、导管行程、精度、变异性、主观评分及运动学数据进行了分析。

Result: 3D组的任务完成时间平均为54.6秒、导管行程1,939mm，而2D组为267.5秒和7,854mm。3D系统速度提升5倍以上，导管行程减少约5倍。3D模式还提高了定位精度、降低了性能变异性，参与者一致给予3D可视化更高的准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值评分。运动学分析显示3D组在深度轴上的导航更平滑，而2D组依赖重复修正动作。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于XR的3D可视化能够显著提升介入手术训练的效率、精度和运动控制能力，具有在临床培训和实际介入手术中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [53] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GWM-VLA提出了一种几何感知的潜在世界建模框架，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过几何感知多视角状态编码、全局上下文条件的目标视角预测以及由机器人动作监督约束的共享潜在动作表示，显著提升了VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在操纵任务中表现强劲，但面对视觉和环境变化时性能下降。潜在世界建模虽被认为可提升鲁棒性，但现有方法通常独立编码各摄像头视角，并预测整体场景动态，缺乏对多视角几何关系的显式建模，这限制了模型对场景结构的理解和泛化能力。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个关键组件：(1) 几何感知多视角状态编码——通过VGGT-Ω在每个时间步联合聚合多视角观测，构建具有几何信息的潜在状态；(2) 全局上下文条件的目标视角预测——只预测选定目标视角（实验中为腕部视角）的下一时刻patch token，利用多视角聚合后的patch和register token，避免预测完整多视角状态，同时保留几何信息；(3) 共享潜在动作表示——动作表示同时条件化潜在世界模型和流匹配动作头，使潜在预测监督与真实机器人动作监督共同塑造同一潜在动作表示。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界环境中进行的实验都验证了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GWM-VLA通过显式建模多视角几何关系、选择性目标视角预测以及共享潜在动作表示，为VLA学习提供了更具鲁棒性的潜在世界建模框架，在仿真和真实环境中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [54] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRASER（带铰接脊柱的可重构履带机器人），通过可移动铰接点和内部质量实现前后端可达性与运动稳定性的联合优化。采用带式弹簧机构将柔顺性局部化于弯曲区域，同时保持机体其余部分高刚度，以提升前端可达性和质心移动能力。实验实现了分别占机体长度74%、66%和59%的台阶、悬空平台和沟壑越障能力，为已知履带移动机器人中最高水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带机器人在非结构化环境中广泛使用，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的根本性权衡，现有设计难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出可重构履带机器人TRASER，采用带式弹簧机构将柔顺性集中在弯曲区域并保持其余部分高刚度；同时开发几何和静态模型，分析铰接点位置和质心位置对台阶与沟壑越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明TRASER可实现占机体长度74%的台阶越障、66%的悬空平台跨越和59%的沟壑越障，且据作者所知，这些结果为已报道履带移动机器人中最高的越障能力。

Conclusion: TRASER通过同时调节铰接点和内部质量位置，有效打破了前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的固有矛盾，显著提升了履带机器人的越障性能上限。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [55] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的凸多面体间可微有符号距离（Hölder Signed Distance），通过引入Hölder最小和Hölder最大算子替换经典SDF公式中的min-max算子，实现闭式可微计算，避免了迭代算法的收敛问题，并支持GPU并行化，适用于机器人运动规划与控制中的实时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划与控制需要在实时优化中使用可微的距离度量，但欧几里得有符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微，且现有替代方法往往在可微性、符号信息或计算效率之间难以兼顾。

Method: 首先提出名为Hölder minimum和Hölder maximum的可微版本的最小/最大算子，然后用这些算子替换经典SDF公式中的原始min-max算子，得到Hölder signed distance。该距离以闭式形式计算，不依赖迭代算法。

Result: 通过运行时对比实验验证了该距离相对于现有方法的计算性能优势；此外在机器人机械臂实验中证明了其在实际控制应用中的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Hölder signed distance在保持可微性的同时具备闭式计算、无收敛问题、易于GPU并行化等优势，是一种适合机器人控制与规划应用的凸多面体距离度量。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [56] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型多机器人公式化方法，用于解决"最大容量下的有序存储与检索问题"，通过利用无重排（relocation-free）布置的特性，设计了一个在线、优先化的多智能体路径规划算法，在单边界接入的矩形网格仓库中实现高密度存储与高效检索之间的平衡，并保证无死锁、可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库面临存储密度与检索吞吐量之间的根本性权衡。拼图式存储（PBS）通过消除通道提高容量，但在高密度空间中协调多机器人因死锁风险而计算困难。

Method: 提出一种"有序存储与检索问题"的多机器人新公式化方法，聚焦于单边界可达的矩形网格。利用无重排布置的几何可行性特性，指导一种在线、优先化的多智能体路径规划算法。该算法利用特定存储布置的不变量保证完备性并防止死锁，同时通过鲁棒存储布置处理离场序列的不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，在机器人数量达到网格宽度C之前，makespan随机器人数量呈近似线性改善。支持鲁棒性的算法开销可忽略不计：处理离场序列不确定性时，执行速度与非鲁棒基线相比无显著损失。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，相比通用集中式规划器，能利用存储布置的特定不变量实现完备性保障和死锁预防，从而具备可扩展性，在无需显著性能代价的前提下提供鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [57] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于clothoid（欧拉螺旋）与自适应前视距离的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，通过准反馈控制算法实时构造最优clothoid轨迹并引入前馈滤波器补偿横向动力学延迟，在TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真中验证了其在多种驾驶场景下的横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横向控制方法在路径跟踪过程中存在曲率突变问题，导致乘客不适和车辆侧翻风险。本研究旨在通过平滑的曲率过渡（欧拉螺旋）和自适应前视机制，提升自动驾驶车辆在不同道路几何和车辆动态条件下的横向控制性能。

Method: 该方法基于clothoid（欧拉螺旋）构建平滑曲率过渡路径；采用自适应前视距离机制，根据实时车辆动力学和道路几何动态调整前视距离；通过准反馈控制算法在每个时间步构造最优clothoid以生成转向输入；引入前馈滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学延迟，提升控制响应速度与稳定性。采用TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真进行验证。

Result: 联合仿真结果表明，所提出的控制器在多种驾驶场景下均显著提升了横向控制性能，包括更高的路径跟踪精度和更好的横向稳定性，同时降低了乘客不适感和侧翻风险。

Conclusion: 基于clothoid的自适应横向控制策略有效改善了自动驾驶车辆在复杂道路条件下的路径跟踪性能。未来工作将扩展至更高车速场景，并优化铰接车辆的离轨误差最小化问题。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [58] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
