*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoCoNav框架，结合在线共形校准与运行时认证规划，用于人群中的安全高效机器人导航。通过水平特定的共形比例-积分控制器自适应轨迹误差边界，并通过"先松弛后验证"的规划器保证求解可行性，同时实现碰撞避免、任务成功与导航效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式方法易产生振荡行为，预测性规划器要么将预测视为精确、要么依赖受限的误差模型，而将保守的不确定性集作为硬约束又可能导致模型预测控制(MPC)不可行。需要一种既能处理预测误差不确定性又能保持规划器可行性的导航框架。

Method: CoCoNav由两部分组成：(1) 水平特定的共形比例-积分(conformal PI)控制器，用于在线调节轨迹误差界以控制长时间经验覆盖率，适应变化的预测误差；(2) "先松弛后验证"(relax-then-verify)规划器，先用软约束MPC生成标称轨迹以保持求解可行性，再在执行前对标称轨迹及应急机动动作依据校准后的误差界进行单独认证。

Result: 仿真与四足机器人实验表明，CoCoNav在碰撞避免、任务成功率与导航效率方面相较于所评估的基线方法取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: CoCoNav通过将共形校准与运行时认证规划相结合，有效解决了人群导航中预测误差不确定性与MPC可行性问题，实现了安全性与效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高海拔雪地环境的无人机(UAV)-无人车(UGV)协同导航框架，通过定制的U-Net进行实时道路分割（精度96.5%），结合EKF融合GPS/IMU实现无人机定位（误差±0.5米），利用YOLOv5与深度数据追踪UGV，并采用动态路径规划应对雪堆障碍，在能见度低、地面不稳定的环境中实现了成功导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔雪地地形中能见度低且地面不稳定，传统导航方法失效，亟需一种可靠的协同导航解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出满足实时计算约束的高效U-Net架构，结合新颖的合成雪数据增强技术实现道路分割；2) 采用扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)融合机载GPS和IMU数据实现无人机定位；3) 通过YOLOv5和无人机RGB-D相机深度数据构建视觉追踪管线确定UGV位置；4) 利用分割结果驱动动态路径规划算法以适应雪堆漂移。

Result: 道路分割准确率达96.5%，无人机最大位置误差为±0.5米，在能见度受限的测试环境中以最小偏差成功完成导航。

Conclusion: 所提出的UAV-UGV协同框架能够有效应对高海拔雪地的恶劣环境挑战，通过视觉分割、融合定位与动态路径规划的协同作用，实现了可靠导航。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [60] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过材料选择（而非解剖结构）在软垫内部创建额外约束边界，以扩展"刚性指甲约束"对薄平物体（如硬币）夹持的有效工作范围。实验将Shore E10中心与Shore A60侧边的混合刚度仿生指尖与均匀E10指尖进行对比，发现混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持高成功率，但均匀材质指尖在沿手指轴的硬币位置变化时表现更优，呈现条件依赖性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄平物体（如硬币）在硬表面上的机器人抓取仍然具有挑战性，传统方法需要重新定向物体、接触其底面或增加专用指甲机构。作者此前证明刚性指甲能抑制软垫变形并形成几何约束以改善精密抓取，本研究想探究能否在垫内通过材料选择创建额外的约束边界，以扩展该约束成立的条件范围。

Method: 制作中心为Shore E10硅胶、两侧为Shore A60的混合刚度仿生指尖，与均匀E10软指尖对比。使用自动化装置执行倾斜旋转指尖捏取，使垫接触硬币侧面并从无间隙的平齐接触中抬起硬币。系统变化水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转等参数进行测试，并额外进行了沿指轴位置变化和不同硬币尺寸（六种日本硬币面值）的试验。

Result: 混合刚度指在更多测试条件下比均匀材质指保持更高的成功率（在几何约束形成和向稳定抓取过渡阶段均如此）；无指甲的对照组在所有36种条件中全部失败；但均匀材质指在硬币沿指轴位置变化时表现更好；经尺寸调优后，两种指尖均能抓取全部六种日本面值硬币。

Conclusion: 薄物抓取的工作范围不仅取决于垫的软硬度，还取决于刚度在指甲支撑垫内的放置位置。边界放置（boundary placement）有望成为指尖设计中的一个候选设计变量。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [61] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPF，一个无需训练的模态概率融合框架，用于检测和纠正视觉-语言-动作策略训练数据中的指令-轨迹不匹配（ITM）问题，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人数据上显著提升检测准确率和下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集中可能存在一种隐蔽但有害的故障模式：轨迹在行为上是正确的，但配对了错误的语言指令（即指令-轨迹不匹配，ITM）。与失败的滚动轨迹不同，ITM看起来往往合理，但会污染策略学习到的语言-行为映射关系，影响下游任务性能。因此需要一种有效的审计方法来识别和纠正这类问题。

Method: 提出Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF)训练免费审计框架。将每个模态视为一个专家，通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布，然后使用预测熵权重以乘积专家（Product of Experts）方式融合多模态信息，实现ITM检测与标签纠正。

Result: 在LIBERO基准（注入指令不匹配）和带噪声的真实机器人数据上，MMPF在ITM检测和标签纠正准确率上均达到最优。审计能改善大多数需要语言来消除任务歧义的下游策略学习。真实机器人实验表明该方法可提升策略性能，并揭示了过滤演示与重标记之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 多模态概率融合的审计方法能有效识别和纠正指令-轨迹不匹配，进而提升下游视觉-语言-动作策略的学习效果；在实际应用中需根据需求权衡过滤与重标记两种处理策略。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [62] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表征对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），弥补2D像素空间与3D动作执行空间之间的表征差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）联合建模视频预测和动作生成，但通常在2D像素空间中表示视频，与机器人动作执行的3D空间存在表征鸿沟。现有的3D方法虽引入了3D信息，却未能充分利用3D结构的动态特性。

Method: 提出4D-WAM训练策略，通过两个互补目标函数注入时空知识：1）运动对齐（motion alignment），对齐相邻帧间的时序特征变化，促进局部4D感知；2）目的地对齐（destination alignment），通过最小化源帧与最终目的地之间的注意力相似度分布差距，引导模型推断最终目标。两者共同提供局部运动监督和长时程目标引导，使模型学习轨迹级时空表征。

Result: 在多种基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，模型在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和通用性方面均取得了改进。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过局部与全局双重对齐目标，有效提升了WAMs对4D时空动态的理解和动作执行能力，展示了跨模型的可迁移性和通用性。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [63] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DA-NBV，一种方向感知的下一最佳视角规划策略，通过将方向观测统计融入占据状态，配合可学习的Position Advantage Field (PAF)、局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖率塑形奖励，在海上船舶3D重建中显著提升完整性和效率，并配套构建了SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况模拟环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上船舶3D重建对海事监管和自主作业至关重要，但传统高质量数据采集依赖人工设计轨迹或熟练操作者，成本高且难以扩展。现有NBV策略仅建模空间占据而忽略方向观测历史，导致船舶复杂上层结构和严重自遮挡下重建不完整；同时海面波浪引起的升沉、横摇、纵摇不断改变船舶姿态和可见性，加上风干扰与有限机载功率对扫描效率的更高要求，进一步加剧了挑战。

Method: 提出DA-NBV方向感知NBV策略：(1) 在传统占据状态基础上增强方向观测统计信息；(2) 引入可学习的Position Advantage Field (PAF)利用方向信息引导视点选择；(3) 采用局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖率塑形奖励提升扫描效率；(4) 构建面向船舶的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况模拟环境。

Result: 在不同升沉、横摇和纵摇条件下的实验中，DA-NBV将重建完整性提升约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，同时实现了更高的路径效率。

Conclusion: DA-NBV通过引入方向感知策略有效解决了海上动态环境下船舶复杂结构的3D重建完整性问题，相较现有NBV方法在重建质量和路径效率上均取得显著提升，为海事场景下自动化数据采集提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [64] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态地平线（TSH）概念，用于衡量智能体需跟踪的任务相关状态转换跨度，并构建 RoboGraph 任务编译器及包含 588 个回合、84 个场景的基准，评估 15 种先进智能体模型，发现多数模型在长状态地平线下存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要通过动作序列长度和子任务复杂度来衡量任务难度，忽视了智能体需跟踪由自身探索和环境动态引起的演化任务状态这一独特挑战。

Method: 定义任务状态地平线（TSH）作为衡量指标；提出 RoboGraph，一种将状态转换依赖编译为可执行符号图的机器人任务编译器；构建包含空间和时间因果依赖（包括任务执行中意外失败和干预诱导的依赖）的基准，共 588 个回合、84 个场景。

Result: 在语义和视觉闭环环境中评估 15 种先进智能体模型，发现大多数模型在高 TSH 任务下表现困难，在维护、探索和更新长时程任务相关状态方面存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 当前前沿智能体模型在长任务状态地平线下的跟踪能力不足，RoboGraph 基准揭示了这一被现有评测忽视的重要维度，为长时程具身智能评估提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [65] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysX-CoT提出将单图像3D资产生成问题重构为显式的结构化物理推理过程，通过可解析的部件级状态轨迹（涵盖分解、2D/3D定位、关系、粗几何与表面线索）进行监督、条件化几何生成并作为奖励目标，在统一评测协议下超越最先进的全任务基线，并在Unreal Engine 5中实现高有效性的物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D资产生成方法采用"输出中心"视角，将图像到3D的推理隐式地封装在全局坐标令牌流中，导致部件放置与局部形状纠缠，且中间物理状态无法用于监督、条件化或验证，成为性能提升的瓶颈。

Method: 将生成任务建模为有序且机器可解析的部件级状态轨迹；几何解耦为3D包围盒承载放置信息与局部编码承载形状信息；对各类状态分别监督、用于条件化几何生成和作为奖励目标；采用CoT对齐的GRPO优化解析有效性、定位、几何、放置与物理一致性。

Result: 在统一协议（相同骨干网络、数据与冻结解码器）下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上全面优于最接近的全任务基线；Oracle、令牌匹配和状态顺序对照实验表明显式状态是功能性的而非装饰性的；在Unreal Engine 5中生成的资产具有高解析、碰撞与关节化有效性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化物理推理状态相比隐式输出式方法能更好地引导单图像3D资产生成，中间状态的可监督性、可条件化性与可验证性提升了最终资产的质量与物理可用性。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency. Under a unified protocol that retrains all learned baselines on the same backbone, data, and frozen decoder, PhysX-CoT outperforms the closest full-task baseline across geometry, scale, and physical-attribute metrics. Oracle, token-matched, and state-order controls show the explicit states are functional rather than cosmetic, and in Unreal Engine~5 the generated assets parse, collide, and articulate at high validity.

</details>


### [66] [SurgWMBench: A Vision-Based Benchmark for World-Modeling Surgical Instrument Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08070)
*Huanrong Liu,Weiliang Huang,Bob Zhang,Weichao Cai,Chunlin Tian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgWMBench，一个面向手术运动规划与动力学预测的视觉基准，用于评估手术世界模型在短时程器械运动预测和连续推演稳定性方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法聚焦于识别当前手术阶段或模仿专家示范，缺乏对器械运动如何重塑后续手术状态的显式建模；已有的工具运动预测方法虽能预测轨迹，但未捕获与未来手术视频状态的耦合演化；现有手术世界模型研究的评估指标（如FVD、CD-FVD）偏向视觉生成质量，无法直接衡量预测轨迹的几何准确性、时间连贯性及对下游规划的可执行性；此外，领域缺乏公开数据集和标准化评估协议。

Method: 提出SurgWMBench基准，基于术中图像序列和历史器械轨迹输入，评估两方面的能力：(1) 近未来器械运动预测；(2) 在连续滚动推演或输入扰动条件下的稳定性。

Result: 构建了面向手术世界模型的运动中心化基准评测框架，弥补了现有生成导向指标（FVD/CD-FVD）与器械运动规划需求之间的对齐缺口。

Conclusion: SurgWMBench为手术场景下的运动感知世界模型提供了标准化的评估基础设施，推动手术规划从单纯的步骤识别和示范模仿走向真正面向动力学预测的运动规划。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models that move beyond recognizing the current surgical step or imitating expert demonstrations, and instead anticipate how instrument motion reshapes subsequent operative states. Most surgical video understanding methods focus on recognizing phases, actions, or workflow states, while providing limited support for explicitly modeling instrument motion. Conversely, existing tool motion prediction methods can forecast instrument trajectories, but they generally do not capture the coupled evolution of future surgical video states. World models offer a natural framework for jointly modeling visual state transitions and instrument motion dynamics. However, existing surgical world model studies remain largely centered on visual generation quality, relying on generation-oriented metrics such as FVD and CD-FVD. These metrics are poorly aligned with instrument motion planning, as they do not directly measure whether predicted trajectories are geometrically accurate, temporally coherent, or actionable for downstream planning. This limitation is partly structural, since the field lacks public datasets and standardized evaluation protocols that provide the benchmarking infrastructure needed to assess motion-centric capabilities in surgical world models. In this paper, we introduce SurgWMBench, a vision-based benchmark for short-horizon surgical motion planning and dynamics prediction. Given intraoperative image sequences and historical instrument trajectory, SurgWMBench evaluates both near-future instrument motion prediction and stability under continuous rollout or input perturbations.

</details>


### [67] [Event-Time Hybrid Optimal Control for Robotic Table Tennis Serves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08157)
*Thomas Gossard,Till Köpff,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于事件时间最优控制（OCP）的机器人乒乓球发球生成方法，同时优化球拍撞击速度和朝向以及弹跳、过网和落点时间，在相边界直接施加落台和过网约束；通过第二个OCP将球拍运动转化为满足关节位置、速度和力矩限制的完整运动。数值评估和KUKA Agilus真机实验表明，相比固定步长+根定位方法，求解时间降低4.1倍且精度相当，可实现受控的上旋、下旋和侧旋发球。

。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人乒乓球发球要求高球速和高旋转，同时需满足机器人动力学运动学限制。与传统对打击球不同，有效发球必须先在己方台面弹跳并越过球网，构成具有非线性飞行和碰撞动力学的混合系统，这使得发球轨迹生成成为一个复杂的最优控制问题。

Method: 将含旋转的发球生成建模为事件时间最优控制问题（OCP），将球拍撞击速度/朝向与弹跳时刻、过网时刻和落地时刻一起作为优化变量，从而在相位边界直接强化台面弹跳和过网约束。随后通过第二个OCP将球拍参考运动转化为满足关节位置、速度和力矩限制的完整运动学可行轨迹。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比固定步长+根定位方法，事件时间公式将中位求解时间降低4.1倍，同时保持相当的落点精度、旋转精度和发球有效性。KUKA Agilus真机实验实现了受控落点及上旋、下旋、侧旋发球，平均落点误差为13.1±7.3 cm，旋转速率最高达30 rps。

Conclusion: 事件时间最优控制方法能高效地生成物理有效、运动学动力学可行的发球轨迹，同时充分考虑了非线性空气动力学和碰撞效应，验证了该框架在机器人乒乓球发球任务中的实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis serves require high ball velocity and spin while respecting the robot's kinodynamic limits. Unlike rally strokes, a valid serve must also bounce on the server's side and clear the net, yielding a hybrid system with nonlinear flight and impact dynamics. We formulate spin-controlled serve generation as an event-time \ac{OCP} that optimizes the racket impact velocity and orientation together with the bounce, net-crossing, and landing times, enabling direct enforcement at phase boundaries of table-bounce and net-clearance constraints. The racket velocity and orientation are then converted into a complete kinodynamically feasible motion through a second \ac{OCP} enforcing joint-position, velocity, and torque limits. We evaluate the method numerically and on a KUKA Agilus robot. Compared with a fixed-step formulation with root localization, the proposed event-time formulation reduces the median solve time by a factor of 4.1 while maintaining comparable landing accuracy, spin accuracy, and serve validity. Real-robot experiments demonstrate controlled placement and topspin, backspin, and sidespin serves, with a mean landing error of $13.1 \pm 7.3$~cm and spin rates up to 30~rps. These results show that event-time optimal control efficiently generates physically valid, kinodynamically feasible serves while accounting for nonlinear aerodynamic and impact effects.

</details>


### [68] [Multi-modal Interactive Control of Robotic Arm based on Offline Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08183)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种基于离线大语言模型的"苏格拉底模型- ChatGLM"算法，用于在PyBullet平台上实现机械臂的多模态交互控制，能够以低成本处理复杂的文本-图像集成多步长时序机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数大语言模型（如ChatGPT）并非开源友好，用户需要持续付费使用AI服务。因此，在本地服务器上部署开源大语言模型被视为一种成本更低、使用更稳定的方案，用于设计和实现创造性的具身AI算法。

Method: 基于"苏格拉底模型"框架与ChatGLM相结合，在PyBullet仿真平台上构建多模态交互控制的机械臂算法，利用离线部署的大语言模型处理文本与图像集成的复杂操控指令。

Result: 该算法在多模态机械臂控制中表现出良好性能，并且展现出处理复杂的文本-图像集成多步长时序机器人操作任务的潜力。

Conclusion: 部署开源大语言模型于本地服务器是一种高效途径，能够以更低成本实现稳定的具身AI算法，"苏格拉底模型-ChatGLM"验证了这一方向的可行性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly revolutionized the modern society with numerous advanced interactions between humans and AI agents, whereas the usage of most large language models including ChatGPT are not friendly open-sourced and must require the users paying a lot for such AI services continuously. Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以人为本的个人化运动缩放方法，根据每位操作者的延迟、方向和距离特征拟合个性化缩放参数，以应对遥操作中的通信延迟问题。20名参与者的虚拟延迟到达任务实验和遥手术机器人评估表明，运动缩放可带来高达20-25%的性能提升，尤其在较长延迟下效果显著，个性化方案在特定条件下进一步提升了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信延迟是遥操作中的核心挑战，会破坏视觉-运动协调并降低任务精度。运动缩放是抑制延迟引起过冲的有效对策，但现有方法依赖统一增益，忽视了个体差异和情境变化（如延迟、方向、距离），缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出一种人类中心化的方法，为每位参与者拟合延迟、方向、距离特定的个性化缩放参数。在虚拟模拟器中，缩放增益通过最小化各条件组合下的平均过冲来计算。评估同时在虚拟模拟和遥手术机器人上进行，使用过冲量、端点误差、轨迹平滑度、运动经济性及复合误差-时间指标衡量性能，覆盖多种延迟、距离和运动方向。

Result: 与无辅助条件相比，运动缩放一致性地改善了性能，关键指标提升高达20-25%，且在较长延迟下效果最为显著。个性化方案在中度延迟下、短距离内向运动场景中展现出额外的精度优势。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了个性化缩放作为更自适应遥操作框架基础的潜力，该框架可整合情境信息以提升遥操作手术过程的安全性与精度。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [70] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALVA是一种基于动作和语言条件的视频轨迹评估器，通过两阶段VLM流程（先总结帧间视觉转换，再对照指令评估生成总结）为具身智能体提供离散的轨迹级进度分数，在模拟3D家居环境中表现出近零假阳性的保守评估模式，并能作为闭环策略优化的有效终端反馈，缩小与真实标注oracle的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的具身智能体在执行多步自然语言指令时，需要能够评估完整轨迹任务进度的反馈机制。现有的基于最终帧匹配或连续嵌入相似度的方法可能忽略判断指令是否完成所必需的中间转换过程。

Method: ALVA采用预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）分两阶段工作：(1) 基于执行的动作序列对帧间视觉转换进行总结；(2) 将生成的总结与自然语言指令进行对照评估，输出离散的轨迹级进度分数。评估时同时考虑视觉观测、执行的动作序列和自然语言指令三个输入条件。

Result: 在模拟3D家居环境中，ALVA表现出保守的评估模式，假阳性率接近零。作为闭环策略优化的终端反馈时，其反馈效果优于静态图像和基于嵌入的视觉基线方法，并缩小了与真实标注oracle之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 动作和语言条件化的视频评估可以作为所评估模拟具身控制任务中一种可解释的反馈机制，有效补充现有基于静态图像或嵌入相似度的反馈方法的不足。

Abstract: Vision-based embodied agents executing multi-step natural language instructions require feedback mechanisms that assess task progress over complete trajectories. Conventional approaches based on final-frame matching or continuous embedding similarity may overlook intermediate transitions that are necessary for determining whether an instruction has been completed. We propose ALVA (Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment), a trajectory evaluator that conditions its assessment on visual observations, the executed action sequence, and the natural language instruction. The method uses a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM) in two stages: it first summarizes frame-to-frame visual transitions conditioned on the executed actions and then assesses the generated summary with respect to the instruction to produce a discrete trajectory-level progress score. In simulated 3D household environments, ALVA exhibits a conservative assessment pattern with near-zero false-positive rates. When used as terminal feedback for closed-loop policy optimization, it provides more effective feedback than the evaluated static image and embedding-based visual baselines and reduces the performance gap to a ground-truth oracle. These results support action- and language-conditioned video assessment as an interpretable feedback mechanism for the evaluated simulated embodied-control tasks.

</details>


### [71] [Scalable High-Speed Lateral Control for Single-Body and Articulated Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08293)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的横向控制框架，用于高速场景下单体及铰接式自动驾驶车辆（如卡车-拖挂）的鲁棒路径跟踪。该框架基于回旋曲线（clothoid）控制器，通过三项关键改进实现：集成切向检查和Fréchet距离的预瞄优化、实时轨迹段分类以动态调整预瞄搜索范围、以及响应横向误差和前方路径几何的双自适应速率控制预瞄机制。框架还支持灵活的控制点选择和曲率-转向查表，无需改变核心架构即可控制牵引车后轴、铰接点或挂车位置。在TruckSim和Simulink高保真仿真中，对双车道变更和蜿蜒道路场景进行了验证，结果显示高速下稳定跟踪、振荡减少、低横向误差和可接受的横向加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速场景下，单体车辆和铰接式车辆（如牵引车-挂车）的自主路径跟踪面临显著挑战：传统预瞄控制方法在高速时容易产生振荡，铰接式车辆由于多刚体动力学特性进一步增加了控制难度。现有方法往往针对单一车型设计，缺乏可扩展的统一控制架构，难以同时适用于单体和铰接式车辆。

Method: 基于回旋曲线（clothoid）控制器进行扩展，包含三个核心改进：(1) 集成切向检查和Fréchet距离方法，用于优化预瞄点选择和抑制振荡；(2) 实时轨迹段分类，根据轨迹特征动态调整预瞄搜索范围；(3) 双自适应速率控制的预瞄机制，同时响应横向误差和前方路径几何形状。此外，引入灵活的控制点选择和曲率-转向查表，支持牵引车后轴、铰接点及挂车相关位置的控制，无需更改核心控制架构。

Result: 通过TruckSim和Simulink高保真仿真在双车道变更和蜿蜒道路场景中验证，结果表明：该框架在高速条件下实现了稳定的路径跟踪、减少了振荡、预瞄机制自适应有效、横向误差低，且在各车辆配置下保持可接受的横向加速度。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持采用一种统一的横向控制架构，能够从单体自动驾驶车辆扩展适用于铰接式自动驾驶车辆，为多车型通用的横向控制提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable lateral control framework for robust path tracking of single-body and articulated autonomous vehicles at high speeds. A clothoid-based controller is extended with three key adaptations: 1) an integrated tangential check and Frechet distance method for optimized lookahead and oscillation mitigation; 2) real-time trajectory segment classification for dynamic adjustment of the lookahead search range; and 3) a dual-adaptive, rate-controlled lookahead mechanism responsive to cross-track error and upcoming path geometry. To support different vehicle configurations, the framework also incorporates flexible tracking point selection and curvature-to-steering lookup tables, enabling control of points such as the tractor rear axle, hitch point, or trailer-related locations without changing the core control architecture. The controller is evaluated using high-fidelity TruckSim and Simulink simulations for both standalone and articulated vehicles across dual lane changes and winding-road scenarios. Results demonstrate stable high-speed path tracking, reduced oscillations, effective lookahead adaptation, low cross-track errors, and acceptable lateral acceleration across different vehicle configurations. The results support the use of a unified lateral control architecture that can scale from single-body to articulated autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [72] [Diminishing Returns of Intelligence: The Non-Linear Relationship Between LLM Scale and User Perception in Short-Duration Open-Ended Social Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08320)
*Amanda Rasille Røn Volf,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过19名参与者的组内实验，探究LLM参数规模（4B、8B、30B）对短时人机社交互动中用户感知的影响。结果显示更大模型并未带来显著的用户感知优势，且4B模型在自然度和智能感知上与30B模型无显著差异，提示模型规模在短暂开放式社交HRI中存在收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地驱动具身社交智能体，但尚不清楚在短暂的人机交互场景中，更大规模的LLM是否能显著改善用户感知体验。现有研究缺乏对短时、开放式社交交互中模型参数大小影响的系统考察。

Method: 采用组内实验设计，19名参与者与由Qwen3-VL系列（4B、8B、30B参数）驱动的机器人面部进行交互，从感知智能、自然度、愉悦度和幽默感四个维度进行评估。

Result: 30B模型相对小模型无显著整体偏好优势；在感知自然度和智能方面与4B模型无显著差异；AI交互频率与30B模型的智能排名存在显著关系，表明经验丰富的用户对模型能力差异更敏感。

Conclusion: 在短暂开放式社交HRI中，模型规模扩展存在收益递减；对话流畅性、响应性和社交适宜行为可能与原始参数量同等重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to drive embodied social agents, yet it remains unclear whether larger models improve user perception during brief human-robot encounters. This paper examines the effect of LLM parameter size on short-duration, open-ended social interactions with a robot interface. In a within-subjects study, 19 participants interacted with robot faces driven by Qwen3-VL models at 4B, 8B, and 30B parameters. Participants evaluated the interactions in terms of perceived intelligence, naturalness, enjoyment, and humor. Results showed no significant overall preference for the 30B model over the smaller variants, including no significant advantage over the 4B model in perceived naturalness or intelligence. A significant relationship between AI interaction frequency and intelligence rankings for the 30B model suggests that more experienced users may be more sensitive to differences in model capability. Overall, the findings indicate diminishing returns from model scaling in brief open-ended social HRI, where conversational flow, responsiveness, and socially appropriate behavior potentially matter as much as raw parameter count.

</details>


### [73] [MPPI Planning with Gaussian Based Human Cost Function for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08323)
*Chinmay Mundane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PGIF-MPPI，一种将行人预测信息编码为时空各向异性高斯排斥场的安全导航规划方法，在300个随机人群场景中实现0%碰撞率，性能优于传统MPPI（最高82%碰撞率），且无计算开销增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MPPI规划器将行人视为当前静态点障碍物，在动态拥挤场景中会低估行人运动带来的碰撞风险，导致导航安全性不足。需要一种能够预测行人未来位置并将其纳入规划的成本函数。

Method: 提出Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF)框架：将行人的预测轨迹沿整个规划时域传播，编码为沿行人运动方向对齐的各向异性高斯排斥场。每个场的向前扩散范围随行人速度增长，形成"运动锥"危险区，对进入行人行进路径的机器人轨迹施加强惩罚，而对从后方接近的轨迹惩罚较弱。该成本函数为闭式表达，可跨rollout全并行计算。

Result: 在三个密度水平共300个随机人群场景中，PGIF-MPPI在各个密度下均达到0%碰撞率，而传统MPPI碰撞率最高可达82%；同时保持了实时规划性能，无额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 将行人的运动预测显式编码进MPPI成本函数，能够在不牺牲计算效率的前提下显著提升拥挤动态环境中的机器人导航安全性，为基于采样的规划器提供了一种简洁有效的时空风险建模方法。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in crowded spaces requires planning that accounts for where people will be, not only where they are now. Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is an effective sampling-based planner, but many implementations encode humans as static point obstacles at their current positions, underestimating risk in dynamic scenes. We propose Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF), a spatiotemporal cost formulation that propagates pedestrian predictions forward over the full planning horizon and encodes them as anisotropic Gaussian repulsive fields aligned with each pedestrian's direction of motion. The forward spread of each field grows with the pedestrian's speed, creating a motion cone danger zone that penalises robot trajectories entering the pedestrian's path of travel more strongly than those approaching from behind. The formulation is closed-form and fully parallelisable across rollouts, adding no measurable computational overhead. Evaluated over 300 randomised crowd scenarios at three density levels, PGIF-MPPI achieves a 0\% collision rate at every density level, compared with up to 82\% for vanilla MPPI, while maintaining real-time planning performance.

</details>


### [74] [Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Networks on Lie Groups for Learning Underwater Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08356)
*Evan F. Palmer,Ross L. Hatton,Geoffrey A. Hollinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的随机水下航行器动力学学习框架，利用欧拉-庞加莱动力学和李群几何构建随机物理信息神经网络，在仿真和真实港口环境中验证了其精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器的精确运动模型对于海洋任务（如基础设施检查和科学采样）的自主执行至关重要，但传统基于物理的方法难以准确刻画其复杂的随机动力学特性。

Method: 利用Euler-Poincaré动力学和李群几何，构建随机物理信息神经网络架构，保持水下航行器的物理与几何约束；采用保结构的随机积分方法，结合矩匹配和有限维匹配实现几何一致的训练。

Result: 在仿真环境以及水下航行器穿越港口码头桩柱的真实场景中，该方法能够学习到精确且稳健的动力学模型，支持在复杂海洋环境中进行安全基于模型的控制。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架有效克服了传统物理方法在水下航行器动力学建模中的局限性，同时通过学习准确稳健的随机动力学模型，为复杂海洋环境下的安全自主控制提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Accurate models of underwater vehicle motion are needed for autonomous execution of marine tasks like infrastructure inspection and scientific sampling. However, such motion is challenging to characterize using traditional physics-based methods. This paper presents a novel data-driven framework for learning stochastic underwater vehicle dynamics. Using Euler-Poincaré dynamics and the geometry of Lie groups, we develop a stochastic physics-informed neural network architecture that respects the physical and geometric constraints of underwater vehicles. Our approach leverages structure-preserving stochastic integration and builds upon moment matching and finite dimensional matching to ensure geometrically-consistent training. We evaluate our approach in simulation and on an underwater vehicle navigating dock pylons in a harbor environment. The results demonstrate that our method learns accurate and robust dynamics models, enabling safe model-based control in challenging marine environments.

</details>


### [75] [EsaacSim: A Multimodal Event Camera Add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08522)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Malte Kuhlmann,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero,Miguel Campusano,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EsaacSim，一个面向NVIDIA Isaac Sim的多模态事件相机仿真插件，支持灰度与Bayer RGGB事件生成、多分辨率配置，并同步输出RGB、APS、事件、深度和IMU数据（通过ROS2接口）。通过运动引导的帧间间隙合成策略提升有效时间分辨率，在RTX 4060 GPU上实现240–960Hz有效事件率，单帧事件生成耗时6.98–29.16ms，额外GPU内存占用低于400MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机作为机器人感知的重要范式，其应用受限于传感器可用性不足以及缺乏与现代机器人平台集成的一体化仿真工具，亟需一个支持在线、多模态、可配置的事件相机仿真解决方案。

Method: 在Isaac Sim中构建事件相机插件，支持灰度与Bayer RGGB事件生成、多分辨率配置；采用运动引导的帧间间隙合成（motion-guided frame-gap synthesis）策略提高有效时间分辨率，同时保持与Isaac Sim渲染管线的兼容性；通过原生ROS2接口同步输出RGB、APS、事件、深度与IMU数据。

Result: 在代表性机器人场景中实现同步多模态仿真；在NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU上支持五种事件相机分辨率，有效事件率240–960Hz；灰度事件生成耗时6.98–27.28ms，Bayer RGGB事件耗时7.58–29.16ms；额外GPU显存占用低于400MB。

Conclusion: EsaacSim可实现在线多模态事件相机仿真，为机器人研究与合成数据生成提供支持；作者已发布早期版本并公开当前架构与性能数据。

Abstract: Event-based vision is becoming an increasingly important sensing paradigm for robotics, yet its adoption remains limited by sensor availability and the lack of integrated simulation tools for modern robotics platforms. This paper presents EsaacSim, a multimodal event camera add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that enables online simulation of configurable event cameras with grayscale and Bayer RGGB event generation. The framework supports multiple event camera resolutions and provides synchronized RGB, APS, event, depth, and IMU outputs through native ROS2 interfaces. A motion-guided frame-gap synthesis strategy further increases the effective temporal resolution while preserving compatibility with the Isaac Sim rendering pipeline. Experimental evaluation demonstrates synchronized multimodal simulation across representative robotic scenes and efficient online performance over five event camera resolutions at effective event rates from 240 to 960Hz. Event stream generation requires 6.98--27.28ms for grayscale events and 7.58--29.16ms for Bayer RGGB events while using less than 400MB of additional GPU memory on an NVIDIA RTX~4060 GPU. These results show that EsaacSim enables supports online multimodal event-camera simulation for robotics research and synthetic data generation. We release an early version of the simulator and report its current architecture and performance.

</details>


### [76] [Curriculum Generation under Structured Parametric Environments for Robust Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08545)
*Prishita Ray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重参数化的课程生成框架，通过单向梯度优化在结构化连续环境参数空间中进行课程调整，并结合分布偏移正则化目标增强多模态观测下的鲁棒性，在连续控制环境中显著优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体的鲁棒导航策略需要在不断变化的环境条件（如转向率、障碍物、摩擦、坑洞、坡度等）下泛化。课程生成提供了一种原理性的机制来提升泛化能力，但设计样本高效且自动化的课程仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出重参数化的课程生成框架，针对结构化连续环境参数使用单向基于梯度的优化方法；针对图像和标量输入组成的多模态观测空间，引入分布偏移正则化目标以鼓励学习更细粒度的潜在表示。

Result: 在两种连续控制的OpenAI Gym环境（2D障碍物的Car Racing变体和Bipedal Walker变体）中，在五个随机种子下，该方法一致优于vanilla策略训练、随机参数采样、手动课程、前沿方法、SPRL、ALP-GMM以及反向课程学习等基线。消融实验证实了重参数化课程机制的有效性，并揭示了辅助正则化目标的收益具有环境依赖性。

Conclusion: 重参数化课程生成框架能够以样本高效且自动化的方式在连续环境参数空间中生成有效的课程，分布偏移正则化在多模态观测下进一步提升了鲁棒性，整体方案在不同连续控制任务中展现出良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Robust navigation policies for autonomous agents must generalize across continuously varying environmental conditions such as turn rates, obstacles, friction, pits, and slopes. Curriculum generation provides a principled mechanism for improving generalization by progressively adapting training environments, but designing such curricula in a sample-efficient and automated manner remains challenging. This paper proposes a reparameterized curriculum generation framework for structured continuous environment parameters using unidirectional gradient-based optimization. To improve robustness in multimodal observation spaces consisting of image-based and scalar inputs, a distribution-shift regularization objective is incorporated to encourage the learning of finer-grained latent representations. The proposed method is evaluated across two continuous-control OpenAI Gym environments: a 2D obstacle-based Car Racing variant and Bipedal Walker variant, where coupled environment parameters jointly influence policy performance. Across five random seeds, our method consistently outperforms vanilla policy training, random parameter sampling, manual curricula, frontier-based methods, Self-Paced Reinforcement Learning (SPRL), Absolute Learning Progress with Gaussian Mixture Models (ALP-GMM), and reverse curriculum learning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the reparameterized curriculum mechanism across both environments, while highlighting environment-dependent benefits of the auxiliary regularization objective.

</details>


### [77] [Vid2WAM: Distilling Video Diffusion Priors into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08558)
*Chenhao Qiu,Ruixiang Wang,Runyi Zhao,Sixu Lin,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia,Yanwei Fu,Simo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vid2WAM是一个离线蒸馏框架，利用大规模视频基础模型的视觉扩散先验，将任务条件未来视频和逆动力学模型生成的伪动作蒸馏到紧凑的World Action Model（WAM）学生模型中，在免去大量专家示范依赖的同时提升新任务泛化能力和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM通过联合建模未来视觉动态和动作来提升机器人策略学习，但其可扩展性和泛化能力受限于对昂贵专家示范数据的依赖。作者质疑未来监督是否必须来自目标任务的专家轨迹，探索利用视频基础模型作为替代监督来源。

Method: Vid2WAM通过两条互补通道进行蒸馏：(1) 任务条件下的未来视频rollout直接监督学生模型的未来预测分支；(2) 逆动力学模型恢复具身特定的伪动作用于动作学习。此外引入源感知残差动作适配（source-aware residual action adaptation），在共享动作主干上学习源特定的修正，以缓解合成监督与真实监督之间的干扰。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，Vid2WAM在有限专家示范下显著提升了新任务泛化能力和数据效率，同时保持了低延迟推理。推理时视频教师和逆动力学模型均被丢弃，仅保留WAM学生模型用于高效部署。

Conclusion: 未来视觉监督不一定需要来自目标任务的专家轨迹，通过视频基础模型的先验蒸馏可以获得有效的WAM训练信号，在降低数据成本的同时提升模型泛化性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and actions. However, their scalability and generalization remain constrained by their reliance on costly expert demonstrations. We challenge this by asking whether future supervision for WAMs must originate from target-task expert trajectories. In this paper, we propose Vid2WAM, an offline distillation framework that transfers visual diffusion priors from a large video foundation model into a compact WAM student. Given an observation and language instruction, Vid2WAM distills supervision through two complementary channels: task-conditioned future rollouts directly supervise the student's future prediction branch, while an inverse dynamics model recovers embodiment-specific pseudo-actions for action learning. To robustly integrate synthetic and real supervision, we introduce source-aware residual action adaptation that learns source-specific corrections around a shared action backbone and mitigates interference from noisy pseudo-actions. During inference, both the video teacher and inverse dynamics model are discarded, leaving only the WAM student for efficient deployment. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Vid2WAM improves novel-task generalization and data efficiency under limited expert demonstrations while preserving low-latency inference.

</details>


### [78] [Knowledge-Distilled End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Smooth 6-DOF Thrust Control and Rapid Adaptation to Ocean Currents in Remotely Operated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08598)
*Tiankuang Wen,Huiping Li,Gang Liu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRCA-PPO方法，一种基于端到端强化学习的两阶段蒸馏框架，用于远程操作运载器（ROV）在洋流扰动下的最优控制，同时在稳态跟踪误差、瞬态响应、能量效率和推力平滑性方面全面优于传统级联P-PID控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端强化学习方法在洋流扰动下难以实现最优控制，缺乏一个统一的控制框架，能够同时兼顾低稳态跟踪误差、快速瞬态响应、高能效运行以及平滑的控制力输出。

Method: 提出TSRCA-PPO方法，采用两阶段蒸馏学习框架学习近最优策略；核心创新在于奖励函数设计与特权多编码器架构（privileged multi-encoder architecture）。

Result: 消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。仿真结果表明TSRCA-PPO在所有评估指标上均优于传统级联P-PID控制器：稳态位置误差、稳态姿态误差、稳定时间、能量指标和推力平滑度指标分别为P-PID对应值的42.7%、76.5%、10.6%、93.5%和15.9%。

Conclusion: TSRCA-PPO为ROV在洋流扰动下的控制提供了统一的强化学习解决方案，在多项关键性能指标上显著优于传统控制方法，验证了奖励函数设计和特权多编码器架构的有效性。

Abstract: With the continuous improvement of computational capabilities, end-to-end reinforcement learning has been rapidly developed for remotely operated vehicles control. Nevertheless, existing end-to-end reinforcement-learningbased methods still face challenges in achieving optimal control under oceancurrent disturbances. In particular, there remains a lack of a unified control framework that can simultaneously achieve low steady-state tracking error, rapid transient response, energy-efficient operation, and smooth controlforce outputs under disturbances. To address the issue, this paper proposes the thrust smoothness rapid current adaptation proximal policy optimization (TSRCA-PPO) method which learns a near-optimal strategy by a twostage distillation learning framework. The core innovations of this work lie in the reward-function design and the privileged multi-encoder architecture. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each module. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed TSRCA-PPO method consistently outperforms the conventional cascaded P-PID controller across all evaluation metrics. Specifically, TSRCA-PPO reduces the steady-state position error, steady-state attitude error, settling time, energy index, and thrustsmoothness index to 42.7%, 76.5%, 10.6%, 93.5%, and 15.9% of the corresponding P-PID values, respectively.

</details>


### [79] [RAG-Based Auto-Configuration for Industrial Fieldbus Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08618)
*Aadil Gani Ganie,Saad Ezzini,Naveed Farooz Marazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SysName是一个面向工业设备调试的生产级管道，可从异构PDF手册中端到端自动提取协议参数并生成设备配置，支持Modbus RTU、OPC-UA、Profibus DP和CANopen四种协议。它结合混合稠密-稀疏检索索引、基于ECLASS/AAS/SOSA/SSN的本体图谱、BGE-M3编码器和交叉编码器重排器定位相关手册段落，并利用本地LLM（T=0.1）通过协议特定提示和四步修复管道生成本体对齐的JSON-LD配置。两阶段弃权门控（重排器分数阈值+IRI解析率）结合SHACL验证拦截不安全输出。在28个现场级查询的黄金集上，混合检索器HitRate@10达0.96，重排器将MRR@10从0.56提升至0.63；生成器现场级F1=0.87，12次运行中9次完全匹配。H100 GPU上端到端每设备2.6-6.6秒，五设备基准上零不安全写入、零静默失败。案例研究在纯模拟的UR5e机器人上从不经修改的厂商文档（254页手册、8页寄存器列表、496个块）实现F1=1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备调试目前依赖工程师从异构PDF手册中手工提取数百个协议特定参数并转录到监控系统，该流程耗时且易出错。现有方法缺乏端到端的自动化方案，且LLM在工业场景中可能产生不安全输出却无法可靠检测。因此需要一个面向生产环境的、能自动完成设备配置生成并具备安全弃权机制的管道。

Method: SysName管道由以下部分组成：（1）混合稠密-稀疏检索索引，结合ECLASS、AAS、SOSA/SSN构建的本体图谱，使用BGE-M3编码器并通过交叉编码器重排器对相关手册段落进行排序；（2）本地LLM（温度0.1）配合协议特定提示和四步修复管道生成本体对齐的JSON-LD配置；（3）两阶段弃权门控——结合重排器分数阈值和IRI解析率，用于拦截不安全的LLM调用并过滤低覆盖率配置；（4）SHACL验证确保配置符合本体约束；（5）部署验证与读回和关节一致性检查。

Result: 在28个现场级查询黄金集上，混合检索器HitRate@10达0.96；重排器将MRR@10从0.56提升至0.63，且弃权分数分离完美。生成器现场级F1=0.87，12次运行中9次精确匹配。H100 GPU上每设备端到端运行2.6-6.6秒，五设备基准上零不安全写入和零静默失败，所有不成功运行均由弃权或部署验证标记。组件级评估发现唯一的系统性失败在OPC-UA生成，该问题在单独端到端指标中不可见。案例研究在UR5e机器人上达到F1=1.0。

Conclusion: SysName证明了工业设备配置可端到端自动化，且通过本体引导、弃权门控和部署验证等机制能够实现零不安全写入和零静默失败的生产级可靠性。组件级评估揭示了仅依赖端到端指标的盲区（OPC-UA生成失败），强调了细粒度评估的必要性。系统在真实场景（UR5e机器人）上达到完美现场级F1，表明该方法在工业领域具有实用部署价值。

Abstract: Industrial device commissioning requires engineers to manually extract hundreds of protocol-specific parameters from heterogeneous PDF manuals and transcribe them into supervisory control systems, a time-intensive, error-prone workflow. This paper presents SysName, a production-oriented pipeline that automates device configuration end-to-end for Modbus RTU, OPC-UA, Profibus DP, and CANopen. It builds a hybrid dense-sparse retrieval index augmented by an ontology graph derived from ECLASS, AAS, and SOSA/SSN, using a BGE-M3 encoder with a cross-encoder reranker to surface relevant manual passages. A local LLM (T=0.1) generates ontology-aligned JSON-LD configurations via protocol-specific prompts and a four-step repair pipeline. A two-stage abstention gate, combining a reranker-score threshold and an IRI resolution ratio, blocks unsafe LLM invocations and filters low-coverage configurations before SHACL validation. On a gold set of 28 field-level queries, the hybrid retriever reaches 0.96 HitRate@10, and the reranker raises MRR@10 from 0.56 to 0.63 with perfect score separation for abstention. The generator attains field-level F1=0.87 with exact match on 9 of 12 runs. End-to-end runs on an H100 GPU complete in 2.6-6.6s per device with zero unsafe writes and zero silent failures on a five-device benchmark; every unsuccessful run is flagged by abstention or deployment verification. Component-wise evaluation localises the single systematic failure to OPC-UA generation, invisible to end-to-end metrics alone. A case study commissions a physics-simulated Universal Robots UR5e robot from unmodified vendor documentation (254-page manual, 8-page register list, 496 chunks), reaching field-level F1=1.0 over three runs with read-back and joint-consistency verification. An ablation study and comparison with five industrial-LLM systems complete the analysis.

</details>


### [80] [Anchor-Based AI Approach for Pre-Crash Object Detection Utilizing Micro-Doppler Signatures in Automotive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08701)
*Patrick Zaumseil,Rainer Engert,Dagmar Steinhauser,Jonathan Wache,Soumya Dewangan,Thomas Brandmeier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于锚框的AI模型，利用高分辨率车载雷达的微多普勒特征进行碰撞前目标检测与运动参数估计，并通过创新的雷达图像膨胀技术增强局部雷达模式，在真实前端传感器数据上优于传统雷达跟踪方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级自动驾驶依赖安全约束系统的可靠激活，而碰撞前场景中准确、及时的目标检测和运动参数估计对于智能安全气囊和自适应安全带等不可逆安全系统的早期触发至关重要。传统雷达跟踪方法在动态场景和近场条件下存在漏检和参数估计不准确的问题。

Method: 提出一种基于锚框(anchor-based)的AI模型，处理高分辨率雷达数据并利用微多普勒(micro-Doppler)签名来提升碰撞前目标检测和运动参数估计精度。针对雷达点云稀疏和波动的问题，开发了创新的雷达图像膨胀(radar-image dilation)技术作用于特征输入通道，以放大局部雷达模式，增强对多径反射和鬼影目标的鲁棒性。

Result: 在采用系列传感器和真实碰撞前相关场景录制的雷达数据集上进行验证，结果表明该基于锚框的AI模型优于先进的雷达跟踪方法，在动态场景中擅长估计目标参数，并能有效处理不同数据集。

Conclusion: 基于锚框的AI模型结合微多普勒特征与雷达图像膨胀技术，能够显著提升碰撞前场景下的目标检测可靠性和运动参数估计精度，为智能安全约束系统的早期可靠激活提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced automated driving presents significant potential to improve modern automotive safety systems, but it depends highly on the reliable activation of restraint systems. Forward-looking sensors are crucial for immediate and precise object detection. Recent developments in automotive radar technology enable detailed environment detection and the recognition of high-resolution features, such as micro-Doppler signatures. Combined with advanced AI techniques, these features significantly enhance object detection and improve the accuracy of kinematic parameter estimation. This is essential for the early and reliable activation of irreversible safety systems, such as smart airbags and adaptive seat belts. Therefore, an anchor-based AI model is presented, designed to process high-resolution radar data with an explicit focus on micro-Doppler signatures to improve pre-crash object detection. Furthermore, these signatures can improve the accuracy of kinematic object parameter estimation and reduce false negatives, especially in the critical near-field. To address the challenges of sparse and fluctuating radar point clouds, an innovative radar-image dilation technique on the feature input channels was developed to amplify local radar patterns, like micro-Doppler features. Therefore, this approach increases the system's reliability and increases its ability to detect objects in pre-crash scenarios despite radar multipath reflections and ghost objects. In order to investigate the applicability and compare the model's performance with advanced automotive radar tracking methods, a radar data set using series sensors and pre-crash relevant scenarios was recorded. The results demonstrate the advantages of the anchor-based AI model over established tracking approaches. It excels at estimating object parameters in dynamic scenarios and underscores its ability to process different data sets effectively.

</details>


### [81] [WA-SpecDec: World-Aware Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08725)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WA-SpecDec 是一个世界感知的推测解码框架，通过在世界模型预填充阶段注入物理场景感知，在保持宽松接受规则不变的情况下提升 VLA 策略的闭环推断速度与安全性，相较 VLA 推测解码基线实现 1.5 倍匹配成功率加速并平均降低 18.6% 的近接触失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略的自回归生成方式导致闭环延迟高，推测解码虽能并行验证草稿 token 并放宽 token 级接受标准，但这种放宽是场景无关的——固定 token 距离容差在不同状态下将相同的动作偏差视为同等安全，而自由空间中无害的偏差在接近接触时可能引发碰撞或抓取失败。

Method: 提出 WA-SpecDec 框架，在 VLA 预填充阶段注入从世界模型推导的物理场景感知，生成共享的世界感知预填充状态，用于草稿提出和目标验证两个阶段，且不改变原有的宽松接受规则。

Result: 在三种最先进的宽松接受方案上，WA-SpecDec 在更宽松的约束下保持更高的任务成功率并支持更长的接受前缀；在成功率相当的操作点下，相比 VLA 推测解码实现 1.5 倍匹配成功率加速，并相对对应推测基线平均减少 18.6% 的近接触失败（NCF）。

Conclusion: 通过为推测解码注入场景感知能力，WA-SpecDec 在不修改接受规则的前提下显著提升了 VLA 策略在物理接触相关场景中的安全性与推理效率，证明了世界模型感知与推测解码结合的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate robot controls autoregressively, making closed-loop latency dominated by repeated target-model forward passes. Speculative decoding reduces this cost by verifying blocks of draft action tokens in parallel, and recent VLA methods further relax token-level acceptance because small differences in action-token space often map to similar continuous controls. However, this relaxation remains scene-agnostic. A fixed token-distance tolerance treats the same action-token deviation as equally safe across states, although deviations that are harmless in free space can cause collisions or grasp failures near contact. We propose WA-SpecDec, a world-aware speculative decoding framework that injects world-model-derived physical scene awareness during the VLA prefill stage, producing shared world-aware prefill states for draft proposal and target verification without changing the relaxed acceptance rule. Across three state-of-the-art relaxed acceptance schemes, WA-SpecDec preserves higher task success under looser relaxation and enables longer accepted prefixes. At comparable-success operating points, WA-SpecDec achieves a 1.5x matched-success speedup over VLA speculative decoding alone and reduces near-contact failure (NCF) by 18.6% on average relative to the corresponding speculative baselines.

</details>


### [82] [OnEvoMemory: Evolving Memory through Online Robot Rollouts for Pretrained Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08749)
*Zhongxi Chen,Shenqi Zong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OnEvoMemory，一种价值引导的记忆模块，用于预训练机器人策略，通过维护近期上下文、高价值经验和关键状态转换，结合离线演示初始化与在线回滚演化，提升长时程操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要策略追踪已完成子任务和关键交互事件，但现有记忆机制过度依赖外部模型或预定义更新规则，缺乏自主判断哪些经验应被保留的能力。

Method: OnEvoMemory包含三部分记忆：近期上下文、高价值经验、关键状态转换。从轨迹结果中学习经验保留策略；离线演示用于初始化记忆先验，成功与失败的在线回滚则细化记忆选择，帮助策略识别任务阶段转换并避免重复已完成子任务。

Result: 在长时程操作基准实验上，通过离线初始化和在线记忆演化，OnEvoMemory提升了基础VLA策略的性能。

Conclusion: 价值引导的记忆模块能够有效提升预训练机器人策略在长时程操作任务中的表现，且离线初始化与在线演化相结合的记忆学习范式是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires policies to track completed subtasks and critical interaction events. However, existing memory mechanisms heavily rely on external models or predefined update rules. To address this, we propose OnEvoMemory, a value-guided memory module for pretrained robot policies. It maintains recent context, high-value experiences, and salient transitions, while learning which experiences should be retained from trajectory outcomes. Offline demonstrations initialize the memory prior, whereas successful and unsuccessful online rollouts refine memory selection, helping the policy recognize task-stage transitions and avoid repeating completed subtasks. Experiments on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks show that OnEvoMemory improves the performance of the base VLA policy through both offline initialization and online memory evolution.

</details>


### [83] [PEEL: Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly Planning via Scale-Invariant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08773)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Zachary Kingston,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL算法，用于解决长时域多部件物体拆卸问题。PEEL采用尺度不变采样方案估计物体尺度，并集成到多臂老虎机RRT（MAB-RRT）规划器中，通过并行运行批量规划器获得有序的拆卸图。实验表明MAB-RRT在76个装配体上实现100%的单部件拆卸成功率，PEEL成功解决了使用Fetch机械臂的4个涉及10至17个部件的长时域拆卸任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域多部件物体拆卸要求机器人在存在狭窄、紧凑的逃逸通道时，仍能计算无碰撞拆卸运动的可行序列。这类问题在计算上具有挑战性，传统方法难以高效求解。

Method: PEEL使用基于采样的运动规划方法，通过两阶段方案计算单物体运动：先在burn-in阶段估计物体尺度，再使用利用该尺度的方向采样器。该采样方案集成到MAB-RRT（多臂老虎机RRT）规划器中，根据奖励信号在不同采样器之间切换。PEEL并行运行一批规划器，获得指定部件拆卸顺序的有序图。

Result: MAB-RRT在76个装配体上实现100%的单部件拆卸成功率，且对参数具有鲁棒性。PEEL在Fetch机械臂上成功解决了4个长时域拆卸问题，涉及10至17个物体部件。

Conclusion: PEEL结合尺度不变的采样策略与MAB-RRT多臂老虎机框架，能够高效且鲁棒地求解长时域拆卸规划问题，并在真实机械臂平台上验证了其有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Long-horizon multi-part object disassembly requires robots to compute feasible sequences of collision-free removal motions, even in the presence of tight, narrow escape corridors. To efficiently solve such disassembly problems, we propose Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly (PEEL), an algorithm which efficiently computes disassembly motions for object assemblies and feeds them to a robot manipulator for execution. PEEL uses sampling-based motion planning to compute single-object motions through the use of a scale-invariant sampling scheme, where the object scale is estimated in a burn-in phase and a subsequent directional sampler exploits the scale. This sampling scheme is integrated into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner, which switches between different samplers depending on the reward signal received. Using MAB-RRT, the PEEL algorithm runs a batch of planners in parallel to obtain an ordered graph specifying the sequence in which object parts have to be removed. We show that MAB-RRT can efficiently solve single-part disassemblies with 100 percent success rate on 76 assemblies, and that it is robust to its parameters. By integrating MAB-RRT into PEEL, we solve four long-horizon disassembly problems using the Fetch manipulator robot involving 10 to 17 individual object parts.

</details>


### [84] [SG-WAM: Text-Grounded and Spatial-aware Semantic Guidance for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08839)
*Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Zhide Zhong,Haodong Yan,Ruixin Li,Yangyang Zheng,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Yuqiao Du,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SG-WAM，一种通过视觉语言模型提供语义引导（文本接地和空间感知的语义预测）来增强世界-动作模型指令跟随能力的语义引导方法，在仿真和真实环境中展现出精确操作和强指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）主要依赖视觉线索而非语言指令生成未来视频和动作，因为现成的文本编码器将指令与视觉观察独立嵌入，导致预测视频与语言指令语义不对齐，进而降低动作预测精度。

Method: 训练一个基于VLM的规划器来预测文本接地和空间感知的语义预测：文本接地的语义预测通过识别正确目标物体来接地指令，空间感知的语义预测提供场景几何信息用于精确操作；然后将这些语义预测注入世界-动作模型作为高层语义引导，确保未来视频生成和动作预测都忠实遵循语言指令。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验表明，该方法在精确操作和指令跟随能力上具有优越性。

Conclusion: 通过VLM语义规划器提供的高层语义引导，可以有效提升世界-动作模型的指令接地能力，实现更精确的操作和更强的指令跟随性能。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, most existing WAMs generate future videos and actions by relying mainly on visual cues rather than language instructions, since off-the-shelf text encoders embed instructions independently of visual observations. As a result, the videos predicted by these WAMs are often semantically misaligned with their corresponding language instructions, which degrades the accuracy of the predicted actions. To overcome this limitation, we propose SG-WAM, a semantic guidance method for world-action models that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) as a semantic planner to enhance the instruction-grounding capacity of world-action models. Specifically, we train a VLM-based planner to predict text-grounded and spatial-aware semantic foresight. The text-grounded semantic foresight grounds the instruction by identifying the correct target objects, and the spatial-aware semantic foresight provides the scene geometry for precise manipulation. We then inject this foresight into the world-action model as high-level semantic guidance, ensuring that both future-video generation and action prediction faithfully follow the language instruction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the superiority of our semantic guidance method, showcasing precise manipulation and strong instruction-following capabilities.

</details>


### [85] [Hierarchical Topology-Aware Planning and Control of Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Systems in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08871)
*Mohamed Abdelwahab,Ruggero Carli,Damiano Varagnolo,Alberto Dalla Libera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA，一种面向水下机器人-机械臂系统（UVMS）在受限、杂乱且部分已知环境中自主干预的三层分层规划与控制框架，通过全局连通性推理、联合轨迹优化和基于高斯过程的模型强化学习（MC-PILCO）实现安全可靠的自主操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人-机械臂系统在狭窄、杂乱且部分已知的环境中执行自主干预任务时，存在机动性差、通道窄、执行不确定等问题，可能导致机器人进入不可恢复区域，亟需一种结构化的规划与控制框架来保证任务安全和成功率。

Method: 提出MANTA三层层级框架：第一层在保守缩小的基座空间中执行全局连通性推理，提取通往任务区域的可行走廊候选；第二层通过联合优化连续基座运动与机械臂轨迹，细化每个候选走廊，生成无碰撞的基座-机械臂联合轨迹；第三层利用基于高斯过程的模型强化学习（MC-PILCO）学习"到达-保持"基座策略，实现轨迹跟踪与操作状态下的位置保持。执行过程中，框架监控地图更新，在活动通道不可行时触发恢复与路径修复。

Result: 在120次匹配规划查询中，MANTA相比全状态采样基线实现了更高的任务成功率，同时产生更大的安全裕度和更低的机械臂运动量。学习的MC-PILCO策略在训练及未见过的管状参考轨迹上均降低了位置和偏航角跟踪误差。

Conclusion: MANTA作为一种结构化且数据高效的规划与控制框架，适用于洞穴、管道和杂乱海底结构中的安全自主水下干预，在任务成功率、安全裕度和跟踪精度方面均具备显著优势。

Abstract: This paper addresses autonomous intervention with an underwater vehicle--manipulator system (UVMS) in confined, cluttered, and partially known environments, where poor maneuverability, narrow passages, and uncertain execution may cause the robot to enter unrecoverable regions. We propose MANTA, a three-layer hierarchical planning-and-control framework that couples passage accessibility, manipulation feasibility, and closed-loop execution. The first layer performs global connectivity reasoning in a conservative reduced base space to extract traversable corridor candidates toward the task region. The second layer refines each candidate corridor by jointly optimizing the continuous base motion and arm trajectory, producing a collision-free base--arm trajectory. The third layer learns a reach-and-hold base policy using Gaussian-process model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) through MC-PILCO, enabling trajectory tracking and station keeping at the planned manipulation state. During execution, the framework monitors map updates and can trigger recovery and route repair when the active passage becomes infeasible. MANTA is evaluated in confined UVMS planning and closed-loop tracking experiments. Across 120 matched planning queries, it achieves higher task success than full-state sampling-based baselines while producing larger clearance margins and lower arm motion. The learned MC-PILCO policy further reduces position and yaw tracking errors on both training and unseen tube-like references. These results show MANTA as a structured and data-efficient framework for safe autonomous underwater intervention in caves, tubes, and cluttered subsea structures.

</details>


### [86] [SHRIMP: Iterative Refinement of Robot Task Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08884)
*Mya Schroder,Yuna Hwang,Callie Y. Kim,Leqian Cheng,Jeffrey Li-cheng Liu,Chenchen Zheng,Xinning He,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHRIMP是一个通过自然语言自动生成层次化机器人原始任务计划（primitive plan）的系统，用户可通过重新提示或明确修正来迭代修改计划，并先在仿真环境中验证再部署到物理机器人上。通过35名参与者的用户研究，SHRIMP显著提升了用户对系统的感知控制感和机器人透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人已进入制造、农业、医疗等领域，但编程或调整机器人行为通常需要专业性知识，大多数终端用户不具备这些技能。虽然大语言模型使自然语言转任务计划成为可能，但语言指令存在语义歧义，且生成模型缺乏从语言到机器人动作的透明可解释性，导致用户难以在执行前验证计划。

Method: SHRIMP系统利用大语言模型将自然语言指令自动转化为层次化的机器人原始计划（hierarchical robot primitive plan）。系统支持两种修订方式：重新提示（re-prompting）和显式修正（explicit correction）。每次修订后，用户可在仿真环境中验证计划，满意后再部署到物理机器人上执行。

Result: 通过包含35名参与者的用户研究（完成桌面厨房任务规划），验证了SHRIMP相比基线方法能有效提升用户的感知控制感（perceived control）和机器人透明度（robot transparency）。

Conclusion: SHRIMP通过可迭代验证的自然语言规划流程，在无需机器人专业知识的条件下帮助用户生成并修正机器人任务计划，同时提高了系统的可控性与透明性，为面向非专业用户的机器人编程提供了有效范式。

Abstract: As collaborative robots have entered domains such as manufacturing, agriculture, and healthcare, programming or adapting robot behavior typically requires robotic expertise that most end users lack. Natural language lowers this barrier. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have made it feasible to translate natural language into robot task plans. However, language-based task specification suffers from semantic ambiguity, and generative models lack transparency for how language instructions become robot actions, making it difficult for users to validate the plan before execution. To address these issues, we introduce SHRIMP, a system that allows users to automatically generate a hierarchical robot primitive plan using natural language and iteratively revise their plan through re-prompting and explicit correction. At each revision, SHRIMP allows users to validate their plan in simulation, and once satisfied, execute it on the physical robot. Through a user study involving participants planning tabletop kitchen tasks (n=35), we validate that SHRIMP improves perceived control and enhances robot transparency. System videos and source code are available at https://wisc-hci.github.io/SHRIMP.

</details>


### [87] [Estimation of Spacecraft Inertia Tensor Using Attitude-Only Data from Torque-Free Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08886)
*Daigo Kobayashi,Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖姿态数据的航天器归一化惯量张量估计框架，支持连续单弧段与多段短无转矩弧段联合估计，无需陀螺仪或已知控制力矩。通过KKT线性初始化与基于精确Jacobi椭圆解的牛顿打靶法（配以Magnus展开四元数映射）进行非线性精化。实验表明，在受控姿态噪声下，单段500秒弧段的惯量张量误差较同初始化的EKF提升约一个数量级，计算量减少近两个数量级；三段100秒弧段的联合估计精度相近且仍快一个数量级以上。在逼真的近距操作仿真中，2000秒单弧段可实现亚千分之一的中位惯量误差，并支持10小时姿态预测（中位误差个位数度）；三段30-300秒弧段场景中均优于EKF精化，性能由旋转激发程度和时间采样决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空间近距操作等场景中，未知航天器的惯量参数估计至关重要，但传统方法依赖陀螺仪或已知控制力矩，且EKF类方法计算量大、精度受限。作者希望开发一种仅使用姿态观测的轻量级、高精度惯量张量估计框架，能够灵活处理单段连续观测和多段短弧段联合估计场景。

Method: 提出姿态专属（attitude-only）估计框架：(1) 将估计问题建模为Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）约束优化，提供快速线性初始化；(2) 利用Euler方程精确的Jacobi椭圆解进行非线性打靶（shooting）精化；(3) 采用Magnus展开构造四元数映射以传播姿态；(4) 该框架无需陀螺仪测量或已知控制力矩，支持单连续弧段和多短弧段联合估计。

Result: 受控姿态噪声仿真中：单段500秒弧段较同初始化的EKF惯量张量误差降低约一个数量级，计算量减少近两个数量级；三段100秒弧段联合估计精度提升类似且仍快一个数量级。在逼真近距操作仿真中：2000秒单弧段取得亚千分位中位惯量误差，可支持10小时姿态预测（中位误差约个位数度）；三段30-300秒弧段场景中均稳定优于EKF精化。

Conclusion: 该姿态专属框架在无陀螺仪和无控制力矩信息的约束下，能够以远低于EKF的计算成本获得更优的惯量张量估计精度，具备单弧段与多弧段联合估计的灵活性。性能主要受旋转激励程度和时间采样质量影响，在近距操作等实际应用中展现出良好的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present an attitude-only framework for estimating a spacecraft's normalized inertia tensor from torque-free rotational motion. Our method supports both continuous single-arc observations and the joint use of multiple short torque-free arcs, while requiring neither gyroscope measurements nor known control torques. A Karush-Kuhn-Tucker formulation provides a fast linear initialization, which is refined by nonlinear shooting using the exact Jacobi-elliptic solution of Euler's equations and a Magnus-expansion quaternion map. Under controlled attitude noise, tests using a single 500-second arc reduced inertia-tensor error by approximately one order of magnitude relative to an Extended Kalman Filter initialized from the same estimate, while requiring nearly two orders of magnitude less computation. Joint estimation from three 100-second arcs provided a similar improvement in accuracy and remained more than one order of magnitude faster. Photorealistic proximity-operations simulations further evaluated both strategies using monocular image-derived attitudes. The 2000-second single-arc cases achieved sub-thousandth median inertia-tensor error and supported 10-hour attitude predictions with single-digit-degree median error. In three-arc cases using 30-300 seconds per arc, our method consistently outperformed the EKF refinement, with performance governed by rotational excitation and temporal sampling.

</details>


### [88] [From Operational Design Domain to Action: A Systematic Behavioral Taxonomy for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08941)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于标准的21项行为能力分类体系，覆盖高速公路、城市和枢纽三个运营领域，通过将ODD层参数化与行为能力规范交叉映射，生成具体的场景族用于系统化行为测试和SOTIF覆盖证据，弥补了ODD规定运营范围但未规定行为验证的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODD规范仅规定自动驾驶系统（ADS）允许运营的范围，但未规定系统在该范围内必须可证明执行的行为，这一运营条件规范与行为验证之间的鸿沟是ADS安全保证中尚未解决的关键挑战。

Method: 从PEGASUS六层模型ODD中系统推导出21项行为能力的结构化分类体系，覆盖HWY、URB、HUB三个运营领域；每项行为沿纵向和横向控制轴分解，并通过四属性框架（安全、合规、舒适、效率）进行表征；将ODD层参数化与行为能力规范交叉生成场景族，并在规则约束的轨迹优化系统中部署验证。

Result: 该分类体系基于AVSC00008202111、SAE J3237和SAE J3016标准进行grounding，并通过在某规则强制轨迹优化系统中的部署验证其作为运营规范层的有效性；同时识别出Hub领域是结构上独特且规范不足的领域，需要专门的研究关注。

Conclusion: 通过将ODD层参数化与行为能力规范交叉映射可生成适合系统性行为测试和SOTIF覆盖证据的具体场景族，该分类体系可作为运营规范层使用，其中Hub领域被认定为需要专门研究的关键空白领域。

Abstract: Operational Design Domain (ODD) specifications describe where an automated driving system (ADS) is permitted to operate, but they do not prescribe what the ADS must demonstrably do once deployed within that domain. This gap between operating condition specification and behavioral validation represents a critical unresolved challenge in ADS safety assurance. This paper presents a structured, standards-grounded taxonomy of 21 behavioral competencies organized across three operational domains-Highway (HWY), Urban (URB), and Hub (HUB)-derived systematically from the PEGASUS six-layer model-based ODD. Each behavior is decomposed along longitudinal and lateral control axes and characterized against a four-property framework: Safety (gap maintenance, conflict avoidance, kinematic stability), Compliance (legal rules and behavioral norms), Comfort (rider dynamics and trust), and Efficiency (mission completion and product-level metrics). We further demonstrate that the crossing of ODD layer parameterizations with behavioral competency specifications yields concrete scenario families suitable for systematic behavioral testing and SOTIF coverage evidence. The taxonomy is grounded in AVSC00008202111, SAE J3237, and SAE J3016, and is validated as an operational specification layer through its deployment in a rule-enforced trajectory optimization system. The Hub domain is identified as a structurally distinct, underspecified domain warranting dedicated research attention.

</details>


### [89] [Personalized Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance via Preference-based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09015)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏好的贝叶斯优化（PbBO）框架，用于高效地个性化外骨骼助力参数优化，通过20次迭代即可收敛到用户偏好的参数（验证精度90.7%），并在跑步机和户外实验中验证了优化后参数可将代谢率降低14.5%-15.4%、心率降低6.3%-7.6%、肌肉激活降低6.7%-31.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外骨骼控制参数优化方法严重依赖大量人机在线交互，优化速度慢，不仅造成用户疲劳，还影响优化效果。需要探索一种高效的、基于用户偏好的优化框架，以最少的交互学习最优控制参数。

Method: 提出偏好贝叶斯优化（PbBO）方法，通过利用候选集采样分布的先验知识提升样本效率；同时设计层次化控制器，为不同任务生成个性化扭矩并实现实时交互扭矩跟踪。

Result: PbBO在优化六个控制参数时，仅需20次迭代即可达到90.7%的验证精度；跑步机和户外实验表明，与无辅助行走相比，优化参数可降低代谢率14.5%-15.4%、心率6.3%-7.6%、肌肉激活6.7%-31.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于偏好的贝叶斯优化框架能以最小的人机交互实现高效的外骨骼控制参数个性化，显著提升助力舒适性和效率，为外骨骼机器人的自适应控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A significant challenge in exoskeleton robotics is the need to dynamically adapt control profiles to individual motion preferences, thereby ensuring both efficient and comfortable assistance. Currently, since user experience can serve as a comprehensive metric for evaluating the effectiveness of assistance, user preference-based optimization methods have been widely studied for parameter tuning. However, the existing methods rely heavily on extensive human-robot online interactions and suffer from slow optimization speed, which not only induces user fatigue but also compromises optimization effectiveness. Therefore, this paper aims to explore an efficient preference-based optimization framework for personalized exoskeleton assistance that can learn optimal parameters with minimal interaction. We propose a preference-based Bayesian optimization (PbBO) approach that can improve sample efficiency by leveraging knowledge about the sampling distribution of candidate sets. For optimizing six control parameters, PbBO can converge to user-preferred parameters with 90.7% validation accuracy via 20 iterations. Moreover, the hierarchical controller is designed to generate personalized torque for different tasks and achieve interaction torque tracking in real time. The results of treadmill and outdoor experiments demonstrate that the optimized parameters can reduce metabolic rate by 14.5%-15.4%, heart rate by 6.3%-7.6%, and muscle activation by 6.7%-31.5% compared to unassisted walking.

</details>


### [90] [Latent World Models with Monotone Planning Costs for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09073)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Siwei Cai,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于DINO冻结编码器的潜在世界模型，通过自回归展开损失和单调成本排序（MCR）损失训练，解决图像目标导航中规划成本排序不佳的问题，在GNM数据集上超越NWM、DINO-WM等方法，并实现零样本真实机器人部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像目标导航中的潜在世界模型不仅需要准确的未来预测，还需要能可靠地对候选动作序列进行排序的规划成本函数。作者发现余弦距离定义的规划成本可能产生错误的排序，从而误导CEM等基于采样的规划器，因此需要改进成本函数的训练目标。

Method: 提出基于冻结DINO族编码器的潜在世界模型，采用两个互补训练目标：(1) 自回归展开损失，缩小训练与多步规划展开之间的差距；(2) 单调成本排序（MCR）损失，直接鼓励扰动程度递增的动作序列获得更高的规划成本。同时研究了基于InfoNCE的动作对比训练，但发现时间排列负样本会扭曲潜在几何结构并降低规划性能。

Result: 在GNM导航数据集上超越NWM、DINO-WM、OmniVLA和NoMaD，达到最先进的图像目标导航性能，相比同编码器的DINO-WM基线将方向误差降低2.7倍。在物理机器人上零样本部署成功，能在未见过的室内外环境中遵循目标导向路径。

Conclusion: 单调成本排序损失比动作对比训练更适合潜在世界模型的规划成本学习，DINO冻结编码器配合自回归展开与MCR损失能有效提升图像目标导航的规划质量，并具备良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation with latent world models requires not only accurate future prediction, but also a planning cost that reliably ranks candidate action sequences. We define the cost as the cosine distance between the predicted future embedding and the goal embedding, and show that poor cost ordering can mislead sampling-based planners such as Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). To address this, we propose a latent world model built on a frozen DINO-family encoder and train it with two complementary objectives. An autoregressive rollout loss reduces the gap between training and multi-step planning rollouts, while a Monotone Cost Ranking (MCR) loss directly encourages increasingly perturbed action sequences to receive higher planning costs. We also study InfoNCE-based action-contrastive training and find that temporal permutation negatives distort the latent geometry and degrade planning performance. On the GNM navigation dataset, our method outperforms Navigation World Models (NWM), DINO-WM, OmniVLA, and NoMaD, achieving state-of-the-art image-goal navigation performance while reducing orientation error by $2.7\times$ over the same-encoder DINO WM baseline. We also deploy the model zero-shot on a physical robot, where it follows goal-directed paths in unseen indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [91] [UnsDrive: Towards Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving in Unstructured Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09098)
*Nanxin Zeng,Ruiqi Song,Xiangyu Guo,Baiyong Ding,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UnsDrive，一种专为非结构化矿区场景设计的端到端自动驾驶规划器，通过未知感知占据表示和流匹配规划器处理部分可观测性，并配套MineLoop矿区闭环仿真器进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法主要针对结构化城市道路设计，在非结构化矿区环境中泛化能力差。矿区场景存在道路结构弱、地形遮挡、能见度差和大面积未观测区域等特点，使得安全规划极具挑战。

Method: UnsDrive构建一种未知感知的占据表示，利用多帧可见性线索显式建模占据、自由和未知空间，并将流匹配规划器条件于该表示以生成多模态未来轨迹。此外引入占据轨迹一致性损失和不确定性感知轨迹评分器，惩罚进入不可通行或未观测区域的轨迹。同时提出MineLoop矿区闭环仿真器用于评估。

Result: 在开环和闭环设置下的实验表明，UnsDrive在轨迹精度、碰撞避免和长时程驾驶鲁棒性方面一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了在非结构化矿区自动驾驶中显式进行未知空间推理的价值。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has shown strong promise for autonomous driving, but most existing methods are designed for structured urban roads and generalize poorly to unstructured mining environments. In such settings, weak road structure, terrain-induced occlusions, degraded visibility, and large unobserved regions make safe planning particularly challenging. To address these challenges, we propose UnsDrive, an end-to-end planner designed for unstructured mining scenes. UnsDrive builds an unknown-aware occupancy representation that explicitly models occupied, free, and unknown space using multi-frame visibility cues, and conditions a flow-matching planner on this representation to generate multimodal future trajectories. To improve safety under partial observability, we further introduce an occupancy trajectory consistency loss and an uncertainty-aware trajectory scorer that penalize trajectories entering non-traversable or unobserved regions. We also present MineLoop, a mining-oriented closed-loop simulator for evaluating autonomous driving under irregular road geometry, degraded visibility, heavy-vehicle interactions, and mining-specific operational constraints. Experiments in both open-loop and closed-loop settings show that UnsDrive consistently outperforms strong baselines in trajectory accuracy, collision avoidance, and long-horizon driving robustness. These results demonstrate the value of explicit unknown-space reasoning for autonomous driving in unstructured mining environments.

</details>


### [92] [ROEVO: Robust Organized Edge Feature-based Visual Odometry Using RGB-D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09112)
*Mingrui Liu,Xingxing Zuo,Renlang Huang,Minglei Zhao,Jiming Chen,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"组织化边缘"(organized edges)的视觉里程计系统，将离散边缘像素转化为序列化聚类，实现边缘级跨帧关联，并设计了边缘级跟踪、形状保持边缘拟合和基于组织化边缘的Bundle Adjustment方法，在室内环境中达到或超越现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于边缘的视觉里程计方法未能充分利用边缘特征丰富的纹理和结构信息，且像素级处理方式限制了边缘在跨帧关联和位姿估计中的潜力。

Method: 1) 提出"组织化边缘"特征表示，将离散边缘像素转化为序列化聚类以增强纹理和结构信息的保留与利用；2) 利用组织化边缘实现边缘级跨帧关联，建立共视性图；3) 跟踪阶段采用边缘级残差而非像素级残差进行帧间配准；4) 联合优化阶段提出形状保持边缘拟合方法和基于组织化边缘的Bundle Adjustment方法，将传统BA分解为拟合和配准两个子问题以保持结构完整性；5) 构建仅使用组织化边缘特征的完整VO系统。

Result: 大量实验表明该系统在室内环境中具有高精度和高鲁棒性，性能优于或与最先进方法相当。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过将边缘组织化为序列化结构并设计针对性的跟踪与联合优化方法，能够充分挖掘边缘特征的潜力，构建高效且精确的纯边缘视觉里程计系统。

Abstract: This work presents a visual odometry (VO) system that leverages image edge features. Edges are spatially expressive cues commonly present across diverse environments, offering rich textural and structural information. However, existing edge-based VO methods often fail to fully exploit this potential. To this end, we introduce a novel feature representation termed \textit{organized edges}, which transforms disjoint edge pixels into sequentialized clusters, enabling more effective retention and utilization of the underlying textural and structural information. Another nice property of this formulation is that organized edges can perform edge-level association across multiple frames, enabling the establishment of a co-visibility graph. To achieve precise and efficient pose estimation, we propose a range of particularly designed tracking and joint optimization methods based on the characteristics of organized edges. For tracking, we formulate edge-wise rather than pixel-wise residuals to achieve robust and accurate inter-frame registration. For joint optimization, we introduce a novel shape-preserving edge-fitting method and an organized edge-based Bundle Adjustment (BA) approach, which decomposes the traditional BA problem into fitting and registration to preserve the structural integrity. Based on these novel techniques, we develop a complete VO system that exclusively employs organized edge features, achieving efficient tracking and precise local mapping.
