Extensive experiments demonstrate its accuracy and robustness in indoor environments, outperforming or achieving comparable performance to state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/liumingrui814/ROEVO

</details>


### [93] [Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision for Reliable Dual-Arm Surgical Subtask Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09125)
*Mingwu Su,Guankun Wang,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Ziyi Hao,Zhiwei Fang,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Fan Zhang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TDHD（Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision），一种用于手术机器人VLA模型的测试时自适应执行机制。通过测量噪声扰动下两条流匹配生成轨迹的散度估计逐步动作可靠性，并用双阈值规则触发及时重规划，在真实达芬奇式双臂平台上将针操作成功率从55%提升至60%、组织操作从55%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA手术策略依赖固定长度的开环动作序列，场景变化会导致误差累积，在手术操作中带来潜在风险。同时大多数学习方法针对单一任务训练，难以跨手术流程复用，需要更可靠且可组合的自适应执行控制机制。

Method: 将手术VLA部署形式化为自适应执行水平决策问题，提出TDHD机制：在小噪声扰动下测量两条流匹配生成轨迹之间的散度来估计逐动作可靠性，采用双阈值规则截断执行以触发及时重规划。同时建立真实达芬奇式双臂基准，配备同步多视角感知和语言指令，采集600次遥操作演示，覆盖针（reach、pick、regrasp）和组织（reach、lift、resection）两类操作套件。

Result: 在每任务20次试验的真实硬件测试中，TDHD一致优于最新VLA基线：针操作成功率从55%提升至60%，组织操作成功率从55%提升至80%，在最终操作阶段增益最大。

Conclusion: 自适应执行控制对VLA模型在手术机器人操作中的可靠部署至关重要，TDHD通过测试时轨迹散度检测与动态截断策略有效提升了手术操作的成功率与安全性。

Abstract: Surgical robotic systems are increasingly being adopted as clinical workload rises, motivating autonomous solutions for repetitive manipulation subtasks. Learning-based controllers improve generalization compared with rule-based and analytic approaches, but most are trained for individual tasks and remain difficult to reuse across procedures. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework that integrates visual perception, language grounding, and action generation, offering a promising path toward more composable surgical autonomy. However, existing VLA policies rely on fixed-length open-loop action sequences, where changing scene conditions can lead to accumulated errors and potential risks in surgical manipulation. To mitigate this issue, we formulate surgical VLA deployment as an adaptive execution-horizon decision problem and propose Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision (TDHD), a test-time mechanism that estimates step-wise action reliability by measuring the divergence between two flow-matching-generated trajectories under small noise perturbations and truncates execution using a dual-threshold rule to trigger timely replanning. We further establish a real-world da Vinci-like dual-arm benchmark with synchronized multi-view perception and language instructions, and collect 600 teleoperated demonstrations across needle (reach, pick, regrasp) and tissue (reach, lift, resection) manipulation suites. On real hardware with 20 trials per task setting, TDHD consistently improves performance over the latest VLA baselines: success increases from 55\% to 60\% for needle manipulation and from 55\% to 80\% for tissue manipulation, with the largest gains observed in the final manipulation stages. These results highlight the importance of adaptive execution control for reliable deployment of VLA models in surgical robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [94] [High Fidelity Capture, Reconstruction, and Transfer of Human Demonstrations for Robot-Assisted Bathing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09127)
*Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Jonathan P. King,Ethan Zuo,Rohit Satishkumar,Hongyi Chen,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Dan Ding,Zackory Erickson,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以接触区域为核心处理原语的框架，用于从临床医生对人类受试者的洗澡演示中高保真地采集、理解和迁移接触丰富的数据，并成功将数据迁移到机械臂驱动的柔性软手上执行洗澡任务，发布了首个包含同步运动、形状、接触和力的持续人-人接触数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器人在高价值临床任务（如洗澡）中需求巨大，但当代系统仍缺乏与人类进行复杂、持续物理交互所需的安全性和可靠性。核心瓶颈在于采集、理解并有效迁移高度动态且接触丰富的洗澡演示数据十分困难，即便使用现代运动和触觉传感设备也面临挑战。

Method: 提出了一个简洁但有效的框架，将接触区域作为关键处理原语（contact regions as a key processing primitive），用于高保真地采集和处理临床医生的洗澡演示；基于该框架构建了临床医生对人类受试者的洗澡演示数据集；利用该数据集分别通过开环和闭环策略设计并控制一个臂挂式灵巧软手（arm-mounted dexterous soft hand）在人体模型上执行洗澡任务。

Result: 所构建的数据集是首个在持续、接触丰富的人-人交互过程中提供高质量同步运动、形状、接触和力数据的数据集；开环和闭环迁移策略在多个层级的机器人技术栈中展示了数据的高效利用。

Conclusion: 该框架和数据集为物理人机交互（pHRI）研究提供了新的基础资源，所有相关材料将公开发布，以促进该领域的进一步进展。

Abstract: Despite the demand for robots in high-value clinical tasks like bathing, contemporary systems still lack the safety and reliability required for complex, sustained physical interaction with humans. A key challenge hindering the development of such systems is that collecting, understanding, and effectively transferring highly dynamic, contact-rich human bathing demonstrations is difficult, even with modern motion and tactile sensing equipment. We present a straightforward, but effective framework for doing so with high fidelity by utilizing contact regions as a key processing primitive. We use our framework to build a dataset of bathing demonstrations performed by trained clinicians on human subjects. We then use this dataset to design and control an arm-mounted dexterous soft hand to perform bathing tasks on a mannequin using open- and closed-loop strategies. Our dataset is the first to provide high quality synchronized motion, shape, contact, and force during sustained, contact-rich human-human interaction, and our transfer strategies demonstrate effective use of these data across multiple levels of the robotics stack. All relevant materials will be publicly released to enable further advancements in physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) research.

</details>


### [95] [SpeedTuning: Speeding Up Policy Execution with Lightweight Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09138)
*David D. Yuan,Tony Z. Zhao,Kaylee Burns,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpeedTuning框架，通过强化学习学习预测最优执行速度来加速机器人操作策略，无需额外数据采集，实现超过2.4倍的速度提升同时保持足够的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型机器人策略在实际部署中常受限于执行速度过慢的问题。模仿学习策略天然受硬件约束和数据采集时操作者速度的限制，且缺乏加速模仿学习策略的成熟方法，执行速度与任务成功率之间的经验关系也尚未被充分探索。

Method: SpeedTuning是一个基于强化学习的框架，学习预测动作的最优执行速度，从而作为基础策略的补充模块。该框架无需额外数据采集，通过强化学习优化速度预测策略，以平衡执行速度与任务成功率。

Result: SpeedTuning实现了超过2.4倍的执行速度提升，与原始任务策略和线性插值等固定速度加速方法相比，能在保持足够成功率的同时获得显著的速度增益。在倒水、投掷和抓取等多种动态和精确任务中验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpeedTuning为加速机器人操作策略提供了一种无需额外数据采集的有效框架，证明了通过强化学习学习最优执行速度是提高机器人实际部署中操作效率的可行路径，在多样化任务中均表现出良好性能。

Abstract: While learned robotic policies hold promise for advancing generalizable manipulation, their practical deployment is often hindered by suboptimal execution speeds. Imitation learning policies are inherently limited by hardware constraints and the speed of the operator during data collection. In addition, there are no established methods for accelerating policies learned via imitation, and the empirical relationship between execution speed and task success remains underexplored. To address these issues, we introduce SpeedTuning, a reinforcement learning framework specifically designed to enhance the speed of manipulation policies. SpeedTuning learns to predict the optimal execution speed for actions, thereby complementing a base policy without necessitating additional data collection. We provide empirical evidence that SpeedTuning achieves substantial improvements in execution speed, exceeding 2.4x speed-up, while preserving an adequate success rate compared to both the original task policy and straightforward speed-up methods such as linear interpolation at a fixed speed. We evaluate our approach across a diverse set of dynamic and precise tasks, including pouring, throwing, and picking, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness in enhancing real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [96] [Particle-Based Conformal Prediction for Contact-Aware Uncertainty Calibration in Stratified Configuration Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09166)
*Luís Marques,Kristian Popov,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CaPTURe算法，一种基于几何感知的共形预测方法，用于生成具有概率保证的机器人未来构型预测区域。该方法通过校准数据集局部校准运动不确定性估计，能处理接触丰富场景中多模态、非连通或低维流形上的不确定性分布，在两项仿真规划任务中相比基线达到30%的绝对任务成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在与环境交互时，必须处理由随机性和模型失配引起的不确定性，而机器人运动模型常因数据有限、简化假设等原因不准确。此外，机器人接触障碍物时未来构型分布可能变为多模态、非连通或位于低维流形上，现有方法难以在这些复杂情况下提供校准且概率有效的不确定性表示，这对安全高效规划至关重要。

Method: CaPTURe利用校准数据集中的系统转移来局部校准运动不确定性估计，结合共形预测理论构建保证以用户设定概率包含未来机器人构型的预测区域。该方法在任意保真度的基于粒子的模型上工作，通过区分接触丰富与无接触运动之间的不确定性差异来实现充分的覆盖。

Result: 在两项仿真规划任务（迷宫大理石控制和紧公差销孔插入装配）上，CaPTURe在接触和非接触场景均达到用户指定的覆盖要求，相比最佳基线任务成功率提升高达30%的绝对值。

Conclusion: CaPTURe通过几何感知的共形校准方法，有效解决了接触丰富环境中复杂的不确定性表征问题，在保证概率有效预测区域的同时显著提升了任务成功率，为自主系统在接触场景下的安全与高效规划提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty representation is essential for deploying autonomous systems that interact with their environment, as robots must reason about how uncertainty arising from both stochasticity and model mismatch is impacted by contacts with obstacles (e.g., when navigating through a cluttered environment or inserting a part into an assembly). We propose Calibrated Particle-sets for Trans-dimensional Uncertainty Representation (CaPTURe), a geometry-aware, conformal prediction-based algorithm that generates probabilistically valid prediction regions of the unknown future system configuration using particle-based models of arbitrary fidelity. While calibrated uncertainty predictions are essential for safe and efficient planning, analytical or learned motion models are often inaccurate - due to limited data, simplifying assumptions, unmodeled effects, etc. - which can lead to unsafe executions or task failure. Additionally, when a robot contacts an obstacle, the distribution of its future configurations can become multimodal or disjoint, or lie along manifolds of lower intrinsic dimension than the space of possible robot configurations. Our method uses a calibration dataset of system transitions to locally calibrate motion uncertainty estimates, constructing regions guaranteed to contain the future robot configuration at a user-set probability. Our calibration procedure captures how motion uncertainty varies between contact-rich and contactless motions, leading to sufficient coverage in both cases. We evaluate our method on two simulated planning tasks: controlling a marble around a labyrinth and performing tight-tolerance peg-in-hole insertion with a manipulator. Compared to relevant baselines, CaPTURe achieves the user-specified coverage requirement both in and out of contact and achieves up to a 30% absolute improvement in task success rate over the best baseline.

</details>


### [97] [SAIN: Structure-Aware Interactive Navigation with Active Dialogue Grounding for Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09196)
*Yuhao Cao,Xiao Liu,Yang Xie,Lu Liu,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAIN是一个零样本框架，将交互式实例导航中的主动对话转化为持久化的导航状态（而非一次性文本提示），通过结构化的记忆元组（价值、房间、图、对象）存储目标证据、走廊记忆和候选标签，并在VL-LN IIGN基准上将SR从20.2提升至25.4、SPL从13.07提升至14.17，无需任务特定的策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人常遇到模糊、不完整或欠定的人类指令，需要主动提问来解决不确定性。然而现有对话式方法将oracle答案仅作为即时决策的瞬态文本上下文加以消费，缺乏持久的空间或对象导向的结构化状态，导致长期导航任务中信息利用率低、决策不稳定。

Method: SAIN将主动对话转化为四种持久记忆类型：价值记忆（value）、房间记忆（room）、图记忆（graph）和对象记忆（object）。具体地，将oracle回答编译为目标证据（target evidence）、路线级走廊记忆（route-level corridor memory）与对象候选标签（object-candidate labels），由统一策略对这些结构化状态进行消费，用于前沿点（frontier）排序和最终目标接近（final target approach）。整个框架为零样本，无需任务特定的策略训练。

Result: 在VL-LN IIGN基准上，SAIN将SR从20.2提升至25.4（+5.2），SPL从13.07提升至14.17（+1.1），超越了此前最强对话式基线，且无需任务特定的策略训练。

Conclusion: 对话到状态的转换（dialogue-to-state conversion）是一种有效的零样本机制，能够支持长时程的交互式实例导航任务，验证了将主动对话持久化编码为结构化导航状态的价值。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language navigation tasks assume that instructions are complete and unambiguous. However, real-world robots often encounter natural human instructions that are ambiguous, underspecified, or incomplete, requiring them to resolve such uncertainties through active questioning. Interactive Instance Goal Navigation (IIGN) requires an embodied agent to find the specific instance under an ambiguous category-level instruction through active dialogue. However, existing dialogue-enabled methods often consume oracle answers as transient textual context for immediate decisions, rather than persistent spatial or object-centric structured state. We present SAIN, a zero-shot framework that turns active dialogue into persistent navigation state. Instead of consuming oracle answers as one-step text hints, SAIN compiles them into target evidence, route-level corridor memory, and object-candidate labels. These states are stored in structured value, room, graph, and object memories, then consumed by a unified policy for frontier ranking and final target approach. On the VL-LN IIGN benchmark, SAIN improves SR from 20.2 to 25.4 and SPL from 13.07 to 14.17 over the strongest reported dialogue-enabled baseline, while requiring no task-specific policy training. The results support dialogue-to-state conversion as an effective zero-shot mechanism for long-horizon interactive instance navigation. Project website: https://zorattc.github.io/SAIN/

</details>


### [98] [Ultra-Low-Impedance Robotic Gripper for High-Bandwidth and Transparent Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09198)
*Joon Lee,Ari Choi,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种9自由度三指差动直驱（DDD）夹爪，结合直驱电机与低减速比（1:2）差动传动，在保证抓取扭矩的同时降低摩擦、反射惯量和机械阻抗，实现了约18N的抓取力和低物理阻抗，为无传感器力估计和高带宽交互奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人夹爪依赖高减速比传动产生抓取扭矩和外部力传感器测量交互，但高减速比会增加摩擦、反射惯量和机械阻抗，外部传感器又增加硬件复杂度。本文旨在解决扭矩产生与物理透明性之间的权衡矛盾。

Method: 提出一种9自由度三指差动直驱（DDD）夹爪。采用直驱电机与低减速比（1:2）差动传动相结合，将执行器质量集中于基部以最小化运动连杆惯量，并通过差动架构将两个电机并联耦合以放大屈曲时的扭矩。

Result: 样机实现约18N的额定抓取力和4.7N的指尖力，电机对系统惯量的贡献低至0.236%，无源（电机断电）状态下最大被动机械阻抗为50.1 N/m。

Conclusion: 该硬件有效平衡了扭矩、物理透明性和运动灵巧性之间的矛盾，为高带宽交互和无传感器本体感觉力估计提供了基础。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers often use high-ratio transmissions to generate grasping torque and external force sensors to measure physical interaction. High-ratio transmissions increase friction, reflected inertia, and mechanical impedance, while external sensors add hardware complexity. To address these trade-offs, this study proposes a novel 9-DOF, three-fingered Differential Direct-Drive (DDD) gripper that combines DD motors with a low-ratio (1:2) differential transmission. The mechanism centralizes actuator mass at the base to minimize moving-link inertia, while the differential architecture couples two motors in parallel to amplify torque during flexion. Experiments show that the prototype delivers a nominal grasping force of approximately 18 N and a fingertip force of 4.7 N, while maintaining a low motor contribution to system inertia (0.236%) and low passive mechanical impedance, with a maximum measured value of 50.1 N/m when the motors are unpowered. The proposed hardware addresses the trade-offs among torque, physical transparency, and kinematic dexterity, providing a foundation for high-bandwidth interaction and sensorless proprioceptive force estimation.

</details>


### [99] [Task-Oriented Formation Decision via Reinforcement Learning: Herding an Attacking Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09258)
*Zhaozong Wang,Guibin Sun,Jinyong Chen,Rui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人系统的任务导向编队决策问题，聚焦于牧群（herding）任务。提出将编队形状编码为低维参数向量，通过参数优化解决预定义形状灵活性不足的问题，并采用离线训练的强化学习策略在线调控编队参数以应对攻击者的未知策略。仿真和物理实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于多机器人形状编队的实现，而缺乏针对任务的编队决策研究。牧群任务中攻击者具有更高的机动性和未知策略，给防御方带来挑战，需要一种能自适应调整编队形状的方法来弥补机动性劣势。

Method: 1) 将编队形状编码为低维参数向量，将编队决策转化为参数优化问题，增强形状灵活性；2) 设计基于强化学习的策略来调控编队参数，在包含多种攻击策略的仿真环境中离线训练，实现在线部署时应对攻击者不可预测性。

Result: 对比三个基线方法的仿真实验表明，所提方法能成功完成具有挑战性的牧群任务；可扩展性仿真验证了其在包含数十个机器人的场景中的适用性；在包含3个攻击者和7个防御者的物理机器人平台上验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 通过参数化编队表示结合强化学习调控，可以有效解决多机器人牧群任务中机动性劣势和攻击者策略未知的挑战，兼具仿真与物理实验的可行性验证。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can accomplish tasks that are difficult for a single robot by organizing into task-specific formations. Different from existing studies on multi-robot shape formation, we here study the task-oriented formation decision problem, with a focus on the herding task. This task is challenging due to the attackers' superior maneuverability and their unknown strategies. To address these challenges, we propose the following novel results. First, we encode the formation shape using a low-dimensional parameter vector. This parametric representation reformulates the formation decision as a parameter optimization problem, thereby resolving the limited flexibility of predefined shapes. By optimizing these formation parameters, the defenders' maneuverability disadvantage is mitigated through a formation shape that continuously adapts to task requirements. Second, we develop a reinforcement learning-based policy to regulate the formation parameters. Trained offline in simulations covering diverse attacking strategies, the learned policy can effectively handle adversarial unpredictability during online deployment. Comparative simulations against three baselines demonstrate that our method can successfully accomplish challenging herding tasks. Additional scalability simulations further verify its applicability to simulated scenarios involving dozens of robots. We also validate the practical feasibility of our method on a physical robotic platform with 3 attackers and 7 defenders.

</details>


### [100] [WorldSimProbe: Diagnosing Simulator Faithfulness in Action-Conditioned World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09298)
*Peterson Co,Sicheng Hu,Chunxuan Jiao,Hongyang Cheng,Yulin Luo,Yijie Xu,Sixiang Chen,Zhongxia Zhao,Zihao Wang,DaFeng Chi,Peidong Liu,YuTong Chen,Henghua Liu,Zhihao Yuan,Huizhu Jia,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Liang Lin,Huajie Tan,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能力的ACWM（动作条件世界模型）模拟器保真度评估框架，通过可观测模拟器契约（Observable Simulator Contract）和WorldSimProbe基准，系统诊断六种开源ACWM在局部控制敏感性、全局轨迹变化、交互接地和动力学等维度的模拟器保真度，揭示了动作实现退化、交互接地失效等系统性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ACWM评估主要关注视觉质量、任务结果或粗粒度的rollout响应性，并未直接测试模拟器的保真度（即动作条件转换的精确性）。Realizing ACWM在规划、策略评估和数据生成中的潜力，需要精确的动作条件转换而非仅仅是合理的输出，因此需要一种直接评估模拟器保真度的标准化框架。

Method: 首先形式化定义Observable Simulator Contract（可观测模拟器契约），要求供给的动作必须诱导相应的智能体运动，且环境响应必须基于这一实现的运动。在此基础上，引入WorldSimProbe基准，包含五个受控套件：局部控制敏感性、全局轨迹变化、源多样化动作、交互接地和动力学。每个套件配备专门的评估器，评估模拟器相对校准、稠密动作到运动的对应关系、虚假交互接地的检验以及原始级动力学。

Result: 在RoboTwin、ManiSkill和LIBERO三个基准上，对六种开源ACWM进行超过18,000个实例的评估。WorldSimProbe揭示了跨控制变化中系统性的动作实现退化、交互接地和动力学中的结构化失败，并且评估信号与人类判断和下游结果一致。

Conclusion: 该能力导向的框架为诊断ACWM模拟器保真度提供了一种透明、标准化的范式，超越了粗粒度、以任务为导向的评估方式。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) promise to provide embodied AI with scalable predictive simulators for planning, policy evaluation, and data generation. Realizing this promise requires precise action-conditioned transitions rather than merely plausible outputs. Yet their applicability remains difficult to establish because prevailing evaluations emphasize visual quality, task outcomes, or coarse rollout-level responsiveness without directly testing simulator fidelity. To address this gap, we evaluate ACWMs through the observable capabilities expected of physical simulators. Accordingly, we formalize Observable Simulator Contract, a minimal contract that any action-conditioned physical simulator should satisfy: supplied actions must induce corresponding agent motion, and environment responses must be grounded in that realized motion. To operationalize this contract, we introduce WorldSimProbe, comprising five controlled suites spanning local control sensitivity, global trajectory variation, source-diverse actions, interaction grounding, and dynamics. Suite-specific evaluators assess simulator-relative calibration, dense action-to-motion correspondence, false-interaction grounding, and primitive-level dynamics. We evaluate six open-source ACWMs on more than 18,000 instances across RoboTwin, ManiSkill, and LIBERO. World-SimProbe reveals systematic action-realization degradation across control variation, structured failures in interaction grounding and dynamics, and benchmark signals consistent with human judgments and downstream outcomes. Together, this capability-based framework provides a transparent, and standardized paradigm for diagnosing ACWM simulator fidelity beyond coarse, task-directed evaluation.

</details>


### [101] [SAFE-CHEM: Uncertainty-Aware Policy Switching for Robust Robotic Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09303)
*Laura Jones,Shazil Shahzad,Ayesha Sana,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFE-CHEM，一种面向机器人化学家的不确定性感知框架，通过集成循环神经网络模仿学习策略量化在线认知不确定性，并采用混合控制架构在不确定性超过安全阈值时自动切换至规则备份控制器，从而提升任务成功率并减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人系统在化学实验室中的部署虽加速了实验流程，但基于学习的策略在化学等高危领域中难以区分安全与不安全行为，常出现过度自信外推导致灾难性失败，安全性成为其部署的主要障碍。

Method: 该框架使用集成RNN模仿学习策略，通过动作预测方差在线量化认知不确定性；利用核密度估计刻画成功条件下的方差密度分布，并在不确定性超过校准安全阈值时，从学习策略自动切换至确定性规则备份控制器，构成混合控制架构。

Result: 在三个基础实验室操作任务上的实验表明，与传统单策略基线相比，该混合策略提升了整体任务成功率并减少了关键安全违规；通过零样本仿真到实物的迁移在Franka Production 3机械臂上验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: SAFE-CHEM通过不确定性感知的混合控制策略，在保持学习策略灵活性的同时引入安全备份机制，为学习型机器人化学家的安全部署提供了有效且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous robotic systems in chemistry laboratories is accelerating experimental workflows and providing the foundational data for AI-driven scientific discovery. However, despite the success of data-driven methods in acquiring dexterous skills, safety remains a primary barrier to their deployment in high-risk domains, such as early-stage materials chemistry experiments. Specifically, learning-based policies frequently struggle to distinguish between safe and unsafe actions, leading to overconfident extrapolation and potentially catastrophic failures. To mitigate these safety risks, we propose SAFE-CHEM, an uncertainty-aware framework designed for robust, learning-based robotic chemists. Our approach leverages an ensemble of recurrent neural network-based imitation learning policies to quantify epistemic uncertainty online through the variance of action predictions. By characterising the success-conditioned density of this variance using kernel density estimation, we introduce a hybrid control architecture that autonomously switches from the learned policy to a deterministic, rule-based backup controller when uncertainty exceeds a calibrated safety threshold. We evaluate SAFE-CHEM across three fundamental laboratory manipulation tasks, where our empirical results demonstrate that this hybrid strategy improves overall task success rates and reduces critical safety violations compared to traditional single-policy baselines. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of the framework through zero-shot sim-to-real transfer onto a physical Franka Production 3 robot manipulator.

</details>


### [102] [DH-VLM: Dual-Horizon Cooperative Latent Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09333)
*Ziyi Song,Chen Xia,Hang Yu,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DH-VLM，一种双时域协同潜变量推理框架，通过基础设施聚合多层隐状态生成全局推理引导，经基础设施驱动的潜变量演化机制融入自车模型，在保持本地规划自主性的同时利用远程上下文理解，实现协同自动驾驶中的高效语义推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在自动驾驶中能提供全局理解和长时域规划，但部署在孤立车辆上受感知范围和遮挡限制，且计算和延迟开销大难以车载部署。现有协同驾驶方法在实际约束下的语义推理能力有限。

Method: DH-VLM采用双时域协同潜变量推理框架：基础设施聚合多层隐藏状态形成全局推理时域潜变量引导，通过基础设施驱动的潜变量演化（Infrastructure-Driven Latent Evolution）机制将引导融入自车模型，实现条件化潜变量精炼。同时构建面向协作的问答数据集，涵盖基础场景理解和自车个性化理解，支持反事实与安全感知推理。

Result: 在规划性能上达到SOTA，L2误差降低14.6%，碰撞率降低26.9%。相比基于查询的端到端协同驾驶方法，通信成本降低57.3%，GPU内存使用降低25.5%，且对基础设施引导误差具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DH-VLM提供了一种实用且鲁棒的协同自动驾驶范式，在实现全局语义推理和高效规划的同时，显著降低通信和计算开销，为协同驾驶的大规模语言模型落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale language models for autonomous driving enable enhanced global understanding and long-horizon planning. However, when deployed in isolated vehicles, limited sensing range and occlusions restrict reliable decision-making, and the substantial computational and latency overhead makes on-board deployment impractical. Cooperative driving provides a potential solution by leveraging external agents for information exchange, but existing methods remain limited in semantic reasoning capability under practical constraints. To address these challenges, we propose DH-VLM, a dual-horizon cooperative latent reasoning framework that enables asymmetric semantic cooperation between the infrastructure and ego vehicle. The infrastructure aggregates multi-layer hidden states to form a global-reasoning horizon latent guidance, which is integrated into the ego model through an Infrastructure-Driven Latent Evolution mechanism for conditional latent refinement. This enables the ego vehicle to leverage long-range contextual understanding while preserving autonomous decision-making within its local planning horizon. Furthermore, we construct a cooperation-oriented question-answer (QA) dataset covering fundamental scene understanding and ego-personalized comprehension to support counterfactual and safety-aware reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DH-VLM achieves state-of-the-art planning performance, outperforming the previous state of the art by 14.6% in L2 error and 26.9% in collision rate. Compared with query-based end-to-end cooperative driving methods, our approach reduces the communication cost by 57.3% and GPU memory usage by 25.5%, while maintaining strong robustness against infrastructure guidance errors, providing a practical and robust paradigm for cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [103] [JEPA-WAM: Learning Vision-Language-Action Policies with Joint-Embedding World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09381)
*Yihan Lin,Jiawei He,Shifeng Bao,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Xiaobo Wang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-WAM是一种基于预训练V-JEPA空间的潜空间世界动作模型，通过共享预测器耦合潜空间状态转移预测与连续动作生成，在LIBERO-Plus上达到79.2%（无大规模策略预训练最佳），其π0.5实例版本达到86.3%的整体最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成世界动作模型（WAMs）部署成本高，而现有潜空间WAMs要么过度压缩预测表示，要么将预测建模与动作生成所用表示分离，导致预测能力难以直接服务于动作决策。

Method: 在预训练V-JEPA空间中构建JEPA-WAM，设计空间结构的联合当前-未来目标以捕获任务共享的视觉时间结构并保持密集patch级对应；通过共享预测器使转移监督直接塑造骨干网络，从中提取专用表示用于动作预测；该设计还可实例化为预训练VLA策略而不改变其感知和动作通路。

Result: LIBERO-Plus上JEPA-WAM达79.2%，为无大规模机器人策略预训练的最佳结果；预训练π0.5实例达到86.3%的整体最优。RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界双臂操作实验进一步验证了在视觉与空间偏移下的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在预训练语义空间中耦合潜状态转移预测与动作生成，JEPA-WAM有效降低了部署成本，同时提升了动作预测性能和跨分布泛化能力，且能无缝集成到现有VLA策略中。

Abstract: Robust robot control benefits from explicitly modeling state transitions, but video-generation world action models (WAMs) introduce substantial deployment cost. Existing latent WAMs avoid explicit future generation, but often compress predictive representations or separate predictive modeling from the representations used for action generation. We introduce JEPA-WAM, a latent WAM built in a pretrained V-JEPA space, which couples latent transition prediction with continuous action generation through a shared predictor. JEPA-WAM predicts a spatially structured joint current-future target that captures task-shared visual temporal structure between current and future observations, while preserving dense patch-level correspondence. Through the shared predictor, transition supervision directly shapes the backbone, from which dedicated representations are extracted for action prediction. The same design can also be instantiated in pretrained VLA policies while preserving their original perception and action pathways. On LIBERO-Plus, JEPA-WAM achieves 79.2%, the best result without large-scale robot-policy pretraining, while its pretrained $π_{0.5}$ instantiation reaches 86.3%, achieving the best overall performance. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world bimanual manipulation further demonstrate strong generalization under visual and spatial shifts.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond the Plane: Coupling Planar Vehicle Dynamics with Three-Dimensional Road Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09402)
*Simon Sagmeister,Phillip Pitschi,Nico Haja,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与真实三维道路几何耦合的新方法，通过将平面车辆状态转换到三维空间并计算道路几何引起的力与力矩，在不放弃简单平面模型的前提下缩小平面仿真与真实三维道路之间的差距，并在拉斯维加斯赛车场高速数据和合成赛道上验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统的开发与测试依赖精确的车辆动力学仿真，但大多数车辆动力学模型是二维的，而真实道路是三维的。三维道路几何（如斜坡弯道）对轮胎法向力的影响显著（在拉斯维加斯赛车场上可增加超过66%），导致高精度的平面模型也难以准确复现真实车辆行为。虽然存在三维动力学解决方案，但计算昂贵、复杂且很少被采用。

Method: 提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与真实三维道路几何耦合的新方法：将平面车辆模型中的车辆状态从二维平面转换到三维空间中对应的表示，同时计算由道路几何引起的力和力矩并将其施加到平面车辆模型上。

Result: 使用全尺寸赛车在拉斯维加斯赛车场（带坡度的赛道）录制的告诉数据验证了方法的有效性，并在合成赛道上证明了该方法即使在边界情况下也能产生准确结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，该方法能够在无需放弃简单平面模型的前提下，弥合平面仿真与真实三维道路之间的差距。方法以开源软件形式提供，便于推广应用。

Abstract: Simulation is crucial for developing and testing autonomous driving systems. In particular, the development of localization and control algorithms relies on an accurate vehicle dynamics simulation. However, most vehicle dynamics models are two-dimensional while real-world roads are three-dimensional. For example, effects from the three-dimensional road geometry on the Las Vegas Motor Speedway can increase the normal forces on the tires by more than 66% compared to the nominal load at standstill. As a result, even highly detailed planar vehicle dynamics models struggle to accurately reproduce the real vehicle's behavior. While solutions for three-dimensional vehicle dynamics exist, they are rarely adopted, computationally expensive, and complex. To address this issue, we present a novel method to couple planar vehicle dynamics models with real-world three-dimensional road geometry. We transform the planar vehicle state from the vehicle model's two-dimensional plane to its corresponding representation in three-dimensional space. Additionally, we calculate road-geometry-induced forces and moments and apply them to the planar vehicle model. We validate our approach using high-speed data recorded with a full-scale race car on the banked Las Vegas Motor Speedway. Furthermore, on synthetic tracks, we show that our method yields accurate results even in edge cases. Together, our results demonstrate that the gap between planar simulation and real-world three-dimensional roads can be closed without abandoning simpler planar models. To simplify adoption of our method, we provide the implementation as open-source software on github.com/TUMFTM/3d-road-geometry-coupling.

</details>


### [105] [Skills in Weights, Memory in Code: Hybrid Learning for Memory-Dependent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09410)
*Yunhao Zhao,Zhenyang Ni,Haoyang Chen,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyMeS是一个混合学习框架，利用编码智能体的推理与记忆管理能力来引导马尔可夫视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，解决长时程、非马尔可夫的真实世界操纵任务，在RoboMemArena上显著超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略通常从当前观测或短固定长度历史生成动作，但真实世界操纵往往是非马尔可夫的，需要机器人跨长时间交互历史保留并推理任务相关信息。这形成了"马尔可夫假设"与实际任务需求之间的关键鸿沟，需要一种方法让标准VLA具备长时记忆管理能力。

Method: HyMeS采用混合学习架构：(1) 底层通过梯度式模仿学习获取可复用的低级运动技能；(2) 上层由编码智能体通过启发式学习（从回滚反馈迭代更新可执行启发式系统）获取高级记忆管理策略；(3) 通过多模态阶段完成验证（使用本体感觉信号和多帧VLM判断）闭环连接引导与执行，更新记忆状态。

Result: 在RoboMemArena基准上，HyMeS将平均累积成功率从52.5%提升至66.2%，平均任务成功率从41.3%提升至60.1%（相对pi0.5基线）；相较PrediMem，累积成功率提高4.5个百分点，任务成功率提高14.5个百分点。

Conclusion: HyMeS证明了一种可行且数据高效的范式：无需为每种历史相关任务配置收集端到端演示，仅需可复用运动技能的演示数据即可实现组合泛化；混合学习框架成功弥合了马尔可夫VLA策略与非马尔可夫实际任务之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern vision-language-action (VLA) policies have acquired broad manipulation skills, but typically generate each action chunk from the current observation or a short fixed-length history. However, real-world manipulation is often non-Markovian, requiring robots to retain and reason over task-relevant information from long-horizon interaction histories to determine the next action. To address this challenge, we propose HyMeS, a hybrid learning framework that leverages the reasoning and memory-management capabilities of coding agents to steer a Markovian VLA for memory-dependent manipulation. Specifically, HyMeS learns low-level motor skills through gradient-based imitation learning, while a coding agent acquires high-level memory-management strategies through heuristic learning by iteratively updating an executable heuristic system from rollout feedback. Furthermore, we close the loop between steering and execution through multimodal stage-completion verification, which updates memory using proprioceptive signals and multi-frame VLM judgments. Compared with end-to-end memory-augmented VLAs, HyMeS requires demonstrations only for reusable motor skills rather than for every history-dependent task configuration, enabling data-efficient compositional generalization. On RoboMemArena, HyMeS improves mean cumulative success from 52.5% to 66.2% and mean task success from 41.3% to 60.1% over pi0.5, while outperforming PrediMem by 4.5 points in cumulative success and 14.5 points in task success.

</details>


### [106] [VANE: Reliable Test-Time Training for Vision-Language-Action Models via Future Visual Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09448)
*Hongjin Ji,Guoyang Xia,Luoyang Sun,Fangxiang Feng,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VANE，一种可靠的测试时训练（TTT）框架，用于在闭环操作中安全地适配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。VANE通过基于未来视觉后果的评估来选择性、可逆地提交策略更新，在Sim​​plerEnv WidowX上比TTT基线提升3.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT为VLA策略提供了一种从无标注部署流中轻量适配的途径，但现有的TTT方法在闭环操作中难以可靠使用：共享的适配空间可能混合不兼容的任务修正，在线更新则可能在后果未知前就改变后续动作。

Method: VANE将提示适配条件化于当前视觉-语言上下文，从已执行动作的未来视觉后果中学习。候选更新与实时策略隔离，在后续观测上进行评估，仅在有未来证据支持时才提交更新，从而实现选择性和可逆的适配。

Result: 在Sim​​plerEnv WidowX上，VANE相较对应TTT基线平均成功率提升3.2个百分点；Google Robot上的结果进一步表明部署时的收益依赖于任务与具体实体（embodiment）。

Conclusion: VANE展示了一种受约束、基于证据的方法来在交互过程中适配VLA策略，提高了TTT在闭环操作中的可靠性。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) offers a lightweight way to adapt vision--language--action (VLA) policies from unlabeled deployment streams, but it remains difficult to use reliably in closed-loop manipulation. A shared adaptation space can mix incompatible task corrections, while an online update can alter subsequent actions before its consequences are known. We introduce a reliable TTT framework for VLA policies (VANE). VANE conditions prompt adaptation on the current vision--language context and learns from the future visual consequences of executed actions. Candidate updates are isolated from the live policy, evaluated on subsequent observations, and committed only when supported by future evidence, making adaptation selective and reversible. On SimplerEnv WidowX, VANE improves average success by $3.2$ percentage points over the corresponding TTT baseline. Results on Google Robot further show that deployment-time gains remain task- and embodiment-dependent. Together, these results demonstrate a constrained, evidence-based approach to adapting VLA policies during interaction.

</details>


### [107] [Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser: Topological Graph Guidance for Safe Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09484)
*Nakgyu Yang,KwangBin Lee,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G2SD（Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser），一个层级框架，通过高层拓扑图规划器引导低层扩散模型，在结构层面强制安全性，避免推理时引导导致的流形破裂问题。实验表明在Maze2D导航中目标到达率从40-50%提升至98%，且在运动控制任务中也取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的规划器通常通过推理时的引导来保证安全性，但这种交错式的轨迹变形常常因流形破裂（manifold rupture）而导致运动学可行性退化。作者希望从结构层面解决安全性与可行性之间的矛盾，而非依赖推理时的后验修正。

Method: G2SD采用层级架构：高层拓扑图规划器将数据流形抽象为学习的潜在图（latent graph）并在此图上进行规划；低层扩散规划器生成连续轨迹，并以上层规划器选出的图节点表示作为条件。同时提供了流形破裂发生条件的理论分析。

Result: 理论分析表明G2SD通过增加分段数量可降低约束违反概率。实验上，在Maze2D导航任务中将无碰撞的目标到达率从40-50%提升到98%，在运动控制（locomotion）任务中也取得更优的任务分数。

Conclusion: G2SD通过将安全约束抽象到结构层面（拓扑图规划），有效避免了推理时引导引发的流形破裂，同时从理论上证明其安全性随分段数增加而提升，实验验证了其在实际任务中的显著优势。

Abstract: Many diffusion-based planners enforce safety through inference-time guidance, but such interleaved trajectory deformations often degrade kinematic feasibility due to manifold rupture. We propose Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser (G2SD), a hierarchical framework that leverages a high-level topological graph planner to guide a low-level diffusion model. G2SD enforces safety at a structural level by abstracting the data manifold into a learned latent graph, on which high-level planning is performed. Continuous trajectories are generated by diffusion planners, which are conditioned on the graph node representations selected by the high-level planner. Theoretical analyses demonstrate conditions under which manifold rupture occurs in diffusion planners, and show that G2SD improves safety by reducing the constraint violation probability as the number of segments increases. Experiments demonstrate that G2SD substantially outperforms baselines, increasing goal-reaching rate without any collision from 40-50% to 98% in Maze2D navigation and also achieving superior task scores in locomotion.

</details>


### [108] [Rethink Before You Execute: Adaptive Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09492)
*Feng Ye,Yiming Zhao,Yong Yu,Hongxu Zhou,Yong Pan,Yuan Xue,Peng Jia,Chuanmin Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TempoWAM，一种轻量级的即插即用执行方案，用于世界动作模型（WAMs）。通过循环进度监控器估计任务进度，并由自适应执行协议基于进度评估决定是否重新规划，从而替代固定执行视野，提升执行效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的WAM在每次推理时生成动作块，并执行固定前缀后才重新规划。然而固定的执行时间窗口与实际执行动态不匹配：不同任务阶段动作块的可靠性不同，何时重新规划应取决于累积执行结果，而非固定的步数计数。

Method: TempoWAM包含两个核心组件：(1) 循环进度监控器（Recurrent Progress Monitor），从当前观测、任务指令、剩余动作和执行历史中估计任务进度；(2) 自适应执行协议（Adaptive Execution Protocol），评估当前动作块是否在推进任务，以决定是否需要重新规划。为弥合训练与部署的差距，该协议通过任务依赖的校准因子并配合在线自适应进行校准。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上的实验表明，TempoWAM持续改善了WAM执行的效率-成功权衡。在真实机器人上，简单任务中WAM推理次数减少26.9%且保持成功率，困难任务中成功率提升13.3个百分点。

Conclusion: TempoWAM通过基于进度监控的自适应重新规划时机制，替代固定执行视野，有效解决了WAM执行中动作块可靠性与执行步数不匹配的问题，在多种任务上同时提升了执行效率与成功率，验证了其作为一种通用即插即用执行方案的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future actions and the evolution of the environment. At each inference, a WAM generates a chunk of actions and the robot executes a fixed prefix before replanning. We argue that this fixed execution horizon is poorly matched to execution dynamics: the chunk reliability varies across task stages, so when to replan depends on the result of accumulated execution, not on the step counts. We propose TempoWAM (Timing Execution by Monitoring Progress Online), a lightweight plug-and-play execution scheme for WAMs. A Recurrent Progress Monitor first estimates task progress from the current observation, task instruction, remaining actions, and execution history; and an Adaptive Execution Protocol then evaluates whether the chunk is advancing the task to decide if replanning is needed. To bridge the training-deployment gap, the protocol is calibrated by a task-dependent calibration factor with online adaptation. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks show that TempoWAM consistently improves the efficiency-success trade-off of WAM execution. On real robots, it reduces WAM inferences by 26.9% on easy tasks while maintaining success, and improves success by 13.3 points on difficult tasks.

</details>


### [109] [Tether-Inertial Localization for Planetary Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09515)
*Dielof van Loon,Anton Bredenbeck,Lennart Puck,Martin Azkarate,Salua Hamaza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于系绳的惯性定位方法（Tether-Inertial Localization），利用系绳长度和角度测量值估计无人机相对基站的位姿，结合解析悬链线模型与高斯过程残差补偿，在圆形、三角形和8字形轨迹（绳长≤4.5m，总飞行37分钟）上实现5.2cm的平均RMSE，比现有技术优一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星探测中无人机（如Ingenuity直升机）虽能提供宝贵测绘数据，但有限的有效载荷能力约束了飞行时间和定位计算资源，限制了其应用范围。系绳连接可将电池和计算负担转移到基站漫游车，同时系绳本身可作为无漂移定位的感知源，因此需要一种利用系绳测量实现高精度定位的方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的系绳-惯性定位方法：1）利用系绳长度和角度测量估计无人机相对基站的位置；2）结合计算高效的解析悬链线模型进行位置估计；3）引入高斯过程（GP）残差误差补偿，以校正系统传感器误差和模型局限。

Result: 在圆形、三角形和8字形轨迹上进行实验验证，系绳长度最大4.5m，总飞行时间37分钟。仅基于系绳位置估计反馈时，解析悬链线模型达到7.4cm平均RMSE，经GP残差补偿后进一步降至5.2cm，比现有最优方法优一个数量级。

Conclusion: Tether-Inertial Localization被确立为系绳无人机（TUAV）在视觉和GNSS定位之外的一种实用替代方案，展示了系绳定位在行星探测等受限场景中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent developments in planetary exploration have shown the potential of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), such as the Ingenuity helicopter that provided valuable mapping data. However, limited payload capabilities constrain the flight times and compute available for localization, which restrict their applicability. By providing a tethered connection, issues such as battery and computational constraints are offloaded to the base rover. At the same time, the cable can be exploited for non-drifting localization. This work presents a novel Tether-Inertial Localization approach that uses tether length and angle measurements to estimate the UAV position relative to its base. The method combines a computationally efficient analytical catenary model with a Gaussian Process (GP) residual error compensation. This accounts for systematic sensor inaccuracies and model limitations. Experimental validation across circular, triangular, and figure-eight trajectories with tether lengths up to 4.5 m and a total flight time of 37 minutes demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Using only tether-based position estimates for feedback, the analytical catenary model achieves an average RMSE of 7.4 cm, which is further reduced to 5.2 cm through GP-based residual compensation, one order of magnitude better than the state-of-the-art. These results establish Tether-Inertial Localization as a practical alternative to vision- and GNSS-based localization for Tethered Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (TUAVs).

</details>


### [110] [HarnessWAM: Bridging Prediction and Deliberation in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09516)
*Zhaopeng Gu,Bingke Zhu,Tianxi Lin,Guibo Zhu,Yingying Chen,Kai Wang,Tingyu Yuan,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhaowen Li,Peng Su,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessWAM框架，通过引入VLM驱动的任务管理器、能力条件化的可执行空间投影，以及事件驱动双时间尺度反馈循环，弥合了世界动作模型（WAMs）在有限步预测与复杂具身任务全局规划之间的"预测-深思"鸿沟，在RoboMemArena和RoboCerebra基准上取得SOTA成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models（WAMs）联合学习环境动力学与机器人动作，但有限时域的动作预测不足以支撑复杂具身任务所需的全局规划、跨阶段状态维护、执行验证与失败恢复，这一不匹配被称为"预测-深思鸿沟"（prediction-deliberation gap）。

Method: HarnessWAM由三个核心组件构成：（1）基于VLM的任务管理器，维护证据驱动的场景信念与结构化任务图；（2）能力条件化的可执行空间投影，将开放语义计划约束为满足任务依赖、本体状态约束和WAM能力边界的原子技能序列；（3）事件驱动的双时间尺度反馈循环，轻量级进度估计器提供高频执行证据，任务管理器在关键里程碑综合当前观察、任务状态与交互历史进行深思决策（推进、采集信息、修订计划或局部恢复）。

Result: 在RoboMemArena上实现59.6%的全任务成功率和69.9%的子任务成功率，在RoboCerebra Ideal上实现23.7%的成功率，均达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 模型外部的结构化状态维护与闭环自主决策机制能有效将WAMs的局部控制能力扩展为可规划、可验证、可恢复的完整具身任务执行能力，子任务失败后无需丢弃已获取的场景知识即可恢复执行。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly learn environmental dynamics and robot actions, introducing priors over physical evolution into embodied control. However, finite-horizon prediction and action generation are insufficient for complex embodied tasks that require global planning, cross-stage state maintenance, execution verification, and failure recovery. We refer to this mismatch as the prediction-deliberation gap of WAMs. To address this gap, we propose HarnessWAM, an agentic framework for WAMs. HarnessWAM employs a vision-language-model-based Task Manager to maintain an evidence-grounded scene belief and a structured task graph. A capability-conditioned executable-space projection further constrains open-ended semantic plans into sequences of atomic skills that satisfy task dependencies, embodiment-state constraints, and the capability boundary of the underlying WAM. During execution, HarnessWAM operates through an event-driven, dual-timescale feedback loop: a lightweight progress estimator continuously provides high-frequency execution evidence, while the Task Manager deliberates at salient milestones by jointly considering the current observation, task state, and interaction history to determine whether to advance the task, acquire additional observations, revise the plan, or initiate local recovery. This mechanism enables the robot to recover its state after a subtask failure and resume execution without discarding previously acquired scene knowledge. HarnessWAM achieves state-of-the-art full-task and subtask success rates of 59.6% and 69.9% on RoboMemArena, and an SR of 23.7% on RoboCerebra Ideal. These results demonstrate that model-external structured state maintenance and closed-loop agentic decision making can effectively extend the local control capabilities of WAMs into embodied task execution that is plannable, verifiable, and recoverable.

</details>


### [111] [Robotic Fabric Alignment System for Sewing Using Global Local Weighted ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09528)
*Wenbo Dong,Dipankar Bhattacharya Member,Kai Tang,Akinari Kobayashi,Fuyuki Tokuda,Akira Seino,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型自动化织物对齐系统，采用全局局部加权迭代最近点（GLW-ICP）方法，在缝纫前实现上下织物面板的毫米级精确对齐，兼顾全局边缘与局部缝纫线对齐，并在遮挡与非遮挡条件下均表现出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 织物精确对齐是缝纫前的关键步骤，传统方法难以同时兼顾全局边缘和局部缝纫线的对齐精度，且在遮挡条件下鲁棒性不足，需要一种更可靠的自动化对齐方案。

Method: 提出GLW-ICP（Global Local Weighted Iterative Closest Point）新方法：全局对齐织物边缘点，局部对齐缝纫线点与对应CAD模型点，并移除遮挡区域中的不匹配点；系统先估计上下织物面板的位姿，再操控顶部面板实现边缘和缝纫线的精确对齐。

Result: 在多种织物形状的真实世界实验中，系统在遮挡和非遮挡条件下均持续实现毫米级对齐精度，验证了其有效性和实际场景适用性。

Conclusion: GLW-ICP方法为自动化织物对齐提供了可靠解决方案，兼顾全局与局部对齐需求，具备实际缝纫场景中的应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate fabric alignment is a critical step that must be performed before sewing. This paper presents a novel automated fabric alignment system. The system estimates the poses of top and bottom fabric panels, lying flat and wrinkle-free in arbitrary positions, using a new Global Local Weighted Iterative Closest Point (GLW-ICP) method. The system then manipulates the top panel to achieve precise alignment at both edges and sewing lines. Unlike conventional approaches, GLW-ICP robustly aligns both global edges and local sewing lines by globally aligning fabric edge points and locally aligning sewing line points to their corresponding CAD model points, while removing unmatched points in occluded regions. Real-world experiments with various fabric shapes show that the system consistently achieves millimeter-level alignment accuracy under both occlusion and non-occlusion conditions, demonstrating its effectiveness and suitability for automated fabric alignment in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [Model-Based Systems Engineering Framework for SysML-Driven Design of Autonomous UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09547)
*Deekshitha Angadi,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Mohamed Samshad,Bharath Kumar Suryadevara,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SysML的MBSE设计框架，用于自主无人机系统的开发，通过四层模型（需求、功能、逻辑架构、物理/软件分配）实现从需求到ROS 2软件架构的系统性映射，支持需求追溯、接口定义和验证规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主无人机开发依赖文档化需求、分离的架构描述和软件实现工件，存在歧义、接口不一致和早期设计阶段追溯性弱等问题。复杂无人机是融合飞控、导航、感知、通信和任务决策的复杂信息物理系统，亟需形式化建模方法来提高设计的一致性和可追踪性。

Method: 以SysML为形式化设计主干，构建四层连接架构：利益相关者需求层、功能分解层、逻辑架构层和物理/软件分配层。采用SysML需求图、活动图、块定义图、内部块图、状态机图和参数图分别捕获功能性、结构性、行为性、接口和性能方面。将逻辑架构系统映射到ROS 2软件架构：SysML块对应ROS 2节点，流端口和连接器对应话题，请求-响应交互对应服务，目标导向行为对应动作。

Result: 通过代表性自主无人机任务场景（自主起飞、航点导航、悬停稳定、避障、返航、应急处理）在设计层面验证了该框架。生成的模型支持需求分配、接口定义、子系统职责分配和验证规划，可在仿真或物理部署之前完成。

Conclusion: 该MBSE框架有效解决了自主UAV开发中需求、架构和实现之间的断裂问题，通过SysML到ROS 2的系统化映射弥合了模型驱动设计与实际软件实现之间的鸿沟，为无人机系统早期设计提供了可追溯、无歧义的工程方法。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are complex cyber-physical systems that require the coordinated integration of flight control, navigation, perception, communication, power management, and mission-level decision-making under safety, timing, and reliability constraints. However, many autonomous UAV development workflows still rely on document-centric requirements, separated architectural descriptions, and software implementation artifacts, which can lead to ambiguity, interface inconsistencies, and weak traceability during early design. This paper presents a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) design framework for the SysML-driven development of autonomous UAVs. The proposed framework uses the Systems Modeling Language (SysML) as a formal design backbone to structure UAV development across four connected layers: stakeholder requirements, functional decomposition, logical architecture, and physical/software allocation. SysML requirement diagrams, activity diagrams, block definition diagrams, internal block diagrams, state machine diagrams, and parametric diagrams are used to capture the functional, structural, behavioral, interface, and performance aspects of the UAV system. The logical architecture is then systematically mapped to a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) software architecture by relating SysML blocks to ROS 2 nodes, flow ports and connectors to topics, request-response interactions to services, and goal-oriented behaviors to actions. The framework is illustrated at the design level using representative autonomous UAV mission scenarios, including autonomous take-off, waypoint navigation, hover stabilization, obstacle avoidance, return-to-home, and emergency handling. The resulting model supports requirement allocation, interface definition, subsystem responsibility assignment, and verification planning before simulation or physical deployment.

</details>


### [113] [TDMA Based Communications Control Co-Design for Cooperative Carrying: Delay Calibration and Sampling-Rate Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09556)
*Zahra Seifaei,Maximilian Luebke,Torsten Reissland,Danial Dehghani,Norman Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多机器人协作搬运任务中，通过基于网络延迟的自适应采样时间调节与策略性领导者轮换，在保持控制性能的同时公平分配无线通信资源。基于MuJoCo物理仿真和真实无线建模（TDMA、MAC、抖动、排队、丢包），对比了三种控制方案，发现动态采样可降低通信开销且不损害控制性能，领导者轮换能显著提升空中时间的公平性，对团队搬运能力影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队在协作搬运时面临维持稳定控制与保持通信高效之间的根本性挑战，现有方法在无线资源受限场景下缺乏对通信负载公平分配的考虑，需要探索既能控制性能又能高效利用通信资源的策略。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理仿真环境中结合真实无线建模（包括时分多址、介质访问控制、抖动、排队和丢包），评估三种方法：固定采样+静态领导、动态采样+静态领导、动态采样+旋转领导。动态采样根据实测网络延迟自适应调整采样时间间隔，旋转领导策略周期性更换领导者以分散无线负载。

Result: 动态采样有效降低通信开销且不损害控制性能；领导者轮换显著改善空中时间分配的公平性；两种策略的实现对团队搬运能力的影响可忽略不计；动态采样与领导者轮换之间存在重要的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文是首批联合研究动态采样、领导者轮换与无线协议交互作用的物理真实多机器人协作工作，为通信资源受限的真实世界中部署协同机器人团队提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Multi robot teams performing cooperative transportation face a fundamental challenge: maintaining stable control while keeping communications efficient. This paper investigates how adaptive sampling time adjustment informed by measured network delay and strategic leader rotation can distribute wireless load fairly across the team. We use physics based simulation in MuJoCo with realistic wireless modeling, including time division multiple access, medium access control, jitter, queueing, and packet loss, to evaluate three control approaches: fixed sampling with static leadership, dynamic sampling with static leadership, and dynamic sampling with rotating leadership. Our results reveal an important trade off: dynamic sampling effectively reduces communications overhead without compromising control performance, while rotating the leader role meaningfully improves how fairly airtime is distributed all with negligible impact on the team carrying ability. to the best of our knowledge, being among the first to jointly examine dynamic sampling, rotating leadership, and wireless protocol interactions in physicsrealistic multi robot cooperation, this work provides practical guidance for deploying coordinated robotic teams in real world settings where communications resources are limited.

</details>


### [114] [FactorDrive: Adaptive Multi-Step Reasoning Driven by Planning-Critical Factors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09591)
*Guolei Huang,Tengfei She,Yuxuan Lu,Yao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Yongjun Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FactorDrive是一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过构建PCF-CoT思维链数据集将规划推理与轨迹相关的空间-物理证据相结合，并引入QS-GRPO算法用蒙特卡洛树搜索和组相对策略优化来搜索并优化更高规划质量的推理路径，在nuScenes和NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在自动驾驶中未能充分将空间-物理证据整合到规划推理中，推理自适应是粗粒度的，无法满足场景特定的规划需求；同时，推理路径优化在自动驾驶后训练中基本未被探索。

Method: 1) 通过大规模驾驶领域指令微调建立基础驾驶知识；2) 构建PCF-CoT思维链数据集，将规划推理锚定在轨迹相关的空间-物理证据上，并围绕场景特定的规划关键因子组织推理，使推理路径的组合和深度自适应不同规划需求；3) 提出QS-GRPO，利用轨迹级规划奖励引导MCTS发现更高规划质量的推理路径，并通过GRPO优化策略。

Result: 在开放环路基准nuScenes和闭环导向基准NAVSIM上的大量实验表明，FactorDrive取得了最先进的规划性能。

Conclusion: FactorDrive通过规划关键因子驱动的自适应多步推理框架，有效整合了空间-物理证据并优化推理路径，显著提升了端到端自动驾驶的规划质量。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced scene understanding and enabled explicit reasoning in end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods insufficiently integrate spatial-physical evidence into planning reasoning, while reasoning adaptation remains coarse-grained and falls short of scene-specific planning demands. Furthermore, reasoning-path optimization for higher planning quality remains largely unexplored in autonomous-driving post-training. To address these limitations, we propose FactorDrive, an end-to-end autonomous driving framework for adaptive multi-step reasoning driven by planning-critical factors (PCFs). We first perform large-scale driving-domain instruction tuning to establish foundational driving knowledge. Building on this foundation, we construct PCF-CoT, a chain-of-thought (CoT) dataset that grounds planning reasoning in trajectory-relevant spatial-physical evidence and organizes reasoning around scene-specific PCFs, enabling the composition and depth of reasoning paths to adapt to different planning demands. We further introduce Quality Search-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (QS-GRPO), which guides Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with trajectory-level planning rewards to discover reasoning paths with higher planning quality and uses the resulting responses to optimize the policy through GRPO, thereby improving trajectory planning performance. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop-oriented (NAVSIM) benchmarks demonstrate that FactorDrive achieves state-of-the-art planning performance.

</details>


### [115] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control of a Robotic Soft Esophagus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09602)
*Dipankar Bhattacharya,Ryman Hashem,Leo K. Cheng,Weiliang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Robotic Soft Esophagus (RoSE) 2.0版本，通过嵌入TOF和压力传感器实现感知，并采用基于SINDYc模型的模型预测控制(MPC)实现食管蠕动波形的闭环控制，用于食管支架测试和食物粘度研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食管癌引起的狭窄会导致吞咽困难，RoSE作为一种体外平台用于食管支架测试和食物粘度研究。但原RoSE缺乏管腔可视性和嵌入式感知能力，蠕动波生成和控制在开环模式下进行，无法实现精确的自主控制。

Method: RoSEv2.0嵌入TOF传感器测量管腔位移、压力传感器测量气压，利用传感器数据通过Sparse Identification Nonlinear Dynamics with Control (SINDYc)发现动态模型，并基于该模型实现模型预测控制(MPC)来自主控制蠕动和气压剖面。

Result: MPC成功生成了速度20 mm/s、波长75 mm、振幅5/7.5/10 mm的蠕动波，并实现了不同食物浆体稠度下的支架迁移测试。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是将SINDYc-based MPC应用于RoSE的闭环控制问题，实现了期望蠕动波的自主生成，为食管支架测试和食物粘度研究提供了更精确的体外平台。

Abstract: Strictures caused by esophageal cancer can narrow down the esophageal lumen, leading to dysphagia. Palliation of dysphagia has driven the development of a Robotic Soft Esophagus (RoSE), which provides a novel in vitro platform for esophageal stent testing and food viscosity studies. In RoSE, peristaltic wave generation and control were done in an open-loop manner since the conduit lacked visibility and embedded sensing capability. Hence, in this work, RoSE version 2.0 (RoSEv2.0) is designed with embedded Time Of Flight (TOF) and pressure sensors to measure conduit displacement and air pressure, respectively, for modeling and control. Model Predictive Control (MPC) of RoSEv2.0 is implemented to govern the peristalsis and air pressure profile autonomously. The implemented MPC used Sparse Identification Nonlinear Dynamics with Control (SINDYC) models to estimate the future states of ROSEv2.0. The dynamic models are discovered from the TOF and pressure sensor data. Peristalsis waves of speed 20 mm/s, wavelength 75 mm, and amplitudes 5, 7.5, and 10 mm were successfully generated by the MPC. Additionally, RoSEv2.0 with the MPC was employed to perform stent migration testing with various food bolus consistencies. The major contribution claimed in this paper is the application of SINDYC-based MPC to solve the closed-loop control problem of RoSE for achieving desired peristaltic waves.

</details>


### [116] [Predictive safety filter enhanced curriculum learning control for efficient vehicle dynamics controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09653)
*Baocong Zhang,Siliang Lu,Chenyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合课程学习与基于物理的预测安全滤波器的车辆运动与动力学控制方法，在Python-CarSim平台上验证了其在多种机动场景下的性能提升和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型控制在复杂控制任务中表现优异，但难以提供安全保证。以车辆运动与动力学控制为例，传统方法参数标定工作繁重，而现有学习型控制在稳定性与敏捷性上仍有提升空间，亟需在保持性能的同时增强安全性与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个课程学习控制器，并集成基于物理的预测安全滤波器。课程学习用于渐进式训练以提升学习效率与泛化能力，预测安全滤波器则基于物理模型对控制动作进行实时安全约束与修正。

Result: 在Python-CarSim仿真平台上进行了验证，实验结果表明该方法在多种机动工况下相比已有方法展现出更好的性能改进和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将课程学习与物理驱动的预测安全滤波器相结合，可以在保持学习型控制高性能的同时提供安全保证，为车辆控制等领域的安全强化学习提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based control have enabled impressive achievements in solving complex control problems in various domains. However, since learning-based control may not be able to realize safety-guaranties, it is of great importance to enhance safety and robustness while maintaining good performances. Take vehicle motion \& dynamics control as an example, in order to overcome the pain points of traditional methods such as heavy parameter calibration effort and learning-based control to bring better performance and efficiency in stability \& agility over prior work for state-based vehicle control tasks, in this work, our method aims to develop a curriculum learning controller enhanced with physics-based predictive safety filter. The validation is conducted with the Python-CarSim platform, demonstrating better improvements and scalability under various maneuvers.

</details>


### [117] [Removing Infrastructure Barriers in Human-Robot Collaboration Through Wireless Reconfigurable Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09658)
*Emma Takács,Mátyás Hajós,Ádám Juniki,Ádám Fischer,Zoltán Komáromi,Kristóf Abai,Dániel Horváth,Sándor Máthé,Konstantinos Kousias,Bence Tipary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于5G的无线人机协作（HRC）实验测试平台，包含电池供电的多传感器原型和计算机视觉模块（物体检测、姿态估计和手部识别），通过将计算密集任务卸载到边缘端解决带宽-延迟权衡，并在匈牙利和挪威的多种5G基础设施上验证了系统可移植性，实现了低至12ms的往返响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HRC工作单元受电力和数据布线限制，模块化和重构性差；同时可用于实时感知和安全协作的商用无线设备选择有限。在动态、高混合低批量的再制造等工业场景中，频繁的工位重排需要高度灵活的无线协作系统。

Method: 系统整合了电池供电的多传感器平台原型以消除基础设施障碍；开发了基于合成数据和真实数据训练的计算机视觉模块，用于物体检测、方向感知抓取姿态估计和稳健的手部识别；通过5G将计算密集任务卸载到边缘端；系统在匈牙利和挪威的公共/私有、SA/NSA 5G基础设施上进行了部署和评估。

Result: 视觉模型在mAP@50-95上达到97.74±0.10%，平均推理时间12.5ms；网络实验中在兼容的网络-设备配对下实现了低至12ms的往返响应时间，满足安全自适应HRC需求；但也揭示了当前5G部署中与互操作性相关的实际局限性。

Conclusion: 该无线5G系统作为可移植、灵活的HRC实验测试平台在多种5G基础设施上得到验证，适用于再制造、操作员培训和用户研究等场景；然而，当前5G部署的互操作性问题仍需在未来的工作中解决。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) plays a vital role in dynamic, high mix, low volume industrial scenarios such as remanufacturing, which frequently face workcell rearrangements. Traditional setups are constrained by power and data cabling, restricting modularity and reconfigurations, while the selection of commercial wireless devices suitable for real-time perception and safe collaboration are limited in availability. This paper presents a highly flexible, wireless, 5G-based system that serves as a versatile experimental testbed for applications including remanufacturing, operator training, and user studies. To eliminate infrastructure barriers, the workcell integrates a novel battery-powered, multi-sensor platform prototype. Additionally, to support operator safety and system adaptability across environmental shifts, the system integrates a computer vision module for object detection and pose estimation, further augmented for robust hand recognition. Trained on synthetic and real data, the model reliably detects oriented grasping poses and human hands across varying lighting and background conditions (with an mAP@50-95 of 97.74 +- 0.10% and a mean inference time of 12.5 ms). Offloading these computationally intensive tasks to the edge via 5G, the proposed architecture contributes to resolving the bandwidth-latency trade-off. To demonstrate portability, the system was implemented in both Hungary and Norway, and was evaluated across a combination of public and private, Standalone and Non-Standalone 5G infrastructures. The performed network experiments produced results in round-trip response times down to 12 ms in case of compatible network-device pairings, suitable for safe, adaptive HRC. However, these measurements also revealed practical limitations related to interoperability in current 5G deployments that should be addressed in future works.

</details>


### [118] [TAMS: Task-Aware Multi-View Adaptive Streaming for Wireless Telerobotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09731)
*Zexin Deng,Zhenhui Yuan,Lu Tian,Subhash Lakshminarayana,Longhao Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAMS是一种面向无线遥操作机器人的任务感知多视角自适应视频流系统，根据当前操作阶段动态分配各摄像头视频码率，优先保证操作员最相关的主视角质量，同时保持次要视角的基础可见性。在6自由度遥操作平台上、三种受限网络条件下，TAMS相比均等和静态分配基线，显著提升主视角SSIM、缩短任务完成时间并提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线遥操作机器人依赖多视角视频反馈进行精准控制，但上行链路带宽通常有限且动态变化。如何在受限且波动的带宽下，合理地分配各视角的视频码率，使操作员在关键操作阶段获得最相关、最清晰的视觉信息，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: 从轻量级的机器人端信号中推断当前操作阶段（任务感知），根据阶段确定各摄像头视图的优先级：将更多码率分配给与操作员当前任务最相关的摄像头视图，同时为次要视图保留最小基线码率以保证基础可见性，实现动态的自适应码率分配。

Result: 在6自由度遥操作测试平台上、三种受限网络条件下进行实验验证。与均等分配和静态分配基线相比，TAMS在三种条件下均提升了主视角的SSIM、缩短了任务完成时间并提高了任务成功率。在带宽最受限的条件下，平均完成时间从68.9秒降至43.9秒（相对均等分配），任务成功率从48%提升至71%。

Conclusion: TAMS通过任务感知的动态码率分配策略，在有限的无线上行带宽下有效改善了多视角遥操作体验，验证了根据操作阶段智能分配视频资源这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Wireless telerobotic manipulation relies on timely multi-view video feedback, but the available uplink bandwidth is often limited and dynamic. This paper presents Task-Aware Multi-View Adaptive Streaming (TAMS), a system that allocates video bitrate according to the current manipulation phase. TAMS infers task phase from lightweight robot-side signals and prioritizes the camera view most relevant to the operator while preserving baseline visibility for secondary views. Experiments on a six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) teleoperation testbed under three constrained network conditions show that TAMS improves primary view Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), reduces task completion time, and increases trial success rate compared with equal and static allocation baselines. Under the most constrained bandwidth condition, TAMS reduces mean completion time from 68.9 s to 43.9 s relative to equal allocation and increases trial success rate from 48% to 71%. Code is available at: https://github.com/Dzxx623/TAMS.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient Real-World Online Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation via Centralized Training and Critic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09762)
*Changhao Li,Yifang Zhang,Heng Zhang,Davide Torielli,Damiano Gasperini,Arturo Laurenzi,Luca Muratore,Arash Ajoudani,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HIL-HARC框架，结合集中式训练与分散式执行（CTDE）和混合奖励架构（HRA），通过多智能体共享集中式多头批评家（分解为任务头与抓取头）来解决真实世界在线强化学习中多智能体并发训练带来的非平稳性问题，并在远超先前工作的域随机化范围内显著提升样本效率和策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界在线强化学习虽能避免sim-to-real差距并支持人机交互持续优化策略，但现有方法仅局限于小的随机化范围，且面临多智能体并发训练导致的非平稳性挑战，亟需统一框架解决这些局限。

Method: 将CTDE与HRA结合：多个actor共享一个集中式多头批评家，批评家分解为对应稀疏任务奖励的任务头和对应基于势函数的抓取奖励的抓取头；相应地重构批评家和actor目标函数，利用分解的Q值并显式考虑离散抓取策略的分类动作分布。

Result: 在网球球和香蕉抓放、锅复位及模拟方块搬移等任务上，在维度级域随机化（比先前工作大约5-25倍）条件下，相比最先进基线方法：网球球抓放成功率从60%提升到80%，香蕉抓放从60%提升到90%，模拟方块搬移从25%提升到95%，并成功完成了基线始终失败的任务。

Conclusion: HIL-HARC框架通过分解式多头批评家与CTDE的有效结合，在大范围域随机化下显著提升样本效率和策略成功率，为真实世界多智能体在线RL训练提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world online reinforcement learning (RL) provides a promising approach for training robotic manipulation policies directly in the physical world, avoiding the sim-to-real gap and enabling continuous policy refinement through human-in-the-loop interaction. Recent methods have demonstrated sample-efficient learning through human intervention but remain limited to small randomization ranges and encounter challenges with the non-stationarity induced by concurrently training multiple agents. To address these limitations, we introduce a unified framework that combines centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) and a Hybrid Reward Architecture (HRA). This enables multiple actors to share a centralized multi-head critic. The critic is decomposed into task and grasp heads, corresponding to the sparse task reward and a potential-based grasping reward, respectively. We accordingly reformulate the critic and actor objectives to exploit the decomposed Q-values while explicitly accounting for the categorical action distribution of the discrete gripper policy. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework substantially improves both sample efficiency and policy performance. We validate our approach on two robotic arms and a simulated humanoid robot across tennis ball and banana pick-and-place, pot reset, and simulated block relocation tasks under dimension-wise domain randomization, approximately 5-25x larger than those considered in prior work. Compared with a state-of-the-art baseline, our method improves the success rate from 60% to 80% on tennis ball pick-and-place, from 60% to 90% on banana pick-and-place, and from 25% to 95% on simulated block relocation, while also successfully accomplishing a task where the baseline consistently fails. Videos and more details are available at our project website: https://hil-harc.github.io/.

</details>


### [120] [SLIM-0.5B: Learning Action-Grounded Predictive Latents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09771)
*Jingkai Wang,Zihan Tang,Gu Zhang,Mingyu Cao,Jiapeng Chen,Jingjiao Zhao,Xiansheng Chen,Pengwei Wang,Lemao Liu,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SLIM，一个仅0.5B参数的紧凑潜在交互策略模型。SLIM通过自监督掩码轨迹预测学习动作锚定的潜在表示，结合动作重建与未来潜在预测，采用紧凑的Mixture-of-Transformers骨架建模观测潜在与动作token之间的交互，并通过流匹配进行语言条件动作生成。在仿真与真实世界评测中，SLIM以更少的参数、更低的推理延迟和GPU内存使用，达到或超过大尺度VLA与世界动作模型基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略依赖大型多模态骨干网络，其大部分容量服务于开放域语义理解，而连续机器人操作主要需要观测、动作及动作引起的状态转移的紧凑表示。像素级世界模型虽然提供了另一条路径，但预测与控制无关的视觉细节计算成本过高。

Method: SLIM学习动作锚定的预测性潜在表示，同时捕捉动作条件化的未来转移以及解释观测变化的动作。通过自监督掩码轨迹预测训练，结合动作重建与未来潜在预测两个目标。采用紧凑的Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）骨架建模观测潜在与动作token之间的交互，最终使用流匹配训练语言条件动作生成策略。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实世界评测中，SLIM以更少的参数量（0.5B）、无需额外的具身预训练、更低的推理延迟和显著更低的GPU内存占用，达到或超过大尺度VLA和世界动作模型基线的表现。

Conclusion: 通过不预测像素级细节、仅学习动作相关的潜在表示，可以有效压缩控制所需信息，从而实现紧凑高效且性能优异的机器人操作策略，为构建轻量级VLA系统提供了一种可行方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies rely on large multimodal backbones to jointly perform perception, language conditioning, and action generation at every control step. Much of this capacity supports open-domain semantics, whereas continuous robot manipulation primarily requires compact representations of observations, actions, and the transitions induced by actions. Pixel-level world models provide another route, but predicting visual details irrelevant to control can be unnecessarily expensive. We propose SLIM (Self-supervised Latent Interaction Model), a compact 0.5B-parameter latent interaction policy. SLIM learns action-grounded predictive latents that capture both action-conditioned future transitions and the actions that explain observed changes. SLIM learns these representations through self-supervised masked trajectory prediction, combining action reconstruction with future-latent prediction. A compact Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone models interactions between observation latents and action tokens. The resulting policy is trained with flow matching for language-conditioned action generation. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world evaluation, SLIM matches or exceeds representative large-scale VLA and world-action-model baselines with fewer parameters, no additional embodied pretraining, lower inference latency, and substantially lower GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [121] [RoboSeg: Online Part-Level Semantic Reconstruction for Robotic Manipulation via a Single Eye-in-Hand Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09778)
*Zhaochen Lan,Mengxiang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSeg是一种部件级语义重建系统，将VLM功能部件发现、异步在线RGB-D语义重建与任务导向抓取生成相结合，无需CAD模型即可实现语义索引驱动的机器人操作。系统在手动标注对象上达到83.4%的mIoU，物理实验中24次试验接触正确部件率达100%，任务成功率21/24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要识别可操作部件（如把手、边缘、扳机、工具尖端），而不仅仅是对象类别或点云。现有系统缺乏无需CAD模型的部件级语义重建，难以将语义理解与任务条件抓取直接关联。

Method: RoboSeg包含三个核心模块：(1) 在初始RGB观测上向VLM查询紧凑的功能部件提示；(2) 双异步流扫描——高频几何线程负责RGB-D里程计和TSDF融合，关键帧触发的语义线程负责SAM3部件掩码；(3) 投影掩码通过体素级时间投票融合为持久部件标记点云，再利用该地图将AnyGrasp 6-DoF候选分配到语义部件并选择与任务相关部件标签一致的抓取。

Result: 在手动标记对象上达到83.4%的平均部件交并比(mIoU)；在4个对象、8个任务的24次物理试验中，所有试验的选定抓取均接触请求部件，24次中21次组合任务成功。

Conclusion: RoboSeg可作为任务条件操作中的语义索引层，AnyGrasp保留为候选生成器。其部件级语义重建能力为无CAD模型的机器人语义操作提供了一种可行的范式。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires perception systemsthat identify actionable parts such as handles, rims, triggers,and tool tips, not merely object categories or point clouds. This paper presents RoboSeg, a part-level semantic reconstructionsystem that links vision-language model (VLM) functional-partdiscovery, asynchronous online RGB-D semantic reconstruc-tion, and task-oriented grasp generation without requiring CAD models or pre-scanned meshes. RoboSeg queries a VLM onthe initial RGB observation to obtain compact functional part prompts, then scans with two asynchronous streams: a high-frequency geometry thread for RGB-D odometry and truncated signed distance function (TSDF) fusion, and a keyframe-triggered semantic thread for SAM3 part masks. Projectedmasks are fused by voxel-level temporal voting into a persistentpart-labeled point cloud; RoboSeg uses this map to assign AnyGrasp 6-DoF candidates to semantic parts and select grasps consistent with the task-relevant part label. RoboSeg reaches 83.4% mean part intersection-over-union (mIoU) over manually labeled objects; in a 24-trial physical pilot across fourobjects and eight tasks, the selected grasp contacts the requestedpart in all trials and achieves 21/24 combined task successes.These results characterize RoboSeg as a semantic indexing layerfor task-conditioned manipulation, with AnyGrasp retained asthe proposal generator.

</details>


### [122] [WRAP: Wasserstein-Robust Adaptive Plug-in for Robot Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09807)
*Minhyuk Jang,Astghik Hakobyan,Jungjin Lee,Naira Hovakimyan,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WRAP，一种与适配器无关的Wasserstein鲁棒插件，用于非线性扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和误差状态卡尔曼滤波（ESKF）堆栈。通过因果模块提供时变有效过程/测量统计量，并利用均值保持的Wasserstein局部更新计算最不利协方差与鲁棒增益，在不改变传播模型、残差或回缩的情况下实现均值适配与协方差鲁棒化的分离。在18个UWB-IMU序列上，WRAP将平均3D位置RMSE相对标称ESKF降低27.4%，在Jetson Orin Nano上求解仅需0.05ms（UWB）和2.92ms（GNSS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位在传感条件变化时，EKF/ESKF可能遭受有偏误差和协方差校准不准的问题，传统方法难以同时处理均值偏移与协方差欠/过紧的问题，需要一种既能适配均值又能鲁棒化协方差的通用方法。

Method: WRAP由两部分组成：(1) 一个因果模块，提供时变的有效过程和测量统计量（均值适配）；(2) 均值保持的Wasserstein局部更新，计算最不利协方差和鲁棒增益，且不改变传播模型、残差或回缩函数。该方法对传播和感知使用不同的鲁棒半径，实现了均值适配与协方差鲁棒化的解耦。

Result: 在18个从适配器训练中留出的UWB-IMU序列中，适配器单独和WRAP分别相对标称ESKF降低平均3D位置RMSE 19.8%和27.4%；各向同性消融达到19.5%，表明增量收益来自方向性过程协方差再分配。样本内GNSS-INS研究表明均值适配贡献了大部分精度提升，而DR改善了一致性并缓解了过度收紧的经典协方差估计。鲁棒求解在Jetson Orin Nano上耗时0.05ms（UWB）和2.92ms（GNSS）。

Conclusion: WRAP通过分离均值适配与协方差鲁棒化，使用方向性协方差再分配和不同的传播/感知鲁棒半径，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升EKF/ESKF在变化传感条件下的定位精度与一致性，验证了其作为适配无关插件的有效性和实时性。

Abstract: Robotic localization under changing sensing conditions can suffer from biased errors and miscalibrated covariances. We present WRAP, an adapter-agnostic Wasserstein-robust plug-in for nonlinear extended Kalman filter (EKF) and error-state Kalman filter (ESKF) stacks. A causal module supplies time-varying effective process and measurement statistics; a mean-preserving Wasserstein local update then computes least-favorable covariances and a robust gain without changing the propagation model, residual, or retraction. This separates mean adaptation from covariance robustification and uses distinct radii for propagation and sensing. On 18 UWB--IMU sequences held out from adapter training, adapter-only and WRAP reduce mean 3-D position RMSE by $19.8\%$ and $27.4\%$ relative to the nominal ESKF; an isotropic ablation reaches $19.5\%$, linking the incremental gain to directional process-covariance redistribution. An in-sample GNSS--INS study shows that mean adaptation provides most of the accuracy gain, while DR improves consistency and mitigates over-tightened classical covariance estimates. The robust solve takes 0.05 ms for UWB and 2.92 ms for GNSS on a Jetson Orin Nano.

</details>


### [123] [Hierarchical Fast--Slow ReAct Agent for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09816)
*Zhaochen Lan,Zhi Yang,Yuxiang Fu,Mengxiang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层"快-慢"智能体（hierarchical fast-slow agent）用于零样本目标导航（ZSON）。该系统保留值图控制器逐帧运行，同时构建坐标锚定记忆（包含房间类型语义栅格、确认物体实例及带位姿的关键帧），并利用VLM筛选检测结果。上层审议层（deliberative layer）以受限的"推理-检索-行动"循环读取记忆，仅在结构事件触发时唤醒，先基于文本推理，仅在文本无法区分时召回第一人称视角。系统在HM3D val上达到68.75%成功率，在MP3D val上达到47.29%，为对比的零样本方法中最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSON方法存在关键缺陷：值图方法每次决策都是对当前地图的argmax，决策后的证据被立即丢弃；大VLM直接嵌入感知-行动循环的方法则按固定频率从当前视角查询，不重新审视已走过的房间，且失败调用没有明确回退机制。这些方法未能将机器人已观测到的历史信息作为审议对象，导致导航决策缺乏全局推理能力。

Method: 系统采用分层架构：(1) 反应层（reactive layer）：值图控制器每步运行，负责底层导航；(2) 记忆构建：移动过程中写入坐标锚定记忆——房间类型语义栅格+已确认物体实例+带位姿的关键帧有界存储，VLM在写入前筛选候选检测；(3) 审议层（deliberative layer）：以受限的"推理-检索-行动"循环读取记忆，由反应层计算的结构事件触发唤醒，先基于文本推理，仅在文本不足时召回第一人称视角；(4) 资源约束：每次调用和每轮运行有调用上限，无调用第一层解决最常见停滞问题，任何失败回退到反应控制器。

Result: 系统在HM3D val上达到68.75%成功率（SR），在MP3D val上达到47.29%，是所比较零样本方法中最高成功率。配对比较实验表明：对远距离前沿点用argmax而非审议会损失3.40个SR点（95% CI [1.70, 5.05]，2000个HM3D episodes）；而对每个前沿点都进行审议并不能恢复这些损失，说明选择性审议（selective deliberation）是必要的。

Conclusion: 将机器人已观测到的历史信息作为显式审议对象，通过分层"快-慢"架构将高频反应控制与低频审慎推理结合，能够显著提升零样本目标导航的成功率。选择性审议策略——仅在需要时动用VLM资源——是性能与计算成本之间的有效平衡。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) requires a robot to find a named object category in a building it has never entered. The prevailing approach scores frontiers with a vision--language \emph{value map}: every decision is another argmax over the map as it currently stands, and the evidence behind that score is discarded the moment it is taken. Systems that place a large vision--language model inside the perception--action loop typically query it on a fixed schedule from the current view alone; a room the robot walked through minutes earlier is never reconsidered, and a failed call has no defined fallback. We turn what the robot has already seen into the object of deliberation. Our hierarchical fast--slow agent leaves the value-map controller running at every step and writes a \emph{coordinate-anchored memory} as it moves: a semantic grid of room types and confirmed object instances, together with a bounded store of pose-tagged keyframes. A VLM screens each candidate detection before it is written. A deliberative layer reads this memory in a bounded reason--retrieve--act loop. It wakes on structural events the reactive layer computes, reasons first over text, and recalls a first-person view only for candidates that text alone cannot separate. Per-invocation and per-run caps bound its calls, a call-free first tier resolves the most frequent stall, and any failure returns control to the reactive controller. Our system reaches 68.75% SR on HM3D v1 \texttt{val} and 47.29% on MP3D \texttt{val}, the highest success rate among the zero-shot methods compared here. Choosing among far frontiers by argmax instead of deliberating costs 3.40 SR points in a paired comparison over all 2000 HM3D episodes (95\% CI [1.70, 5.05]); deliberating over every frontier does not recover them.

</details>


### [124] [RynnValue: Scaling Robotic Value Foundation Models with Temporal Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09853)
*Dongchi Huang,Hongyin Zhang,Bohan Hou,Siteng Huang,Zhian Su,Hang Guo,Tong Lu,Zhaofeng Xu,Jiahao Tang,Jianfei Yang,Donglin Wang,Peixi Peng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnValue是一个开源的价值基础模型，用时间距离（temporal distance）替代偏好/进度等任务内部锚点作为监督信号，在7000+小时、约300万条指令条件片段上训练，无需偏好标注即可在奖励评估基准上超越全偏好监督的SOTA，并通过势能整形提升真实世界策略成功率（在线52.5%→72.5%，离线63.8%→82.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用奖励模型是机器人学习规模化的瓶颈。现有方法将价值学习监督锚定在偏好或归一化进度等任务内部信号上，这些信号无法跨具身（embodiment）和数据源迁移。需要一种可扩展、可跨域迁移的监督目标来训练价值模型。

Method: 提出时间距离（temporal distance）作为监督锚点——即从当前观测到语言指定目标的定向cost-to-go。时间距离标签可直接从时间戳推导，无需偏好或进度标注，从而能扩展到7000+小时和约300万条指令条件片段。为使大规模时间价值学习可靠，设计了三个技术：随机时间采样、时间顺序打乱、价值隔离注意力，以抑制使预测对失败和回归不敏感的捷径。

Result: 在RBM-EVAL-OOD上平均Kendall's tau_a达到0.675，超越全偏好监督SOTA（0.655），是仅用进度监督方法（0.292）的两倍以上；零样本泛化到未见任务、具身和视角；通过势能整形转换为稠密奖励后，真实世界在线策略成功率从52.5%提升至72.5%，离线从63.8%提升至82.5%。

Conclusion: 时间距离是可扩展的价值监督目标和实用的奖励接口，能在大规模异构数据上训练出通用价值基础模型，显著提升泛化机器人的真实操控性能。

Abstract: General-purpose reward models are increasingly the bottleneck for scaling robot learning, yet the recipe for learning value-related capabilities from large-scale heterogeneous corpora remains underexplored. Existing approaches tie supervision to task-internal anchors such as preferences or normalized progress, none of which transfer cleanly across embodiments and data sources. We introduce RynnValue, an open-source value foundation model for robotic manipulation that replaces these anchors with temporal distance, the directed cost-to-go from an observation to the language-specified goal. Because temporal-distance labels can be derived directly from timestamps, RynnValue scales to over 7,000 hours and roughly 3M instruction-conditioned clips without preference or progress annotations. To make temporal-value learning reliable at scale, we combine random temporal sampling, temporal-order shuffling, and value-isolation attention, suppressing shortcuts that would leave predictions insensitive to failures and regressions. Trained without preference labels, RynnValue attains an average Kendall's tau_a of 0.675 on RBM-EVAL-OOD, surpassing the fully preference-supervised state of the art (0.655) and more than doubling a progress-only counterpart (0.292), while generalizing zero-shot to unseen tasks, embodiments, and viewpoints. Converted into dense rewards via potential-based shaping, it raises real-world policy success from 52.5% to 72.5% online and from 63.8% to 82.5% offline. These results establish temporal distance as a scalable supervision target and practical reward interface for generalist robot policies.

</details>


### [125] [Agentic Harnesses: LLM-Driven Verification Layers for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09857)
*Rohan Bhagra,Mahantesh Halapannavar,Uddhav Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM驱动的验证层，作为机器人规划与执行之间的中间件，利用LLM-as-a-Judge集成方法对机器人行动计划的可行性进行安全评估，实现批准、驳回或升级人工审查三种门控决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主系统研究主要聚焦于规划执行而非验证规划模型提出动作的可行性。与通用LLM类似，机器人规划模型存在诸多风险：倾向于偏向用户指定目标、可能建议违背科学伦理的动作、缺乏记忆先验安全风险的能力、易受对抗性攻击。因此需要在规划与执行之间增加安全验证层。

Method: 提出LLM-as-a-Judge集成验证层，作为规划与执行之间的中间件。该方法结合了chain-of-thought推理（跨多模型）并综合各专家裁判输出，融合了mixture of experts与self-consistency思想。该层对来自服务器规划模块的计划进行门控审查，在到达MCP服务器和机器人底层控制之前，将计划分类为批准、驳回（供重新制定）或升级（供人工审查）。

Result: 系统在accept/escalate/reject三类上达到约85%的精确率；对抗性攻击的遏制率达到97%；接受与拒绝任务之间的错误可忽略，错误主要集中在升级边界。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-Judge集成验证层能有效作为机器人规划与执行之间的安全门控中间件，在保证高精确率的同时有效遏制对抗性攻击，为机器人自主系统的安全执行提供了可行的验证机制。

Abstract: Advances in advanced artificial intelligence tools have sparked research in robot autonomy, but the development of such systems has largely focused on execution rather than verifying the feasibility actions planning models propose. Like general-purpose LLMs, robotics planning models carry risks: biased toward user-specified goals, they may suggest actions misaligned with scientific ethics, they may be unsafe due to an inability to "remember" prior safety risks, or they may be vulnerable to adversarial attacks on the autonomy ecosystem. We propose a LLM-driven verification layer between planning and execution to evaluate action permissibility. Our LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble combines chain-of-thought reasoning across models and synthesizes those expert judge outputs, mirroring a combination of a mixture of experts and self-consistency approach. This layer serves as middleware, gating plans from the server's planning module before they reach the MCP server and therefore the robot's low-level controls: plans are approved, rejected for reformulation, or escalated for human review. With this system, we achieve near 85% precision across accept/escalate/reject categories 97% containment of adversarial attacks, with negligible errors between accepting and rejecting tasks, and errors mostly manifesting at the escalate boundary.

</details>


### [126] [Entanglement-Free Trajectory Planning for Tethered Mobile Robots with a Slack Tether](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09860)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Bart De Schutter*
